中文摘要翻译: 传统RGB相机因其能够捕获丰富的表观和语义信息而被广泛应用于多目标跟踪。然而，在运动模糊、低光照和过曝等复杂现实挑战下，它们的性能往往会退化。受生物启发的事件相机具有高时间分辨率和高动态范围，在极端场景下能够提供互补线索。然而，由于缺乏大规模且标注良好的数据集，RGB-事件多目标跟踪仍处于探索不足的状态。针对这一问题，我们提出了FEMOT，一个大规模RGB-事件多目标跟踪数据集，涵盖了多种现实场景和14种挑战属性。FEMOT同时提供RGB和事件数据以及高质量的标注，为系统评估RGB-事件多目标跟踪方法提供了可靠平台。基于FEMOT，我们重新训练并评估了十多个强跟踪器，从而为未来研究建立了综合基准。此外，我们提出了FEMOTR，一种多模态跟踪框架，该框架将RGB和事件特征解耦并在频域中进行融合，从而有效利用它们的互补特性以实现鲁棒的目标定位和身份关联。在FEMOT和DSEC-MOT数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性。源代码和基准数据集已在https://github.com/Event-AHU/FEMOT上发布。

</details>


### [30] [A New Multi-Domain Benchmark for Micro-Action Recognition and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14096)
*Yanbin Hao,Pengyu Liu,Xing Wei,Xun Yang,Dan Gu,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMA-82，一个大规模多领域微动作基准数据集（82类、4个领域、77,856个实例），并建立微动作识别与多标签检测两大任务，实验表明现有方法在域迁移、长尾分布和时序定位上仍有挑战，且微动作与情绪状态高度相关，可为面部微表情提供补充线索。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有的MA-52基准在规模、场景多样性、任务覆盖度和评估协议方面存在局限，亟需一个更真实、更全面的基准以推动微动作分析向实际应用发展。

Method: 构建MMA-82数据集（82个细粒度类别、4个领域、454名受试者、77,856个标注实例），定义微动作识别（含域内/跨域、少样本/零样本协议）和多标签微动作检测两个核心任务，并进行广泛实验评估。

Result: 现有方法在域迁移、长尾类别分布和复杂时序定位下表现欠佳；微动作与情绪状态强相关，能为面部微表情提供补充线索以提升情绪识别性能。

Conclusion: MMA-82是一个全面且富有挑战性的真实微动作分析基准，为以人为中心的AI提供了有价值的资源。

Original Abstract: Micro-actions are short-duration, low-amplitude subtle body movements at the whole-body level that can reveal latent intentions, involuntary reactions, and fine-grained affective changes. Our previous MA-52 benchmark has provided an important foundation for micro-action recognition, but it remains limited in scale, scene diversity, task coverage, and evaluation protocols. To advance micro-action analysis toward more realistic and comprehensive settings, we introduce MMA-82, a large-scale multi-domain extension of MA-52. MMA-82 expands the label space from 52 to 82 fine-grained micro-action categories and covers four distinct domains, including laboratory interviews, street interviews, psychiatric patient interviews, and emotion-rich television videos, resulting in 77,856 annotated instances from 454 subjects. Built upon MMA-82, we establish two core tasks: Micro-Action Recognition and Multi-label Micro-Action Detection. For recognition, we further define in-domain and cross-domain protocols, including few-shot and zero-shot settings, to evaluate model robustness, transferability, and generalization. Extensive experiments show that current methods still struggle with realistic micro-action understanding, especially under domain shift, long-tailed category distributions, and complex temporal localization. Beyond benchmarking, we investigate the relationship between micro-actions and emotion, showing that micro-actions are strongly associated with emotional states and provide complementary cues to facial micro-expressions for improved emotion recognition. These results demonstrate that MMA-82 serves as a comprehensive and challenging benchmark for realistic micro-action analysis and a valuable resource for human-centered AI. MMA-82 is available at https://github.com/LpyNow/MMA-82.

中文摘要翻译: 微动作是指全身层面的短时、低幅度的细微身体运动，能够揭示潜在意图、无意识反应和细粒度情感变化。我们之前的MA-52基准为微动作识别提供了重要基础，但在规模、场景多样性、任务覆盖度和评估协议方面仍存在局限。为了将微动作分析推向更真实、更全面的场景，我们提出了MMA-82，一个大规模多领域扩展的MA-52版本。MMA-82将标签空间从52个扩展到82个细粒度微动作类别，涵盖四个不同领域，包括实验室访谈、街头访谈、精神科患者访谈和富含情感的电视视频，共包含来自454名受试者的77,856个标注实例。基于MMA-82，我们建立了两个核心任务：微动作识别和多标签微动作检测。在识别任务中，我们进一步定义了域内和跨域协议，包括少样本和零样本设置，以评估模型的鲁棒性、迁移性和泛化能力。大量实验表明，现有方法在真实场景的微动作理解上仍面临挑战，尤其是在域迁移、长尾类别分布和复杂时序定位方面。除了基准测试，我们还研究了微动作与情绪之间的关系，结果显示微动作与情绪状态高度相关，并为面部微表情提供了互补线索，可用于改进情绪识别。这些结果表明，MMA-82是一个全面且富有挑战性的真实微动作分析基准，也是以人为中心的人工智能领域的宝贵资源。MMA-82可在https://github.com/LpyNow/MMA-82获取。

</details>


### [31] [Conditioning Matters: Stabilizing Inversion and Attention in Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14125)
*Zheyuan Zhan,Hongchen Li,Can Wang,Yinfei Ma,Mingzhen Huang,Ruoshi Bai,Jiawei Chen,Siwei Lyu,Defang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SimEdit，一种基于条件感知的框架，通过条件精炼和逐token跨分支注意力控制，解决了基于反转的图像编辑中反转不准确以及编辑保真度与背景保留之间的权衡问题，在PIE-Bench上取得了优于以往注意力操控方法的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于反转的图像编辑方法虽灵活且无需训练，但受到反转精度以及编辑保真度与背景保留之间权衡的困扰。近期方法改进了反转公式或注意力交互，但文本条件在塑造扩散动力学和编辑行为中的作用仍未得到充分探索。作者通过理论和实验证明，文本条件的精度通过调制扩散速度场的几何结构影响反转稳定性，同时影响编辑过程中跨分支注意力的一致性，这些效应直接影响背景保留和语义保真度。

Method: SimEdit框架包含两个互补组件：(a) 条件精炼（conditioning refinement）——构建具有更高语义精度和结构对齐度的条件信号，以实现稳定的反转和一致的注意力操控；(b) 逐token跨分支注意力控制（token-wise cross-branch attention control）——分离编辑相关组件与结构保留组件，并在注意力操控过程中对它们进行非对称调制。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上的大量实验表明，SimEdit在反转重建质量和编辑性能上均持续优于以往的注意力操控方法。

Conclusion: 文本条件的精度对扩散模型的反转稳定性和编辑行为具有重要影响，SimEdit通过条件精炼和逐token注意力控制有效提升了编辑保真度和背景保留能力。

Original Abstract: Inversion-based image editing offers flexible and training-free control but still struggles with inversion accuracy and the trade-off between editing fidelity and background preservation. While recent methods improve inversion formulations or attention interactions, the role of textual conditioning in shaping diffusion dynamics and editing behavior remains underexplored. We show both empirically and theoretically that the precision of textual conditioning influences inversion stability by modulating the geometry of the diffusion velocity field, while also affecting the consistency of cross-branch attention during editing. These effects directly impact background preservation and semantic fidelity. Building on this analysis, we propose SimEdit, a conditioning-aware framework with two complementary components: (a) conditioning refinement, which constructs conditioning signals with improved semantic precision and structural alignment to facilitate stable inversion and consistent attention manipulation, and (b) token-wise cross-branch attention control, which separates edit-relevant and structure-preserving components and modulates them asymmetrically during attention manipulation. Extensive experiments on PIE-Bench demonstrate that SimEdit consistently improves both inversion reconstruction quality and editing performance over previous attention-manipulation approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/zju-pi/SimEdit.

中文摘要翻译: 基于反转的图像编辑方法提供了灵活且无需训练的控制方式，但仍受限于反转精度以及编辑保真度与背景保留之间的权衡。尽管近期方法改进了反转公式或注意力交互，但文本条件在塑造扩散动力学和编辑行为中的作用仍未得到充分探索。我们通过经验和理论证明，文本条件的精度通过调制扩散速度场的几何结构影响反转稳定性，同时影响编辑过程中跨分支注意力的一致性。这些效应直接影响背景保留和语义保真度。基于此分析，我们提出SimEdit，一个条件感知框架，包含两个互补组件：(a) 条件精炼——构建具有更高语义精度和结构对齐度的条件信号，以促进稳定的反转和一致的注意力操控；(b) 逐token跨分支注意力控制——分离编辑相关组件和结构保留组件，并在注意力操控过程中对它们进行非对称调制。在PIE-Bench上的大量实验表明，SimEdit在反转重建质量和编辑性能上均持续优于以往的注意力操控方法。我们的代码已开源在https://github.com/zju-pi/SimEdit。

</details>


### [32] [BoRAD: Bootstrap your Own Representations for Multi-class Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14129)
*Duy Hoang Khuong,Tri Nguyen Minh,Ngu Huynh Cong Viet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoRAD 提出了一种无需标签的统一异常检测训练框架，通过可学习原型库施加空间原型对齐和原型相对全局对齐两种正则化，解决了单一模型在工业检测中复制异常细节和混淆正常类别的问题，在多个数据集上取得了竞争性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将基于重建的异常检测从类别特定训练扩展到统一模型（one-for-all）时，单一模型需要重建多种正常外观但不复制异常细节，这面临两个耦合的失败模式：恒等捷径（异常通过重建路径被复制）和错误重建（不同正常类别之间相互混淆）。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出 BoRAD 框架，将其视为表征容量分配问题。使用共享的可学习原型库施加两种互补正则化：空间原型对齐（缩小局部原型内变异以抑制异常复制）和原型相对全局对齐（保持原型间结构以提高对异常角度偏差的敏感性）。原型库和预测头仅在训练阶段使用，推理时保持标准师生特征差异流程，无需类别标签、负样本对、记忆检索或原型查找。

Result: 在 MVTec AD 上达到 86.2% mAD，VisA 上 80.7% mAD，Real-IAD 上 73.1% mAD。诊断分析表明该方法减少了异常泄漏，改善了正常类别可分性，增强了异常-正常分数分离。

Conclusion: BoRAD 通过原型库正则化有效解决了统一异常检测中异常复制和类别混淆的耦合问题，实现了无需标签的 competitive 性能，且推理阶段保持简洁高效。

Original Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detection is attractive for industrial inspection, but scaling it from category-specific training to a one-for-all setting is challenging. A single model must reconstruct diverse normal appearances without copying abnormal details, which exposes two coupled failure modes: identical shortcut, where anomalies pass through the reconstruction path, and mis-reconstruction, where normal categories are confused with one another. We propose \textbf{BoRAD}, a label-free training framework that treats this as a representation-capacity allocation problem. BoRAD uses a shared learnable prototype bank to impose two complementary regularizers: spatial prototype alignment contracts local within-prototype variation to suppress anomaly copying, while prototype-relative global alignment preserves between-prototype structure and improves sensitivity to abnormal angular deviations. The prototype bank and prediction heads are used only during training; inference remains a standard teacher-student feature discrepancy pass, with no class labels, negative pairs, memory retrieval, or prototype lookup. BoRAD achieves competitive one-for-all anomaly detection performance, including 86.2\% mAD on MVTec AD, 80.7\% mAD on VisA and 73.1\% mAD on Real-IAD. Diagnostic analyses further show reduced anomaly leakage, improved normal-category separability, and stronger anomaly-normal score separation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于重建的异常检测在工业检测中具有吸引力，但将其从类别特定训练扩展到统一模型（one-for-all）设置具有挑战性。单一模型必须重建多样的正常外观而不复制异常细节，这暴露了两个耦合的失败模式：恒等捷径（异常通过重建路径被复制）和错误重建（正常类别之间相互混淆）。我们提出 BoRAD，一个无需标签的训练框架，将其视为表征容量分配问题。BoRAD 使用共享的可学习原型库施加两种互补正则化：空间原型对齐缩小局部原型内变异以抑制异常复制，而原型相对全局对齐保持原型间结构并提高对异常角度偏差的敏感性。原型库和预测头仅在训练阶段使用；推理时保持标准的师生特征差异流程，无需类别标签、负样本对、记忆检索或原型查找。BoRAD 实现了具有竞争力的统一异常检测性能，包括 MVTec AD 上 86.2% 的 mAD，VisA 上 80.7% 的 mAD 和 Real-IAD 上 73.1% 的 mAD。诊断分析进一步表明异常泄漏减少、正常类别可分性改善以及异常-正常分数分离增强。

</details>


### [33] [Encoder Winners Do Not Reliably Transfer Across VLA Backbone Scale: A Frozen-Backbone Grafting Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14153)
*Qingping Zeng,Fei She*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“冻结主干嫁接”诊断方法，发现小规模VLA骨干上最优的视觉编码器选择无法可靠迁移到大规模骨干，编码器选择存在骨干依赖性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常直接沿用上游VLM发布的视觉编码器，但尚不清楚在小规模VLA上验证的最优编码器选择是否能够迁移到更大的骨干网络上，缺乏系统的诊断方法。

Method: 提出“冻结主干嫁接”诊断协议：固定语言模型和动作专家参数，将目标视觉编码器替换到VLA中，仅通过自适应平均池化、LayerNorm和一个可训练的线性投影器进行适配。在4个编码器、2个LIBERO套件、2个骨干（SmolVLA-450M和π0.5-3.3B）上进行了40次主嫁接实验及多种对照实验，使用离线动作MSE作为评价指标。

Result: 小骨干上的最优编码器不能可靠地预测大骨干上的最优选择：SigLIP在SmolVLA上双套件均最优，而在π0.5上DINOv2-small在空间套件领先，目标套件则为种子敏感的接近平局带；4组骨干-套件比较中有3组（以及12个种子级单元中的11个）支持骨干依赖的排名。嫁接包装本身非中性，在两个骨干上符号相反（SmolVLA原生塔上MSE增加45-56%，π0.5上降低50-52%）。

Conclusion: 冻结嫁接是一种低成本的目标骨干诊断工具，可用于在投入大规模训练前为编码器选择提供参考，而非作为闭环部署的最终结论。

Original Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies typically inherit their vision encoder from upstream VLM releases, but it is unclear whether an encoder choice validated on a small VLA transfers to a larger backbone. We introduce a frozen-backbone grafting diagnostic: the vision tower of a released VLA is replaced by a candidate encoder under a fixed protocol (adaptive average pooling, LayerNorm, and a single trainable linear projector), with the language model and action expert frozen. Across four encoders, two LIBERO suites, two backbones (SmolVLA-450M and $π_{0.5}$-3.3B), and two-to-three seeds per cell (40 main grafting runs plus native, LoRA, pooling, and zero-/shuffled-image controls, all scored by offline action MSE), the small-backbone winner does not reliably select the large-backbone top tier: SigLIP is best on SmolVLA across both suites, while on $π_{0.5}$ DINOv2-small leads the spatial suite and the object suite is a seed-sensitive near-tie band; three of the four backbone-suite comparisons (and 11 of 12 seed-level cells) support backbone-dependent rankings. The grafting wrapper is itself non-neutral with opposite sign across backbones (+45-56% MSE on the SmolVLA native tower, -50-52% on $π_{0.5}$), so all conclusions are conditional on the fixed grafting protocol. We position frozen grafting as a cheap target-backbone diagnostic to run before committing to an encoder at scale, not as a closed-loop deployment claim.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常从上游VLM发布中继承其视觉编码器，但尚不清楚在小规模VLA上验证的编码器选择是否能迁移到更大的骨干网络上。我们引入了一种冻结骨干嫁接诊断方法：将已发布VLA的视觉塔替换为候选编码器，采用固定协议（自适应平均池化、LayerNorm和一个可训练的线性投影器），语言模型和动作专家保持冻结。在四个编码器、两个LIBERO套件、两个骨干（SmolVLA-450M和π0.5-3.3B）以及每单元两到三个种子下（40次主嫁接实验，加上原生、LoRA、池化以及零/混洗图像对照实验，全部通过离线动作MSE评分），小骨干上的优胜者无法可靠地选出大骨干上的顶级选择：SigLIP在SmolVLA上两个套件均表现最佳，而在π0.5上DINOv2-small在空间套件领先，目标套件则为种子敏感的接近平局带；四组骨干-套件比较中有三组（以及12个种子级单元中的11个）支持骨干依赖的排名。嫁接包装本身并非中性，在两个骨干上符号相反（SmolVLA原生塔上MSE增加45-56%，π0.5上降低50-52%），因此所有结论均依赖于固定的嫁接协议。我们将冻结嫁接定位为一种低成本的目标骨干诊断工具，在投入大规模训练前为编码器选择提供参考，而非作为闭环部署的最终结论。

</details>


### [34] [VideoWeave: Unlocking Geometric Consistency in Video Generation via Joint Geometry-Video Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14162)
*Xunzhi Xiang,Zixuan Duan,Yabo Chen,Zhengxuan Wei,Guiyu Zhang,Zixiao Gu,Zhe Gao,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Qi Fan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoWeave提出了一种隐空间后训练框架，通过隐式几何模型特征约束视频扩散模型的生成分布，无需显式几何重建，从而提升视频生成的几何一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型在视角变化下常出现几何漂移和运动不合理问题，现有方法依赖显式几何重建（如深度图、点云等）来约束生成，但这类方法对上游几何管线的误差敏感，鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出VideoWeave后训练框架：将隐式几何模型特征适配为几何隐变量，与视频隐变量在共享去噪空间中联合建模，使几何信息在训练中塑造生成分布；同时构建了包含8万视频的GeoVid-80K数据集，提供配对的表观与几何表征。

Result: 在文本生成视频和图像生成视频任务上，VideoWeave在保持高质量视觉表现的同时，显著提升了视频的几何连贯性。

Conclusion: VideoWeave通过隐式几何约束替代显式几何重建，有效缓解了视频扩散模型中的几何漂移问题，提供了一种更灵活、鲁棒的几何一致性增强方案。

Original Abstract: Large-scale video diffusion models often fail to preserve 3D structure over time, causing geometric drift and implausible motion under viewpoint changes. Existing methods usually enforce geometric consistency by using explicit geometry reconstructions, such as depth maps, point clouds, or reconstructed 3D structures, to define conditions, supervision, or reward signals, making the generator sensitive to errors from upstream geometry pipelines. We propose VideoWeave, a latent-space post-training framework that uses implicit geometry-model features to constrain the generative distribution, providing a more flexible and non-rigid form of guidance that mitigates the impact of reconstruction errors from geometry models. Specifically, VideoWeave adapts these features into geometry latents and jointly models them with video latents in a shared denoising space, allowing geometry to shape the generative distribution during training. To support this process, we build GeoVid-80K, an 80K-video dataset with paired appearance and geometry representations. Experiments on text-to-video and image-to-video generation show that VideoWeave improves geometric coherence while preserving strong visual quality. VideoWeave project page at https://videoweave.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 大规模视频扩散模型通常难以在时间维度上保持3D结构，导致在视角变化下出现几何漂移和不合理的运动。现有方法通常通过使用显式几何重建（如深度图、点云或重构的3D结构）来定义条件、监督或奖励信号以施加几何一致性，这使得生成器对上游几何管线的误差敏感。我们提出VideoWeave，一种隐空间后训练框架，利用隐式几何模型特征约束生成分布，提供一种更灵活、非刚性的引导形式，从而减轻几何模型重建误差的影响。具体而言，VideoWeave将这些特征适配为几何隐变量，并与视频隐变量在共享去噪空间中联合建模，使得几何信息能够在训练过程中塑造生成分布。为支持这一过程，我们构建了GeoVid-80K数据集，包含8万个视频及配对的表观与几何表征。在文本生成视频和图像生成视频任务上的实验表明，VideoWeave在保持强视觉质量的同时提升了几何连贯性。项目页面：https://videoweave.github.io/

</details>


### [35] [MUSE: Agentic 3D Scene Authoring via Memory-Grounded Incremental Requirement Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14168)
*Ruijie Xu,Xinnan Zhu,Jiayu Ying,Daoguo Dong,Yuzhou Ji,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MUSE，一种基于记忆的多智能体框架，用于可控3D场景创作，通过增量需求满足实现场景的构建与编辑，并在作者基准上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D场景生成方法虽能产生逼真的场景，但缺乏需求级别的可编辑性和状态追踪能力，导致局部失败常常需要全局重新生成或人工干预。

Method: MUSE是一个基于记忆的多智能体框架，包含三个智能体：Architect（将指令编译为结构化需求）、Sculptor（执行局部场景操作）、Inspector（验证每一步并更新工作记忆、场景记忆和技能记忆）。同时提出AuthorBench基准用于评估需求级可控性和保留感知编辑能力。

Result: 在完整构建任务上，MUSE的All-Goal成功率从37.9提升至80.7，表面约束满足率从35.0提升至92.6；在240例编辑测试中，All-Goal成功率达49.6，保留率为99.9%，非预期修改率仅0.6%。

Conclusion: MUSE作为可控3D场景创作的有效框架，在需求级可控性和保留感知编辑方面均优于现有方法，消融实验验证了记忆设计的有效性。

Original Abstract: Text-driven 3D scene generation is a promising technique for digital content creation, embodied AI simulation, and interactive design, yet practical workflows often require refining, extending, or correcting existing scenes while preserving non-target content. Existing methods can produce realistic and structurally plausible scenes, but they generally lack editability with requirement-level state tracking, so part-level failures often lead to full-scene regeneration or manual intervention. To tackle this challenge, we formulate controllable 3D scene authoring as incremental requirement satisfaction, unifying construction and editing. In this paper, we present MUSE, a memory-grounded multi-agent framework in which an Architect compiles instructions into structured requirements, a Sculptor executes local scene operations, and an Inspector verifies each step while updating Working, Scene, and Skill Memory. To evaluate requirement-level controllability and preservation-aware editing, we introduce AuthorBench, offering 145 constrained construction cases and a 1,584-case preservation-aware editing pool paired with external structured checks. On full construction cases, MUSE improves All-Goal success from 37.9 to 80.7 and surface-constraint fulfillment from 35.0 to 92.6 over the strongest baseline. On a stratified 240-case editing test split, MUSE achieves 49.6 All-Goal success, 99.9 preservation rate, and only 0.6 unintended change rate. Beyond automated metrics, human evaluations on compared local-editing baselines support stronger alignment with user intent, and downstream navigation-proxy tests indicate stronger spatial stability. Combined with ablations validating our memory designs, these results establish MUSE as an effective framework for controllable 3D scene authoring.

中文摘要翻译: 文本驱动的3D场景生成是数字内容创作、具身AI模拟和交互设计中的一项有前景的技术，然而实际工作流程通常需要在保留非目标内容的同时对现有场景进行细化、扩展或修正。现有方法能够生成逼真且结构合理的场景，但普遍缺乏带有需求级状态追踪的可编辑性，因此局部失败往往导致整个场景的重新生成或人工干预。为应对这一挑战，我们将可控的3D场景创作形式化为增量需求满足问题，统一了构建与编辑过程。在本文中，我们提出了MUSE，一个基于记忆的多智能体框架，其中包含一个Architect（将指令编译为结构化需求）、一个Sculptor（执行局部场景操作）以及一个Inspector（验证每一步并更新工作记忆、场景记忆和技能记忆）。为了评估需求级可控性和保留感知编辑能力，我们引入了AuthorBench，提供了145个带约束的构建案例和1,584个保留感知编辑案例池，并辅以外部结构化检查。在完整构建案例上，MUSE的All-Goal成功率从最强基线的37.9提升至80.7，表面约束满足率从35.0提升至92.6。在分层抽样的240例编辑测试集上，MUSE实现了49.6的All-Goal成功率、99.9%的保留率以及仅0.6%的非预期修改率。除自动化指标外，在局部编辑基线对比中的人工评估表明，MUSE与用户意图的对齐程度更强，下游导航代理测试也显示出更强的空间稳定性。结合验证我们记忆设计的消融实验，这些结果确立了MUSE作为可控3D场景创作的有效框架。

</details>


### [36] [Hybrid Classical-Quantum (HCQ) Alzheimer's Classification via Supervised $β$-VAE and Quantum Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14194)
*Tia Tiwari,Vamshi Krishna Kancharla,Neelam Sinha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段混合经典-量子（HCQ）流水线，用于基于3D T1加权结构MRI的阿尔茨海默病二元分类。量子核在监督自编码器端到端学习的疾病感知特征上运行，而非预提取输入。在308名ADNI-1受试者上，稳定性增强变体达到72.1%准确率和0.799 AUC。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有经典机器学习方法在处理高维医学影像（如3D MRI）时面临挑战，而纯量子方法受限于可处理的数据维度。本文意在通过混合经典-量子架构，让经典组件（3D β-VAE）学习疾病相关的紧凑潜在表示，量子组件（量子核SVM）利用量子优势在高维希尔伯特空间中进行更优的分类，两者互补而非独立运行。

Method: 1）训练一个端到端的3D β-变分自编码器（β-VAE），联合优化体素级重建损失、KL散度和焦点分类损失，将3D MRI（96×96×96）压缩为64维潜在编码；2）使用偏最小二乘（PLS）回归从64维编码中选择6个最具判别力的成分，并重新缩放到旋转角度；3）采用ZZ量子特征映射将这6个角度编码到6量子比特寄存器上得到量子态；4）计算每对量子态之间的重叠（内积）构建N×N Gram矩阵；5）将该Gram矩阵作为预计算核输入SVM进行分类。

Result: 在ADNI-1数据集的308名受试者（137例AD，171例CN）上：基线版本准确率67.2%，AUC 0.759；稳定性增强变体准确率72.1%，AUC 0.799，且交叉验证方差减半。3D Grad-CAM热图显示模型关注的脑区与阿尔茨海默病已知相关脑区一致。

Conclusion: 提出的HCQ流水线在AD分类任务上取得了有竞争力的性能，其新颖之处在于量子核直接操作于监督自编码器端到端学习的疾病感知潜在特征，而非预提取特征。该方法可作为跨生物医学影像诊断分类的通用框架。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a two-stage Hybrid Classical-Quantum (HCQ) pipeline for binary Alzheimer's disease (AD) classification from 3D T1-weighted structural MRI volumes, where the classical and quantum components are designed to complement each other rather than operate independently. A supervised 3D $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE) is trained end-to-end under voxel-wise reconstruction, KL-divergence, and focal classification losses that compress each 3D MRI volume (resized from 152 x 184 x 152 to 96 x 96 x 96) into a 64-dimensional latent code. Partial Least Squares (PLS) regression selects the six components in the latent code that best separate Alzheimer's Disease (AD) from cognitively normal (CN) subjects and rescales them into rotation angles, which are encoded onto a six-qubit register using the ZZ quantum feature map to give us the respective quantum states. The input to a precomputed-kernel Support Vector Machine (SVM) is an N x N Gram matrix (N = 308), created by calculating the overlap between every pair of quantum states. The novelty of this work lies in the fact that the quantum kernel operates directly on disease-aware features that are learned end-to-end by a supervised autoencoder, rather than on pre-extracted inputs. On 308 ADNI-1 subjects, consisting of 137 AD and 171 CN subjects, the baseline achieved 67.2% accuracy and 0.759 AUC, while the stability-enhanced variant reached 72.1% accuracy and 0.799 AUC with cross-fold variance halved. 3D Grad-CAM further helped validate our model's focus on brain regions linked to Alzheimer's. The HCQ pipeline could serve as a general-purpose framework for diagnostic classification across biomedical imaging domains that present similar challenges for classical approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种两阶段混合经典-量子（HCQ）流水线，用于从3D T1加权结构MRI体数据中进行二元阿尔茨海默病分类，其中经典与量子组件被设计为相互补充而非独立运行。一个受监督的3D β-变分自编码器在体素级重建损失、KL散度和焦点分类损失的联合监督下进行端到端训练，将每个3D MRI体数据（从152×184×152重采样至96×96×96）压缩为64维潜在编码。偏最小二乘回归从潜在编码中选取六个最能区分阿尔茨海默病与认知正常受试者的成分，并将其重新缩放到旋转角度，再通过ZZ量子特征映射将这六个角度编码到六量子比特寄存器上，得到相应的量子态。预计算核支持向量机的输入是一个N×N的Gram矩阵（N=308），该矩阵通过计算每对量子态之间的重叠得到。本文的新颖之处在于：量子核直接操作于由监督自编码器端到端学习的疾病感知特征，而非预提取的输入特征。在包含137例AD和171例CN共308名ADNI-1受试者上，基线版本达到67.2%的准确率和0.759的AUC，而稳定性增强变体达到72.1%的准确率和0.799的AUC，且交叉验证方差减半。3D Grad-CAM进一步帮助验证了模型对阿尔茨海默病相关脑区的关注。该HCQ流水线可作为跨生物医学影像领域的诊断分类通用框架，适用于那些对经典方法存在类似挑战的应用场景。

</details>


### [37] [A Multi-Domain Feature Fusion Framework for Generalizable Deepfake Detection Across Different Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14230)
*Amna Amjid,Sana Qadir,Mehwish Fatima,Raja Khurram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多域深度融合网络SGFF-Net，通过结合空间、梯度和基于DWT的频率表示，在双残差学习架构下，显著提升了跨生成器和跨范式的深度伪造检测鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法多基于单一域（空间或频域），对扩散模型生成的图像检测效果不佳，且缺乏多域互补表示的系统利用以及跨生成器鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 提出SGFF-Net，该框架将空间域、梯度域和基于离散小波变换（DWT）的频率域表示集成到双残差学习架构中，实现多域特征融合与互补性取证线索学习。

Result: 内数据集评估准确率达98.95%；跨模型设置下准确率70.46%，引入多源训练和数据增强后提升至79.80%；跨范式评估从69%提升至78%；真实世界数据从61.50%提升至75.80%。

Conclusion: 多域表示融合结合数据多样性与增强能显著提升深度伪造检测的泛化能力，为构建更可靠的检测系统提供了实用思路。

Original Abstract: Deepfakes are artificially generated images, audio, or videos that threaten privacy, security, and information integrity. Detecting such content is crucial for countering disinformation, as the latest models generate highly realistic content. While spatial- or frequency-based approaches achieve good detection rates on Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs)-based generated deepfakes, they often struggle with recent diffusion model-generated images. In particular, existing approaches rarely exploit complementary multi-domain representations or systematically evaluate cross-generator robustness. To address these challenges, we propose a multi-domain deepfake detection framework called SGFF-Net (Spatial-Gradient-Frequency Fusion Network) that integrates spatial, gradient, and DWT (Discrete Wavelet Transform)-based frequency representations within a dual residual learning architecture. Experimental results show that the SGFF-Net achieves 98.95\% accuracy in intra-dataset evaluation and improves performance in both cross-model (70.46\%) and cross-paradigm (69.94\%) settings. Incorporating multi-source training and data augmentation further enhances robustness, increasing accuracy from 70.46\% to 79.80\% in cross-model evaluation, from 69\% to 78\% in cross-paradigm evaluation, and from 61.50\% to 75.80\% on real-world data. Unlike single-domain detectors, the SGFF-Net learns complementary forensic cues across spatial, gradient, and wavelet-frequency domains, resulting in greater robustness under cross-generator and cross-paradigm evaluation. The results further show that combining multi-domain representations with data diversity and augmentation substantially improves generalization, providing practical insights for developing more reliable deepfake detection systems.

中文摘要翻译: 深度伪造是人工生成的图像、音频或视频，对隐私、安全和信息完整性构成威胁。检测此类内容对于打击虚假信息至关重要，因为最新模型能够生成高度逼真的内容。基于空间域或频率域的检测方法在生成对抗网络（GAN）生成的深度伪造上取得了良好的检测率，但面对最近的扩散模型生成图像时往往表现不佳。特别是，现有方法很少利用互补的多域表示或系统性地评估跨生成器的鲁棒性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种多域深度伪造检测框架SGFF-Net（空间-梯度-频率融合网络），该网络将空间、梯度和基于离散小波变换（DWT）的频率表示集成到双残差学习架构中。实验结果表明，SGFF-Net在内数据集评估中达到了98.95%的准确率，并在跨模型（70.46%）和跨范式（69.94%）设置下均提升了性能。结合多源训练和数据增强进一步增强了鲁棒性，使跨模型评估的准确率从70.46%提升至79.80%，跨范式评估从69%提升至78%，真实世界数据从61.50%提升至75.80%。与单域检测器不同，SGFF-Net能够跨空间、梯度和小波-频率域学习互补的取证线索，从而在跨生成器和跨范式评估中表现出更强的鲁棒性。结果进一步表明，将多域表示与数据多样性和增强相结合能显著提升泛化能力，为开发更可靠的深度伪造检测系统提供了实用见解。

</details>


### [38] [HiST: A Hierarchical Sparse Transformer for Cross-Modal Spatial Transcriptomics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14251)
*Weiyi Wu,Xinwen Xu,Xingjian Diao,Siting Li,Zhi Wei,Alma Andersson,Jiang Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiST提出了一种层次化稀疏Transformer，用于从全切片H&E图像直接推断空间转录组表达，通过稀疏窗口注意力、分辨率变换算子及玻片校准令牌，显著提升多器官推断性能并降低运行时间和内存消耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 空间转录组学虽能将基因表达与组织形态关联，但成本高、通量低，因此需要从常规H&E染色组织切片推断基因表达的替代方法。然而，全切片H&E与稀疏、不规则分布的空间转录组位点之间的多尺度建模面临挑战，现有方法要么引入密集网格开销，要么使用二次复杂度的令牌混合。

Method: HiST将测量位点视为以网格索引的稀疏场，直接在活跃组织区域上构建二元编码器-解码器。结合稀疏窗口注意力实现局部几何对应，利用分辨率变换算子实现快速多尺度上下文整合。计算复杂度和内存消耗仅随观测位点数量线性增长，而非全切片面积。此外，通过玻片校准令牌（Slide Calibration Token）引入瓶颈式全局条件路径，对滑动特异性采集变异进行校准。

Result: 在多器官基准数据集上，HiST在预测性能上优于已有基线方法，同时显著降低了运行时间和峰值内存消耗。

Conclusion: HiST证明了层次化稀疏建模在病理全切片图像到空间转录组推断中的有效性，兼顾高效性与准确性，为大规模临床应用铺平了道路。

Original Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) links gene expression with tissue morphology but remains expensive and low-throughput, motivating surrogates that infer expression from routine histology. Whole-slide H&E-to-ST inference pairs a gigapixel image with gene measurements at a sparse, irregular set of locations, making multiscale modeling challenging without incurring dense-grid overhead or quadratic token mixing. We propose HiST, a hierarchical sparse transformer that treats measured locations as a lattice-indexed sparse field and builds a dyadic encoder--decoder directly on the active tissue footprint. HiST combines sparse window attention for local geometric correspondence with resolution-changing operators for rapid multiscale context integration. For a fixed window size, the dominant runtime and memory scale with the number of observed locations rather than the dense slide area. To mitigate slide-specific acquisition variation, HiST adds a bottlenecked global conditioning pathway via a \emph{slide calibration token} that summarizes slide-level context and conditions local representations. On a multi-organ benchmark spanning diverse tissues and acquisition sources, HiST improves predictive performance over recent baselines while reducing runtime and peak memory.

中文摘要翻译: 空间转录组学（ST）将基因表达与组织形态联系起来，但仍存在成本高、通量低的问题，这促使研究者寻找从常规组织学切片推断基因表达的替代方法。全切片H&E到ST的推断任务需要将千兆像素级图像与稀疏、不规则位置上的基因测量值进行匹配，这使得在不引入密集网格开销或二次复杂度令牌混合的情况下进行多尺度建模充满挑战。我们提出HiST——一种层次化稀疏Transformer，它将测量位点视为网格索引的稀疏场，并直接在活跃组织区域上构建二元编码器-解码器。HiST将稀疏窗口注意力用于局部几何对应，结合分辨率变换算子实现快速多尺度上下文整合。在固定窗口大小下，主要运行时间和内存消耗随观测位点数量增长，而非全切片面积增长。为缓解切片特异性采集变异，HiST通过一个玻片校准令牌引入瓶颈式全局条件路径，该令牌总结切片级上下文并对局部表示进行条件化。在一个涵盖多种组织和采集来源的多器官基准数据集上，HiST在提升预测性能的同时，降低了运行时间和峰值内存消耗。

</details>


### [39] [One Layer's Trash is Another Layer's Treasure: Adaptive Layer-wise Visual Token Selection in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14277)
*Yongru Chen,Kai Zhang,Zeliang Zong,Yuchen Lu,Wenming Tan,Ye Ren,Jilin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ALVTS，一种分层的自适应视觉令牌选择方法，通过轻量级选择器为不同层保留重要令牌、跳过次要令牌，在89%压缩比下保持96.7%的原始精度，实现了LVLM高效推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉令牌剪枝方法在特定层剪除令牌后，后续所有层都无法再访问这些令牌，导致过早的信息丢失。而实验发现不同层关注的视觉区域不同，每层需要的最优令牌子集也各不相同，因此需要一种分层的自适应令牌选择方法。

Method: 提出ALVTS框架，包含轻量级令牌选择器，为每层识别重要令牌（继续处理）和非重要令牌（跳过该层），两个流在送入下一层前重新整合。基于重要性一致性约束的低秩近似，使选择模块逼近全注意力机制，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT和Qwen2.5-VL上验证有效性。89%令牌压缩比下，ALVTS保留原始模型96.7%的准确率，实现了优越的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: ALVTS通过打破静态令牌剪枝范式，实现分层自适应视觉令牌选择，在显著压缩计算量的同时保持了模型性能，为LVLM的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success across diverse multimodal tasks, yet their practical deployment remains constrained by the computational burden arising from lengthy visual tokens. While visual token pruning has emerged as a promising solution, existing methods suffer from a fundamental limitation: once tokens are pruned at a specific layer, they become inaccessible to all subsequent layers, leading to premature information loss that can compromise model performance. Through empirical studies, we observe that different layers exhibit distinct visual region focus, indicating a varying optimal token subset across layers. Motivated by this insight, we propose Adaptive Layer-wise Visual Token Selection (ALVTS), a novel framework that breaks away from the conventional static token pruning paradigm. ALVTS incorporates a lightweight token selector to identify and route important tokens for further processing, while allowing less important tokens to skip the layer, thus minimizing computational redundancy. These two streams of tokens are seamlessly reintegrated before being fed into subsequent layers, facilitating adaptive compression across the entire model. Grounded in our importance consistency constrained low-rank approximation, the proposed token selection module closely emulates the full attention mechanism, effectively capturing its essential patterns without requiring model retraining. Extensive experiments on LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, and Qwen2.5-VL validate the effectiveness of our method. With an 89% token compression ratio, ALVTS retains 96.7% of the original model's accuracy, achieving a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off for LVLM inference.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在多种多模态任务中取得了显著成功，但其实际部署仍受到由冗长的视觉令牌带来的计算负担的制约。虽然视觉令牌剪枝已成为一种有前景的解决方案，但现有方法存在一个根本性局限：一旦令牌在特定层被剪除，后续所有层都无法再访问这些令牌，导致过早的信息丢失，从而损害模型性能。通过实验研究，我们观察到不同层表现出不同的视觉区域关注，表明各层需要的最优令牌子集不同。受此启发，我们提出自适应分层视觉令牌选择（ALVTS），这是一种突破传统静态令牌剪枝范式的新型框架。ALVTS集成了一个轻量级令牌选择器，用于识别重要令牌并将其路由到后续处理，同时让较不重要的令牌跳过该层，从而最小化计算冗余。这两个令牌流在送入后续层之前被无缝地重新整合，实现了整个模型的自适应压缩。基于我们提出的重要性一致性约束低秩近似，所提出的令牌选择模块紧密模拟了全注意力机制，能够有效捕获其核心模式，且无需重新训练模型。在LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT和Qwen2.5-VL上进行的大量实验验证了我们方法的有效性。在89%的令牌压缩比下，ALVTS保留了原始模型96.7%的准确率，实现了LVLM推理中优越的效率-精度权衡。

</details>


### [40] [A Robust Point Cloud Analysis Framework Inspired By Primary Visual Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14292)
*Jisheng Dang,Dengyue Pan,Delin Deng,Yifan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于脑启发的树突连接连续耦合神经网络（DC-CCNN及其扩展DC-CCNN++），用于点云分析，在保持与SOTA相当性能的同时，显著提升了鲁棒性和能效，特别是在稀疏、遮挡、噪声和空间变换等复杂干扰条件下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于CNN的点云分析方法存在能耗高和鲁棒性不足的问题，受限于CNN本身的结构局限性。受初级视觉皮层启发，本文旨在设计一种更高效、更鲁棒的类脑神经网络架构来替代传统的MLP。

Method: 1) 提出DC-CCNN，通过离散与连续编码相结合的方式，用类脑神经网络替代传统MLP；2) 进一步提出DC-CCNN++，引入神经启发的鲁棒调制与读出模块（NRMR），通过全局上下文增益调制和双码证据整合增强特征稳定性与决策鲁棒性；3) 设计皮层启发的渐进变异性训练策略（CPVT），在训练中逐步引入结构化的环境变异，同时保持稳定的干净样本锚点。

Result: DC-CCNN++在点云分类和部件分割任务上均优于原始DC-CCNN，性能与最先进方法相当；在稀疏性、遮挡、高斯噪声、椒盐噪声和空间变换等复杂干扰条件下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DC-CCNN++凭借其高效性、鲁棒性和生物可解释的设计，为点云分析提供了一种有前景的传统深度学习方法替代方案。

Original Abstract: Despite significant advancements in point cloud analysis, reducing energy consumption and improving robustness remain understudied, largely due to the inherent limitations of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). To address this issue, we draw inspiration from the primary visual cortex and propose a Dendritic-Connected Continuous-Coupled Neural Network (DC-CCNN), a novel Brain-Inspired Neural Network (BINN) architecture for point cloud analysis. By combining discrete and continuous encoding, our design replaces traditional Multilayer Perceptrons (MLPs) with more efficient and robust BINNs. Building upon this framework, we further propose an extended model, DC-CCNN++, to improve robustness under complex corruption conditions. Specifically, we introduce a Neuro-Inspired Robust Modulation-and-Readout Module (NRMR) to enhance feature stability and decision robustness through global-context gain modulation and dual-code evidence integration. We also design a Cortically Inspired Progressive Variability Training (CPVT) strategy, which progressively exposes the model to structured environmental variability while preserving stable clean-sample anchors during training. Experimental results show that DC-CCNN++ improves the performance of brain-inspired networks on point cloud analysis while maintaining performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods. Compared with the original DC-CCNN, it achieves stronger results on both classification and part segmentation, and exhibits enhanced robustness against sparsity, occlusion, Gaussian noise, salt-and-pepper noise, and spatial transformations. With its efficiency, robustness, and biologically grounded design, DC-CCNN++ provides a promising alternative to traditional deep learning methods for point cloud analysis. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DC-CCNNpp-44E3.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管点云分析取得了显著进展，但在降低能耗和提升鲁棒性方面的研究仍显不足，这很大程度上归因于卷积神经网络（CNN）固有的局限性。为解决这一问题，我们从初级视觉皮层中汲取灵感，提出了一种树突连接连续耦合神经网络（DC-CCNN），这是一种用于点云分析的新型脑启发神经网络（BINN）架构。通过结合离散编码与连续编码，我们的设计用更高效、更鲁棒的BINN取代了传统的多层感知器（MLP）。在此框架基础上，我们进一步提出了扩展模型DC-CCNN++，以提升在复杂干扰条件下的鲁棒性。具体而言，我们引入了一个神经启发的鲁棒调制与读出模块（NRMR），通过全局上下文增益调制和双码证据整合来增强特征稳定性与决策鲁棒性。我们还设计了一种皮层启发的渐进变异性训练（CPVT）策略，该策略在训练过程中逐步将模型暴露于结构化的环境变异性中，同时保留稳定的干净样本锚点。实验结果表明，DC-CCNN++在点云分析上提升了脑启发网络的性能，同时保持了与最先进方法相当的表现。与原始DC-CCNN相比，它在分类和部件分割任务上均取得了更强的结果，并且在面对稀疏性、遮挡、高斯噪声、椒盐噪声和空间变换时展现出增强的鲁棒性。凭借其高效性、鲁棒性和生物可解释的设计，DC-CCNN++为点云分析提供了一种有前景的传统深度学习方法替代方案。代码可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DC-CCNNpp-44E3 获取。

</details>


### [41] [Pix2Pix-Hybrid: Structure-Guided Conditional Synthesis of Hajj Crowd Images with Multi-Channel Conditioning and Weak Attribute Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14297)
*Amirah F. Alshammari,Bander A. Alzahrani,Nahed A. Alowidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对朝觐人群计数中标注数据稀缺和隐私问题，本文提出Pix2Pix-Hybrid (P2P-H)混合条件GAN，通过结构引导合成高质量朝觐人群图像进行数据增强，构建了10,000张合成数据集CrowdH，实验证明合成数据能有效降低五个计数模型的MAE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 朝觐场景中领域特定的标注图像稀缺，且大规模集会数据采集存在隐私问题，现有模型难以获得足够训练数据。

Method: 提出P2P-H混合条件GAN，基于Pix2Pix架构，采用八通道输入的U-Net生成器（编码边缘、灰度、人群密度、时间段等结构线索和上下文属性），集成两个多尺度PatchGAN判别器，结合对抗、感知和特征匹配目标训练，并采用自适应数据增强和稳定化策略。

Result: 在993张真实朝觐帧上训练，构建10,000张CrowdH合成数据集；相比Pix2Pix和StyleGAN2-ADA，P2P-H在保持结构的条件合成质量上更优；在CrowdH-Mix-469数据集上，合成数据使五个计数模型MAE均下降，CSRNet提升最显著。

Conclusion: P2P-H能有效合成结构保真的朝觐人群图像，作为数据增强手段可提升下游人群计数模型的性能，缓解了真实标注数据稀缺和隐私问题。

Original Abstract: Developing accurate crowd-counting models for Hajj pilgrimage scenes remains challenging because domain-specific annotated images are scarce and data collection during large gatherings raises privacy concerns. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Pix2Pix-Hybrid (P2P-H), a hybrid conditional GAN for structure-guided Hajj crowd-image synthesis and data augmentation. P2P-H builds on Pix2Pix and employs a U-Net generator conditioned on eight input channels that jointly encode structural cues (edges and grayscale) and contextual attributes (crowd density and time of day). To capture detailed textures in dense scenes, the framework integrates two multi-scale PatchGAN discriminators operating at different resolutions. The training procedure combines adversarial, perceptual, and feature-matching objectives with adaptive data augmentation and stabilization strategies. The model was trained on 993 real Hajj frames collected from 60 publicly available video sources, with conditioning attributes derived automatically to reduce manual labeling effort. Using this framework, we constructed CrowdH, a synthetic dataset of 10,000 high-resolution Hajj crowd images. Experimental results show that P2P-H improves structure-preserving conditional synthesis quality compared with Pix2Pix and StyleGAN2-ADA baselines and shows favorable transfer to other crowd datasets. To assess downstream utility, we further constructed CrowdH-Mix-469, an annotated mixed real-synthetic dataset comprising 384 real Hajj images and 85 selected synthetic images,and evaluated five crowd-counting models under real-only and real-plus-synthetic training. The selected synthetic data reduced MAE across all five models, with the strongest gain observed for CSRNet.

中文摘要翻译: 为朝觐场景开发准确的人群计数模型仍然具有挑战性，因为领域特定的标注图像稀缺，且大规模集会期间的数据采集会引发隐私问题。为解决这些限制，本文提出Pix2Pix-Hybrid (P2P-H)，一种用于结构引导的朝觐人群图像合成与数据增强的混合条件生成对抗网络。P2P-H基于Pix2Pix构建，采用以八个输入通道为条件的U-Net生成器，这些通道联合编码结构线索（边缘和灰度）与上下文属性（人群密度和时段）。为捕捉密集场景中的细节纹理，该框架集成了两个在不同分辨率下运行的多尺度PatchGAN判别器。训练过程结合了对抗、感知和特征匹配目标，并采用自适应数据增强和稳定化策略。该模型在从60个公开视频源收集的993帧真实朝觐图像上训练，条件属性自动推导以减少人工标注工作量。利用该框架，我们构建了CrowdH，一个包含10,000张高分辨率朝觐人群图像的合成数据集。实验结果表明，相比Pix2Pix和StyleGAN2-ADA基线，P2P-H在保持结构的条件合成质量上表现更优，且能较好地迁移至其他人群数据集。为评估下游效用，我们进一步构建了CrowdH-Mix-469，一个包含384张真实朝觐图像和85张精选合成图像的标注混合真实-合成数据集，并在仅真实训练和真实加合成训练两种设置下评估了五个计数模型。所选合成数据降低了所有五个模型的MAE，其中CSRNet的提升最为显著。

</details>


### [42] [What Drives Test-Time Adaptation for CLIP? A Controlled Empirical Study from an Update Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14299)
*Jiazhen Huang,Xiao Chen,Zhiming Liu,Yaru Sun,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对CLIP的测试时自适应方法（TTA4CLIP）进行了系统性对照研究，统一了现有方法的三种范式，构建了开源基准TTABC，并通过控制实验揭示了自适应增益主要来源于测试时证据和可靠代理而非繁重优化，且不存在普适最优的自适应范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 该领域经验性进展远超理论理解，缺乏对TTA4CLIP方法真正驱动因素、增益来源及在不同分布偏移下可靠性的系统认知。

Method: 首先将现有方法按测试时更新内容归纳为三种统一范式；然后构建开源基准TTABC，标准化评估协议并整合20多种代表性方法；最后通过受控实证分析聚焦三个关键方面：参数方法驱动因素、超越参数调优的证据利用、以及不同偏移下各范式的适用性。

Result: （1）参数方法的自适应增益主要由测试时证据和可靠代理驱动，而非繁重优化；（2）通过交叉样本或当前样本证据及轻量级原型更新即可实现有竞争力且高效的效果；（3）没有单一自适应范式在所有分布偏移下普遍最优，最佳范式取决于偏移类型。

Conclusion: TTA4CLIP不存在万能银弹，范式选择应因偏移类型而异；希望该基准和研究成果能为未来研究提供更清晰的理解基础。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP have become a standard backbone for open-vocabulary recognition, yet their zero-shot predictions remain vulnerable to distribution shifts encountered at deployment. Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) has recently been extended to CLIP as a lightweight solution, leading to a rapidly growing body of TTA4CLIP methods. However, empirical progress in this area has largely outpaced our understanding of what truly drives adaptation, where their gains originate, and under which shifts they remain reliable. In this paper, we take a step back from the pursuit of state-of-the-art accuracy and conduct a systematic controlled study of TTA4CLIP. We first organize existing methods into three unified paradigms according to what is updated at test time. We then introduce TTABC, an open-source TTA Benchmark for CLIP, which standardizes evaluation protocols and integrates more than 20 representative methods. Our controlled empirical analysis focuses on three key areas. First, we determine the driving factors in parameter-based methods, revealing that adaptation gains are primarily driven by test-time evidence and reliable proxies rather than heavy optimization. Second, we explore evidence utilization beyond heavy parameter tuning, showing that competitive and efficient performance can be achieved through cross- or current-sample evidence and lightweight prototype updates. Finally, we demonstrate that there is no silver bullet for TTA: no single adaptation paradigm is universally optimal, and the preferred paradigm depends on the nature of shift. We hope our benchmark and study provide a clearer understanding of the current TTA4CLIP landscape and establish a foundation for further research.

中文摘要翻译: 诸如CLIP之类的视觉-语言模型已成为开放词汇识别的标准骨干网络，但其零样本预测在部署时仍易受到分布偏移的影响。测试时自适应（TTA）最近被扩展到CLIP中，成为一种轻量级解决方案，催生了大量快速增长的TTA4CLIP方法。然而，该领域的经验性进展在很大程度上已超越了我们对其真正驱动因素、增益来源以及在何种偏移下保持可靠性的理解。本文从追求最先进准确率的步伐中退一步，对TTA4CLIP进行了系统性受控研究。我们首先根据测试时更新的内容将现有方法归纳为三种统一范式。随后，我们引入了TTABC——一个面向CLIP的开源TTA基准，该基准标准化了评估协议并整合了20多种代表性方法。我们的受控实证分析聚焦于三个关键方面。第一，我们确定了参数驱动方法中的关键因素，揭示出自适应增益主要来源于测试时证据和可靠代理，而非繁重优化。第二，我们探索了超越繁重参数调优的证据利用方式，表明通过交叉样本或当前样本证据以及轻量级原型更新即可实现有竞争力且高效的性能。最后，我们证明了TTA不存在万能银弹：没有单一自适应范式是普遍最优的，优选范式取决于偏移的性质。我们希望我们的基准和研究能为当前TTA4CLIP的景观提供更清晰的理解，并为后续研究奠定基础。

</details>


### [43] [Pano3D: Unified 3D Reconstruction and Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14307)
*Victor Barberteguy,Ahmet Iscen,Mathilde Caron,Alireza Fathi,Gül Varol,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的3D前馈重建框架，同时进行3D重建和全景分割，通过联合训练几何与语义损失实现互利提升，在多个数据集上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D前馈重建网络在无相机参数下取得了密集重建的成功，但缺乏强大的语义理解能力。

Method: 在现有3D重建模型基础上，增加基于集合的掩码解码器；使用几何和语义损失联合训练，先用几何信息初始化特征，再微调以联合捕获几何与语义；适用于在线注意力和全局注意力两种重建骨干网络。

Result: 在ScanNet、ScanNet200和ScanNet++数据集上达到3D全景分割的SOTA；消融实验表明联合训练可实现互利改进。

Conclusion: 联合训练的框架能有效赋予3D前馈重建网络全景分割能力，且几何与语义信息相互促进。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in 3D feedforward reconstruction neural networks have achieved remarkable success in dense reconstruction from images without any camera parameters. Yet, equipping these models with robust semantic understanding remains an open problem. Here we introduce an approach that performs 3D reconstruction and 3D panoptic segmentation in a unified framework. We build on existing 3D reconstruction models and augment them with a set-based mask decoder. The approach is jointly trained with a geometric and semantic loss, which are shown to be mutually beneficial. More precisely, the features are initialized from the geometric information and then finetuned to capture jointly geometry and semantics. We demonstrate the generality of our approach by successfully applying our framework both to online and all-to-all attention reconstruction backbones. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in 3D panoptic segmentation across ScanNet, ScanNet200, and ScanNet++ datasets. Ablation studies show that such joint training of a unified model equips 3D feedforward reconstruction neural networks with panoptic segmentation and yields mutually beneficial improvements.

中文摘要翻译: 近期3D前馈重建神经网络的进展已在无需任何相机参数的情况下，从图像中实现了令人瞩目的密集重建。然而，为这些模型配备稳健的语义理解仍然是一个开放问题。本文提出了一种在统一框架内同时进行3D重建和3D全景分割的方法。我们在现有3D重建模型的基础上，增加了一个基于集合的掩码解码器。该方法通过几何损失和语义损失进行联合训练，实验表明两者具有互惠互利的效果。具体而言，特征从几何信息初始化，然后进行微调以同时捕获几何和语义信息。我们通过将该框架成功应用于在线注意力与全局注意力两种重建骨干网络，证明了其通用性。我们的方法在ScanNet、ScanNet200和ScanNet++数据集上达到了3D全景分割的最优性能。消融研究表明，这种统一模型的联合训练能够赋予3D前馈重建神经网络全景分割能力，并带来互利的性能提升。

</details>


### [44] [CausalMotion: Structured Physical Reasoning as Keyframe and Trajectory Guidance for Training-Free Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14317)
*Sihan Zhuang,Xinyuan Chen,Tianfan Xue,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CausalMotion是一个无需训练的框架，通过视觉-语言模型将文本提示分解为因果一致的关键帧和物体轨迹，作为软约束注入预训练的视频扩散模型，从而在没有额外训练的情况下提升视频生成的物理合理性和时序连贯性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型主要隐式学习物理一致性，在处理长程交互场景时难以生成物理一致且因果合理的动态。作者希望将显式的物理推理注入视频生成过程，以弥补隐式学习的不足。

Method: 提出CausalMotion框架，核心思想是将推理与生成解耦。具体地：1) 使用视觉-语言模型将文本提示分解为因果一致的关键帧序列和物体中心的运动轨迹；2) 将这些中间表示对齐并整合为软约束；3) 在推理阶段将这些软约束注入预训练的视频扩散模型以指导生成过程，无需额外训练或监督。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多种场景下一致地提升了视频的物理合理性和时序连贯性，尤其在动态密集场景中效果显著，同时保持了较高的感知视频质量。

Conclusion: 通过将显式物理推理以结构化中间表示的形式注入视频扩散模型，可以有效提升生成视频的物理一致性和因果合理性，且无需额外训练开销。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion-based video generation have significantly improved visual quality and short-term temporal coherence. However, existing methods still struggle to produce videos with physically consistent and causally plausible dynamics, especially in scenarios involving long-horizon interactions. This limitation arises from the fact that video diffusion models primarily learn physical consistency implicitly, while vision-language models can directly model physical laws. Based on this idea, in this work, we propose \textbf{CausalMotion}, a training-free framework that injects explicit physical reasoning into video generation through structured intermediate representations. Our key idea is to decouple reasoning from generation by leveraging a vision-language model to decompose a text prompt into a sequence of causally consistent keyframes and object-centric motion trajectories. These representations are then aligned and integrated as soft constraints to guide a pretrained video diffusion model during inference. This design enables explicit modeling of object dynamics and causal transitions without requiring additional training or supervision. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently improves physical plausibility and temporal coherence, particularly in dynamics-intensive scenarios, while maintaining high perceptual video quality.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，基于扩散的视频生成技术在视觉质量和短期时序连贯性方面取得了显著进展。然而，现有方法在生成长程交互场景中具有物理一致性和因果合理动态的视频时仍面临困难。这一局限源于视频扩散模型主要隐式地学习物理一致性，而视觉-语言模型可以直接建模物理规律。基于这一思路，本文提出CausalMotion，一种无需训练的框架，通过结构化中间表示将显式物理推理注入视频生成过程。我们的核心思想是将推理与生成解耦，利用视觉-语言模型将文本提示分解为一系列因果一致的关键帧和以物体为中心的运动轨迹。随后将这些表示对齐并整合为软约束，在推理阶段指导预训练的视频扩散模型。这种设计无需额外训练或监督即可实现物体动态和因果转换的显式建模。大量实验表明，我们的方法一致地提升了视频的物理合理性和时序连贯性，尤其在动态密集场景中效果显著，同时保持了较高的感知视频质量。

</details>


### [45] [ForceForget: Reinforcement Concept Removal for Enhancing Safety in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14351)
*Dong Han,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习优化概念擦除奖励（CER）的方法，通过安全适配器（Safe Adapter）在交叉注意力层中调节文本嵌入，实现安全且精准的概念擦除，避免过度擦除对模型效用的负面影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法在移除不安全内容时过度擦除，导致模型对良性概念的生成能力下降，损害模型效用。亟需一种既能消除不安全内容又能保持模型在安全语义理解方面能力的方法。

Method: 通过强化学习优化概念擦除奖励（CER）；引入安全适配器（Safe Adapter）将部分文本嵌入投影到交叉注意力层，实现高效的概念调控，避免过度擦除。

Result: 在多个数据集上，所提方法在减少不安全内容生成方面优于现有SOTA方法，同时保持良性图像的高保真度；在鲁棒性上优于红队攻击工具；在图像到图像（I2I）场景中更有效；此外可扩展至擦除通用概念（如艺术风格和物体）。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习和安全适配器的概念擦除方法能有效平衡安全性与模型效用，在多个维度上超越现有方法，具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: With the advance of generative AI, the text-to-image (T2I) model has the ability to generate various contents. However, T2I models still can generate unsafe contents. To alleviate this issue, various concept erasing methods are proposed. However, existing methods tend to excessively erase unsafe concepts and suppress benign concepts contained in harmful prompts, which can negatively affect model utility. In this paper, we focus on eliminating unsafe content while maintaining model capability in safe semantic meaning interpretation by optimizing the concept erasing reward (CER) with reinforcement learning. To avoid overly content erasure, we introduce the Safe Adapter to project partial text embedding for efficient concept regulation in cross-attention layers. Extensive experiments conducted on different datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in alleviating unsafe content generation while preserving the high fidelity of benign images compared with existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) concept erasing methods. In terms of robustness, our method outperforms counterparts against red-teaming tools. Moreover, we showcase the proposed approach is more effective in emerging image-to-image (I2I) scenarios compared with others. Lastly, we extend our method to erase general concepts, such as artistic styles and objects. Disclaimer: This paper includes discussions of sexually explicit content that may be offensive to certain readers. All images used in this work are synthesized or from public datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 随着生成式人工智能的发展，文本到图像（T2I）模型能够生成多样化的内容。然而，T2I模型仍然可能生成不安全的内容。为缓解这一问题，研究者提出了多种概念擦除方法。然而，现有方法往往过度擦除不安全概念，并抑制有害提示中包含的良性概念，从而对模型效用产生负面影响。本文聚焦于消除不安全内容，同时保持模型在安全语义理解方面的能力，通过强化学习优化概念擦除奖励（CER）来实现。为避免过度内容擦除，我们引入了安全适配器（Safe Adapter），将部分文本嵌入投影到交叉注意力层中，以实现高效的概念调控。在多个数据集上进行的大量实验表明，与现有最先进（SOTA）的概念擦除方法相比，所提方法在减少不安全内容生成方面效果显著，同时保持了良性图像的高保真度。在鲁棒性方面，我们的方法优于红队攻击工具的对应方法。此外，我们展示了所提方法在新兴的图像到图像（I2I）场景中比其他方法更有效。最后，我们将方法扩展到擦除通用概念，如艺术风格和物体。免责声明：本文包含对可能引起某些读者不适的性露骨内容的讨论。本工作中使用的所有图像均为合成图像或来自公开数据集。

</details>


### [46] [FLaRA: Predicting Future Latent Representations for Accident Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14380)
*Lorenzo Caselli,Tomaso Trinci,Tommaso Bianconcini,Simone Magistri,Leonardo Taccari,Francesco Sambo,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLaRA模型，通过预测未来潜在表征而非仅依赖历史观测来提前预测行车记录仪视频中的交通事故，在多个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有交通事故预测方法通常直接将视觉上下文映射到碰撞概率，没有显式建模驾驶场景的未来演化过程，导致预测能力受限。

Method: 基于V-JEPA2架构，利用预测器网络在观测到的上下文帧基础上预测场景的未来潜在特征，再由分类器对这些预测的未来表征进行分类；同时引入联合训练目标，同时优化辅助特征级重建损失和交叉熵分类损失。

Result: 在Nexar数据集上的广泛评估以及在DAD、DADA-2000和DoTA基准上的跨域验证表明，该方法达到了最先进性能，同时保持了现实可行的早期预警能力。

Conclusion: 通过预测未来潜在表征而非仅依赖历史观测，可以有效提升交通事故预警的准确性和及时性，且具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Anticipating traffic accidents from dashcam videos is a critical challenge in intelligent transportation systems. Existing methods typically map visual context directly to a collision probability without explicitly modeling the future evolution of the driving scene. In this paper we propose FLaRA (Predicting Future Latent Representations for Accident Anticipation), a novel predictive architecture that shifts this paradigm by forecasting future latent representations for accident anticipation. Building upon the Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA2), our model conditions a predictor network on observed context frames to predict the forthcoming latent features of the scene. A classifier then operates on these predicted future representations rather than only on past observations. To ensure these forecasts remain grounded in realistic future dynamics, we introduce a joint training objective that simultaneously optimizes an auxiliary feature-level reconstruction loss and a cross-entropy classification loss. Extensive evaluations on the Nexar dataset, alongside cross-domain validations on the DAD, DADA-2000, and DoTA benchmarks, demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining realistic early warning capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 从行车记录仪视频中提前预测交通事故是智能交通系统中的一个关键挑战。现有方法通常直接将视觉上下文映射到碰撞概率，而无需显式建模驾驶场景的未来演化过程。本文提出FLaRA（预测未来潜在表征用于事故预警），这是一种新颖的预测架构，通过预测未来潜在表征来转变这一范式，从而实现事故预警。基于视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA2），我们的模型利用预测器网络在观测到的上下文帧上预测场景即将到来的潜在特征。随后，一个分类器作用于这些预测的未来表征，而不仅仅依赖于过去的观测。为了确保这些预测保持对现实未来动态的约束，我们引入了一个联合训练目标，同时优化辅助特征级重建损失和交叉熵分类损失。在Nexar数据集上的广泛评估，以及在DAD、DADA-2000和DoTA基准上的跨域验证表明，我们的方法在保持现实早期预警能力的同时，达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [47] [IndustryBench-MIPU: Benchmarking Multi-Image Attribute Value Extraction for Industrial Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14383)
*Haonan Qi,Jin Cao,Yongqi Zhang,Xintong Wang,Weidong Tang,Bin Chen,Chengfu Huo,Haojun Pan,Hengyu You,Jing Li,Yingde Wang,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 工业产品（如阀门、断路器）的采购、兼容性和安全规格说明分散在规格表、铭牌、技术图纸等多种产品图像中。本文构建了首个大规模多图像工业产品理解基准IndustryBench-MIPU，包含4,559种产品、27,652张图像和103,703条标注，评估了9种多模态大模型。结果表明模型在单图像设置下精度达到86-94%，但最佳模型在多图像产品级属性提取中仅恢复49.9%的属性；从单图像到多图像提取，召回率下降15-34个百分点。多图像完整性是核心瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业产品（如阀门、断路器）的技术规格说明分散在规格表、铭牌、技术图纸等多种异构产品图像中，但多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能否可靠地从这些多图像中恢复技术规格尚未被探索。

Method: 构建了IndustryBench-MIPU基准，包含4,559种产品、27,652张图像和103,703条标注，覆盖18个工业类别。数据集通过多模型共识和三阶段质量保证构建。基准任务为结构化属性提取，即在产品级别从多张图像中恢复属性-值对，同时探测文本识别、视觉推理、领域知识解码和跨图像证据整合等能力。

Result: 评估9种MLLMs发现：单图像设置下模型精度达86-94%，但最佳模型在产品级多图像设置中仅恢复49.9%的属性；从单图像到多图像提取，召回率下降15-34个百分点。多图像完整性而非单图像精度是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在处理工业产品多图像技术规格提取时，单图像识别精度尚可，但跨图像完整性严重不足，多图像属性完整恢复是当前的核心瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Industrial products such as valves and circuit breakers are defined by dense technical specifications that govern procurement, compatibility, and safety across supply chains. These specifications are scattered across multiple heterogeneous product images, including specification tables, nameplates, and technical drawings, yet whether Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can reliably recover them remains underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce IndustryBench-MIPU, the first large-scale benchmark for multi-image industrial product understanding, built around structured attribute extraction -- recovering property-value pairs from product images. This task jointly probes text recognition on specification tables and nameplates, visual reasoning over technical drawings, domain knowledge to decode industrial terminology, and cross-image evidence integration to assemble scattered specifications. Concretely, the benchmark comprises 4,559 products across 27,652 images with 103,703 annotations spanning 18 industrial categories, constructed through multi-model consensus and three-tier quality assurance. Evaluating nine MLLMs under both single-image and product-level multi-image settings reveals a stark completeness gap: models achieve high precision (86--94%) but the best recovers only 49.9% of product-level attributes; moving from single-image to multi-image extraction costs 15--34 percentage points of recall. Multi-image completeness, not single-image accuracy, is the core bottleneck. Dataset and code are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 阀门和断路器等工业产品由密集的技术规格说明定义，这些规格指导着供应链中的采购、兼容性和安全性。这些规格说明分散在多种异构产品图像中，包括规格表、铭牌和技术图纸，然而多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能否可靠地恢复它们尚未被充分探索。为填补这一空白，本文提出了IndustryBench-MIPU，首个面向多图像工业产品理解的大规模基准，其围绕结构化属性提取构建——从产品图像中恢复属性-值对。该任务联合探测了规格表和铭牌上的文本识别、技术图纸上的视觉推理、解读工业术语的领域知识，以及整合分散规格的跨图像证据融合。具体而言，该基准包含4,559种产品、27,652张图像和103,703条标注，覆盖18个工业类别，通过多模型共识和三阶段质量保证构建。在单图像和产品级多图像两种设置下评估九种MLLMs，揭示了一个显著完整性差距：模型达到了高精度（86-94%），但最佳模型仅恢复了49.9%的产品级属性；从单图像提取转向多图像提取导致了15-34个百分点的召回率下降。多图像完整性，而非单图像精度，是核心瓶颈。数据集和代码已公开。

</details>


### [48] [MooMIns -- Monocular 3D Reconstruction and Object Pose Estimation from Multiple Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14389)
*Robert Langendörfer,Markus Hillemann,Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MooMIns方法，利用单张单目图像中多个物体实例的隐式多视图几何，通过反向高斯泼溅（将传统多相机单场景渲染变为单相机多实例渲染）实现同时的3D重建和6D姿态估计，在未见物体和实际分拣场景中表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从单目单张图像同时进行3D重建和6D姿态估计是病态问题，但工业分拣场景中多个物体实例随机堆放于料箱，同一图像中隐式包含了同一物体的多视角信息，可被利用来解决这一病态问题。

Method: 提出MooMIns方法，反向利用高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）：将原始的多相机→单场景渲染反转为单相机→多实例渲染。初始化使用SAM3实例分割掩码和改进的SfM流程，实现基于几何的真实重建，避免单目深度估计的数据先验幻觉问题。

Result: 在合成和真实分拣场景上验证，MooMIns能准确重建未见过的物体，并对每个实例进行可靠的6D姿态估计。

Conclusion: 利用工业分拣场景中单张图像隐含的多视图几何信息，通过反向高斯泼溅方法，可以有效地从单目图像实现基于几何的3D重建和6D姿态估计，无需依赖训练数据先验。

Original Abstract: Simultaneous 3D reconstruction and 6D object pose estimation from a single monocular image is an inherently ill-posed problem. In industrial settings, however, multiple instances of an object are often randomly arranged in bins, implicitly providing several views of the same object within a single image. We show that this implicit multi-view geometry can be exploited to simultaneously reconstruct the object in 3D and estimate the 6D pose of each visible object instance. We present MooMIns, a new Gaussian-splatting-based approach that inverts the original Gaussian splatting formulation: instead of rendering a single scene from multiple cameras, we render multiple object instances from a single camera. Our method is initialized with SAM3 instance segmentation masks and a modified Structure from Motion (SfM) pipeline. In contrast to learned monocular depth estimation, we perform true geometry-based reconstruction from image evidence, avoiding hallucinations caused by training data priors. We evaluate MooMIns on synthetic and real bin-picking scenarios, and demonstrate accurate reconstruction of previously unseen objects as well as reliable pose estimation of individual instance

中文摘要翻译: 从单张单目图像同时进行3D重建和6D物体姿态估计本质上是一个病态问题。然而，在工业场景中，物体的多个实例通常随机堆放在料箱中，隐式地在单张图像内提供了同一物体的多个视角。本文证明，这种隐式多视图几何可以被利用来同时重建物体的3D结构并估计每个可见物体实例的6D姿态。我们提出了MooMIns，一种基于高斯泼溅的新方法，它反转了原始高斯泼溅的公式：不是从多个相机渲染单个场景，而是从单个相机渲染多个物体实例。我们的方法使用SAM3实例分割掩码和改进的运动恢复结构（SfM）流程进行初始化。与学习的单目深度估计方法不同，我们基于图像证据执行真正的基于几何的重建，避免了训练数据先验导致的幻觉。我们在合成和真实的分拣场景上评估了MooMIns，展示了其对先前未见物体的准确重建以及对单个实例的可靠姿态估计。

</details>


### [49] [Value-order Decomposition for Generalist Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14475)
*Miaoyun Zhao,Jing Chen,Miaoni Zhao,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出值序分解（VOD）方法，通过解耦和抑制类别、缺陷类型和领域特定信息，实现工业异常检测中跨类别、缺陷类型和数据域的三重泛化，仅使用正常和合成异常样本即可泛化到未见过的真实缺陷类型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有通用异常检测方法将特征投影到残差空间仅增强了正常特征的跨域重叠，但异常特征仍与物体类别、缺陷类型和数据域高度绑定，导致对齐和泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出值序分解（Value-order Decomposition, VOD），通过解耦并抑制物体类别、缺陷类型和数据域特定信息，促进正常样本和异常样本各自对齐同时保持两者可分性；利用同一物体内真实缺陷与合成缺陷的强对齐，仅用正常和合成异常参考进行异常检测。

Result: 在多种工业与医学基准数据集上，结合简易的cut-and-paste异常仿真策略，方法在跨类别、跨缺陷类型和跨数据域三个维度均展现出强泛化性能。

Conclusion: VOD作为一种简单有效的技术，能够同时弥合三类泛化鸿沟，实现鲁棒的跨域异常检测泛化。

Original Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection suffers from limited data, making cross-domain generalization particularly challenging. Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to train a unified model on a source domain that can effectively detect anomalies in unseen target domains. In the initial semantic feature space, strong entanglement between anomalies and object categories or defect types hinders effective generalization across domains. Recent works address this issue by projecting features into a residual space; however, such methods primarily increase cross-domain overlap for normal features, while anomalous features remain specific to object categories, defect types and data domains, leading to poor alignment and generalization. To address this limitation, we propose Value-order Decomposition (VOD), a simple yet effective technique that bridges \textbf{three types of generalization gaps} across object categories, defect types (including real and synthetic defects), and data domains. VOD disentangles and suppresses object-category-, defect-type-, and domain-specific information, promoting alignment within normal and abnormal samples while preserving their separability, thereby enabling robust generalization across the three gaps. Leveraging the strong alignment between real and synthetic defects within the same object, we perform anomaly detection using only normal and synthetic-abnormal reference, and effectively generalize to unseen real defect types. Experiments on diverse industrial and medical benchmarks demonstrate that our method, using a simple cut-and-paste anomaly simulation strategy, achieves strong generalization across the three gaps.

中文摘要翻译: 工业异常检测受限于数据稀缺，使得跨域泛化尤为困难。通用异常检测（GAD）旨在训练一个统一模型，使其能够在源域上有效检测未见过的目标域中的异常。在初始语义特征空间中，异常与物体类别或缺陷类型之间的强耦合阻碍了跨域有效泛化。近期工作通过将特征投影到残差空间来解决此问题，然而这类方法主要增强了正常特征的跨域重叠，异常特征仍然与物体类别、缺陷类型和数据领域高度绑定，导致对齐和泛化能力不足。为克服这一局限，我们提出值序分解（Value-order Decomposition, VOD），这是一种简单而有效的技术，能够弥合跨物体类别、缺陷类型（包括真实缺陷和合成缺陷）以及数据领域的\textbf{三类泛化鸿沟}。VOD通过解耦并抑制物体类别、缺陷类型和领域特定信息，促进正常样本和异常样本各自内部对齐，同时保持二者之间的可分性，从而实现对三类泛化鸿沟的鲁棒泛化。利用同一物体内部真实缺陷与合成缺陷之间的强对齐性，我们仅使用正常样本和合成异常参考即可进行异常检测，并能有效泛化至未见过的真实缺陷类型。在多种工业和医学基准上的实验证明，我们的方法采用简单的cut-and-paste异常仿真策略，在三类泛化鸿沟上均实现了强大的泛化性能。

</details>


### [50] [Scratched Lenses, Shifted Depth: Passive Camera-Side Optical Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14504)
*Qinlin He,Zeming Zhuang,Yongji Wu,Lan Zhang,Xiaoyong,Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为SLASH的物理世界对抗攻击方法，通过在相机镜头或保护罩上制造微小划痕，利用划痕与强光源和镜面反射的相互作用产生结构化条纹伪影，从而扭曲深度估计和3D目标检测等几何推理任务。该攻击具有持久性和场景触发选择性，在固定划痕约束下，单目深度估计的相对误差可达32%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物理对抗攻击主要研究场景操纵（如对抗补丁）或相机侧攻击（如贴纸），但忽略了物理缺陷与场景光照和光学系统的相互作用。作者识别出一种新型威胁：被动镜头侧损伤，它持久存在但由场景条件触发，在特定视觉条件下产生光学伪影以偏置几何推理。

Method: 在光学空间中构建攻击模型，将划痕模式建模为触发条件依赖的光学信道；优化固定划痕配置使其在不同视角条件下均有效；在数字和真实场景中评估对单目深度估计和单目3D目标检测的影响。

Result: 固定划痕约束下，单目深度估计的方向性深度偏移相对误差可达32%；对单目3D目标检测产生一致影响；真实物理实验验证了攻击可迁移到真实相机录制，诱导超出模型自然预测基线的深度偏移。

Conclusion: 良性外观的硬件缺陷可作为潜在的、场景触发的对抗机制，挑战了现有关于物理鲁棒性的假设，启发了面向安全视觉系统的防御研究。

Original Abstract: Physical adversarial attacks on vision systems are typically studied through scene manipulation, such as adversarial patches or projections, where the adversary controls what the camera observes. Camera-side attacks using stickers or auxiliary optics have also been explored, but they treat attacks as image-space perturbations from designed patterns. This misses how physical imperfections interact with scene-dependent lighting and optics. We identify a threat: passive lens-side damage that is persistent yet trigger-conditioned, producing optical artifacts that bias geometric inference under particular visual conditions.
  We instantiate this threat through Scratch-induced Lens Adversarial Streak Hijacking SLASH, a physical-world attack caused by small scratches on a camera lens or protective cover. Scratches interact with bright light sources and specular reflections to create structured streak artifacts that distort depth cues. Since the perturbation is fixed in the optical path but triggered by the scene, it is both persistent and selective. We formulate the attack in optical space, model the scratch pattern as a trigger-conditioned optical channel, and optimize one fixed configuration across diverse viewing conditions. We evaluate SLASH on monocular depth estimation and monocular 3D object detection in digital and real-world settings. Under the fixed-scratch constraint, directional depth shifts reach up to 32% relative error for monocular depth estimation, with consistent effects on monocular 3D object detection. Physical experiments confirm transfer to real camera recordings, inducing depth shifts above the model's natural prediction baseline. These findings reveal an attack surface where benign-looking hardware imperfections act as latent, scene-triggered adversarial mechanisms, challenging assumptions about physical robustness and motivating defenses for secure vision systems.

中文摘要翻译: 针对视觉系统的物理对抗攻击通常通过场景操纵来研究，例如对抗补丁或投影，其中攻击者控制相机观察到的内容。也有研究探索使用贴纸或辅助光学元件的相机侧攻击，但这些方法将攻击视为来自设计图案的图像空间扰动，忽略了物理缺陷如何与场景相关的光照和光学系统相互作用。我们识别出一种威胁：被动的镜头侧损伤，它持久存在但由条件触发，在特定视觉条件下产生光学伪影，从而偏置几何推理。我们通过划痕诱导的镜头对抗性条纹劫持（SLASH）来实例化这一威胁，这是一种由相机镜头或保护罩上的微小划痕引起的物理世界攻击。划痕与强光源和镜面反射相互作用，产生结构化条纹伪影，扭曲深度线索。由于扰动固定在光路中但由场景触发，因此既具有持久性又具有选择性。我们在光学空间中构建攻击形式，将划痕模式建模为触发条件依赖的光学信道，并优化一个固定的配置以适应不同的视角条件。我们在数字和真实场景中评估SLASH对单目深度估计和单目3D目标检测的影响。在固定划痕约束下，单目深度估计的方向性深度偏移相对误差可达32%，对单目3D目标检测产生一致影响。真实物理实验验证了攻击可迁移到真实相机录制，诱导超出模型自然预测基线的深度偏移。这些发现揭示了一种攻击面：良性外观的硬件缺陷可作为潜在的、场景触发的对抗机制，挑战了关于物理鲁棒性的假设，并启发了面向安全视觉系统的防御研究。

</details>


### [51] [A Lightweight Fiducial-Based Pipeline for 3D Hyperspectral Mapping of ex-vivo Lumpectomy Specimens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14534)
*Anna Bicchi,Alberto Rota,Leonardo Passoni,Nicola Ancellotti,Andrea Peroni,Lorenzo Vinco,Dario Polli,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种全自动、无标定的流水线，将2D高光谱成像与3D组织表面重建对齐，用于保乳手术中的切缘评估，在离体标本上实现了亚毫米级3D配准误差和快速处理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高光谱成像（HSI）有望用于保乳手术（BCS）中切缘的术中评估，但其临床转化需要将固有的2D光谱信息与切除组织的3D表面对齐，以便精确定位可疑区域进行靶向处理。

Method: 使用消费级RGB图像通过深度学习SfM重建3D几何，通过包含ArUco标记约束的定制光束法平差稳定度量参考系；利用HSI和RGB中均可见的标记点计算平面单应性，再通过正交投影深度图查找恢复3D坐标，无需估计HSI相机位姿。

Result: 在两个离体肿物切除标本上，中位3D配准误差低于1 mm，2D重投影误差低于0.02 mm，单标本处理时间在加速硬件上低于4分钟。

Conclusion: 该结果支持将HSI引导的空间定位整合到保乳手术的术中切缘评估流程中是可行的。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral Imaging (HSI) is a promising modality for intraoperative assessment of resection margins in Breast-Conserving Surgery (BCS), but its clinical translation requires aligning the inherently 2D spectral information onto the 3D shape of the excised tissue so that suspicious regions can be precisely localized for targeted follow-up. We present a fully automated, calibration-free pipeline that produces a 3D hyperspectral point cloud of an ex-vivo lumpectomy specimen from a set of consumer-camera RGB images and a single top-down HSI acquisition. The 3D geometry is reconstructed with a deep-learning Structure-from-Motion backbone, stabilized in a metric reference frame by a custom bundle adjustment that enforces consistency on the corners of four ArUco markers placed around the specimen. The HSI cube is then registered to the reconstruction without recovering the HSI camera pose: the markers, visible in both modalities, define 16 corner correspondences that drive a planar homography, and 3D coordinates are recovered by lookup on an orthographically rendered depth map. Evaluated on two ex-vivo lumpectomy specimens, the pipeline achieves a median 3D registration error below 1~mm and a 2D reprojection error below 0.02 mm, with a total per-specimen processing time under 4 minutes on accelerated hardware. These results support the feasibility of integrating HSI-guided spatial localization into intraoperative margin assessment workflows for breast-conserving surgery.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱成像(HSI)是一种有望用于保乳手术(BCS)中切缘术中评估的成像模态，但其临床转化需要将固有的二维光谱信息与切除组织的三维表面对齐，以便精确定位可疑区域进行靶向处理。我们提出了一种全自动、无需标定的流水线，该流水线利用一组消费级相机RGB图像和单次俯拍HSI采集，生成离体肿物切除标本的三维高光谱点云。三维几何通过深度学习运动恢复结构(SfM)骨干网络重建，并通过自定义的光束法平差在度量参考系中稳定下来，该平差在标本周围放置的四个ArUco标记的角点上施加一致性约束。随后，无需恢复HSI相机位姿即可将HSI立方体配准到重建结果上：两种模态中均可见的标记提供了16个角点对应关系，驱动平面单应性变换，并通过在正交投影深度图上查表恢复三维坐标。在两个离体肿物切除标本上的评估结果表明，该流水线的中位三维配准误差低于1 mm，二维重投影误差低于0.02 mm，在加速硬件上每个标本的总处理时间低于4分钟。这些结果支持将HSI引导的空间定位整合到保乳手术的术中切缘评估流程中的可行性。

</details>


### [52] [Rethinking Global Average Pooling: Your Classifier Is Secretly a Multi-Instance Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14555)
*Aray Karjauv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 标准图像分类器（GAP+线性分类头）天然具有多实例学习（MIL）结构，即使使用单标签训练，也能在空间维度上保留类别证据。本文利用这一特性将图像级logits分解为预测网格，实现后验空间诊断，揭示分类器失败源于均值聚合的已知局限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代图像分类器采用GAP加线性分类头的结构，导致图像级logits等于特征网格上逐点分类logits的平均值。这种线性结构意味着即使图像级预测错误，空间上的类别证据仍然可恢复。作者希望利用这一特性来提取被GAP掩盖的空间类别证据，并提供分类器失败的解释。

Method: 将标准分类器重新解释为多实例学习（MIL）框架：图像被视为一个包含多个空间实例的包（bag）。利用GAP与线性分类头的线性关系，将图像级logits分解回预测网格，从而从训练好的分类器中提取空间类别证据，实现后验诊断。

Result: 系统性评估表明，现成的（off-the-shelf）模型能够一致地在前景区域内恢复真实类别。分类器的常见失败反映了均值聚合的已知局限性。

Conclusion: 标准图像分类器内部的线性结构使其天然具有MIL属性，可以通过logits分解来揭示被GAP掩盖的空间证据；分类器失败的根本原因可归结为均值聚合的固有缺陷。

Original Abstract: Modern image classifiers widely adopt global average pooling (GAP) followed by a linear classification head. This linearity ensures that the image-level logits equal the average of logits obtained by applying the classification head pointwise to the feature grid prior to GAP. Consequently, standard classifiers may inherently retain spatial class evidence that remains recoverable even when the image-level prediction is incorrect. This structure naturally suggests a multiple-instance learning (MIL) interpretation, where an image is viewed as a bag of spatial instances. Within this formulation, we demonstrate that standard classifiers trained with a single label per image can still learn the intended classification task in multi-object scenes. We further exploit this property to decompose image-level logits into a prediction grid, providing a post-hoc diagnostic to extract spatial class evidence that GAP otherwise obscures. Our systematic evaluation reveals that off-the-shelf models consistently recover the ground-truth class within foreground regions. The MIL interpretation further suggests that common classifier failures reflect known limitations of mean aggregation.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像分类器广泛采用全局平均池化（GAP）后接线性分类头的结构。这种线性特性确保了图像级logits等价于将分类头逐点应用于GAP之前的特征网格所得logits的平均值。因此，即使在图像级预测错误的情况下，标准分类器也可能在本质上保留空间类别证据，这些证据仍然可以被恢复。这种结构自然地引出了一种多实例学习（MIL）的解释——将图像视为一个包含多个空间实例的包（bag）。在这一框架下，我们证明了使用单标签训练的标注分类器仍然可以在多目标场景中学习到预期的分类任务。我们进一步利用这一特性将图像级logits分解为预测网格，提供了一种后验诊断方法来提取被GAP掩盖的空间类别证据。我们的系统性评估表明，现成（off-the-shelf）的模型能够一致地在前景区域内恢复真实类别。MIL的解释进一步揭示了常见分类器失败反映了均值聚合的已知局限性。

</details>


### [53] [Visual Quality Score Assessment of Large White Goods in Remanufacture with Multi-View Deformable-DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14556)
*Paul Koch,Vivek Chavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Deformable-DETR的多视图框架，通过自监督预训练和线性投影解释性，实现大型白色家电再制造的自动化视觉质量评分，减少人工标注依赖。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型白色家电再制造中，视觉质量评估依赖人工瓶颈，传统检测方法需要大量标注且难以处理高分辨率多视图数据中的微小缺陷。

Method: 基于Deformable-DETR的多视图框架，聚合冗余视图信息提取细粒度特征；采用自监督预训练+专家标注分数的有监督微调；利用冻结特征图上的线性投影定位感兴趣区域以解释模型决策。

Result: 在工业多视图数据集上，该方法能提供精准的质量评估，同时减少人工标注和逐部件定制需求。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了可扩展且透明的再制造产线质量检测，有望突破人工瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Remanufacturing large white goods is essential for a circular economy, yet visual quality assessment remains a manual bottleneck for training and pricing. Conventional detection methods require extensive annotation and struggle with small defects in high-resolution multi-view data. We present a multi-view framework based on Deformable-DETR for automated quality scoring that aggregates information across redundant views to extract fine-grained features. To enhance robustness with limited labels, we employ self-supervised pretraining followed by supervised fine-tuning on expert-annotated scores. Additionally, a linear projection over frozen feature maps identifies regions of interest to explain model decisions. Evaluated on an industrial multi-view dataset, our approach delivers precise quality assessments while reducing reliance on manual annotation and per-part customization, enabling scalable and transparent inspection for remanufacturing lines.

中文摘要翻译: 大型白色家电的再制造对于循环经济至关重要，然而视觉质量评估仍是训练和定价中的人工瓶颈。传统检测方法需要大量标注，且难以处理高分辨率多视图数据中的微小缺陷。我们提出了一种基于Deformable-DETR的多视图框架，用于自动质量评分，该框架通过聚合冗余视图中的信息来提取细粒度特征。为增强有限标签下的鲁棒性，我们采用自监督预训练，随后在专家标注的分数上进行有监督微调。此外，在冻结的特征图上进行线性投影，以定位感兴趣区域从而解释模型决策。在工业多视图数据集上的评估表明，我们的方法能够提供精准的质量评估，同时减少对人工标注和逐部件定制的依赖，从而实现再制造产线中可扩展且透明的检测。

</details>


### [54] [NEST3D: A High-Resolution Multimodal Dataset of Sociable Weaver Tree Nests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14562)
*Constanza A. Molina Catricheo,Simon Boeder,Ting-Jia Guo,Giacomo May,Clément Berthelot,Devis Tuia,Friedrich Fedor Reinhard,Fabio Remondino,Benjamin Risse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个1.4TB的多模态无人机数据集（包含104棵巢树、近28万张RGB图像、超111万张多光谱图像、约7.81亿个3D点及专家标注），用于对群居织布鸟巢穴进行精细3D结构分析，并基准测试了KPConv、RandLA-Net和Point Transformer V3三种语义分割方法，其中PT-v3在测试集上达到86.35% mIoU。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有群居织布鸟巢穴研究缺乏精细的3D结构数据，且由于巢穴几何形状不规则并与复杂宿主植被交织，获取可用且精确的3D数据极具挑战性。

Method: 构建了包含RGB图像、多光谱图像、3D点云和语义分割标注的多模态无人机数据集；使用KPConv、RandLA-Net和Point Transformer V3三种方法进行语义分割基准测试。

Result: Point Transformer V3在测试集上达到86.35%的mIoU，表现优于其他方法；基于Transformer和逐点方法表现强劲，但基于卷积的方法（如KPConv）面临架构相关的挑战。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过结合光谱、空间和结构信息，推动了3D重建、分割和分类算法的发展，可用于巢穴体积估算到物种保护等生态应用，同时作为一个在极端类别不平衡下暴露架构依赖性能的高要求基准。

Original Abstract: Sociable weaver nests function as complex ecological structures offering thermoregulatory microhabitats and sustaining diverse species; however, datasets used in prior studies lack fine-grained 3D structural detail. Producing usable and accurate 3D weaver nest data is challenging due to their irregular geometry and integration with complex host vegetation. We bridge this gap with an open-access, 1.4 TB multimodal drone dataset of 104 nest-bearing trees, comprising 27,945 RGB images, 111,780 multispectral images, approximately 781 million 3D points, and expert-annotated semantic segmentation labels. We benchmark semantic segmentation using KPConv, RandLA-Net, and Point Transformer V3, with PT-v3 achieving an mIoU of 86.35% on the test set. While the results demonstrate strong performance for transformer-based and point-wise methods, they also highlight architecture-dependent challenges, particularly for convolution-based approaches such as KPConv. By uniquely combining spectral, spatial, and structural information, the presented dataset advances 3D reconstruction, segmentation, and classification algorithms, enabling ecological applications from nest volume estimation to species conservation, and serves as a demanding benchmark that exposes architecture-dependent performance under extreme class imbalance.

中文摘要翻译: 群居织布鸟巢穴作为复杂的生态结构，提供热调节微栖息地并维持多种物种生存；然而，先前研究使用的数据集缺乏精细的3D结构细节。由于其不规则几何形状以及与复杂宿主植被的融合，生成可用且精确的织布鸟巢穴3D数据具有挑战性。我们通过一个开放获取的、1.4TB的多模态无人机数据集（包含104棵巢树，共计27,945张RGB图像、111,780张多光谱图像、约7.81亿个3D点以及专家标注的语义分割标签）弥补了这一空白。我们使用KPConv、RandLA-Net和Point Transformer V3对语义分割进行基准测试，其中PT-v3在测试集上达到了86.35%的平均交并比（mIoU）。虽然结果展示了基于Transformer和逐点方法的强劲性能，但也凸显了架构相关的挑战，尤其是对于KPConv等基于卷积的方法。通过独特地结合光谱、空间和结构信息，本数据集推动了3D重建、分割和分类算法的发展，支持从巢穴体积估算到物种保护等生态应用，并作为一个高要求的基准，揭示了极端类别不平衡下依赖于架构的性能表现。

</details>


### [55] [A Qualitative Review of GenAI-Based Methods for Data Generation and Augmentation in Industrial Computer Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14578)
*Paul Koch,Paul Hofmann,Ferdinand Waßelewsky,Adem Karakurt,Andre Sérs,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了工业计算机视觉应用中数据不足导致的“先有鸡还是先有蛋”困境，回顾了基于GenAI的数据生成与增强方法在初始主动数据爬坡阶段的应用潜力，并指出了源域与目标域之间的领域不匹配问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业计算机视觉应用缺乏现成的高质量数据库，而主动学习方法在数据爬坡过程中可能导致用户信任度下降，形成“数据库与应用均无法发展”的困境。本文旨在通过回顾基于GenAI的数据生成与增强方法，寻求突破这一困境的途径。

Method: 综述了基于生成式AI（GenAI）的数据生成与增强方法的最新进展，并在一项工业计算机视觉分类用例上评估了这些方法的适应性。

Result: GenAI方法在自动数据爬坡方面展现出潜力，但存在源域（训练环境）与目标域（工业用例）之间的领域不匹配问题，具体表现在自然语言定义的上下文和物体特征方面。

Conclusion: GenAI生成的数据与真实工业场景之间存在领域不匹配问题，需要进一步研究以弥合这一差距，从而有效推动工业计算机视觉应用中的数据爬坡与信任建立。

Original Abstract: AI-driven computer vision applications require a profound database to ensure predictable behaviors and performance. Such predictable behaviors are especially important for industrial applications in gaining trust from users. However, such a database is not readily available in industrial applications, and its acquisition is not trivial either. Active learning methods can be applied to ramp up data within a project deployment to iteratively increase the database, and thus the application predictability. Unfortunately, we observe that this often leads to a loss of user trust in the application, which is difficult to regain once lost. This leads to a "chicken-and-egg" dilemma in which neither the database nor the application is developed. In this work, we review state-of-the-art methods and approaches to further boost the database the initial active data ramp-up phase. Here, we focus on recent advancements in GenAI-based data generation and augmentation methods and review their adaptability on an industrial computer vision classification use case. Although we observe a potential for automatic data ramp-up, we also see a domain miss match in between the source (training environment) and target (industrial use-case) - regarding context defined in natural language and object characteristics.

中文摘要翻译: 基于人工智能的计算机视觉应用需要强大的数据库来确保可预测的行为和性能。这种可预测性对于工业应用获取用户信任尤为重要。然而，工业应用中并不容易获得这样的数据库，其获取也并非易事。主动学习方法可以在项目部署内逐步积累数据，迭代地扩充数据库，从而提升应用的可预测性。不幸的是，我们观察到这常常导致用户对应用失去信任，且一旦失去便难以挽回。这形成了一个“先有鸡还是先有蛋”的困境，导致数据库和应用均无法发展。在这项工作中，我们回顾了用于在初始主动数据爬坡阶段进一步扩充数据库的最先进方法与途径。我们重点关注近年来基于生成式AI（GenAI）的数据生成与增强方法的最新进展，并在一个工业计算机视觉分类用例上评估了它们的适应性。尽管我们观察到自动数据爬坡的潜力，但也发现了源域（训练环境）与目标域（工业用例）之间在自然语言定义的上下文和物体特征方面的领域不匹配问题。

</details>


### [56] [S$^2$COPE: Self-Supervised Concept Discovery via Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14586)
*Shilong Xiang,Zirui Zhang,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自监督概念发现框架（SCoPE），通过偏好学习让视觉大语言模型自主发现结构化概念，无需标签即可实现可解释性与大规模扩展性的统一。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有表征学习方法存在根本性权衡：自监督方法可扩展至大数据集但特征不透明，可解释模型则受限于密集人工标注。作者希望在不依赖人类标注的情况下，同时实现模型的可解释性和大规模扩展能力。

Method: 提出SCoPE框架，将视觉大语言模型（VLLM）作为自监督偏好优化循环中的主动参与者，而非静态特征提取器。该框架自主假设、验证和强化候选视觉属性，通过自监督偏好目标直接将概念发现融入VLLM骨干网络，而非依赖静态生成和脱钩过滤。

Result: 在自然、医学和物理等多个领域的实验中，SCoPE成功提取了标准VLLM通常无法生成的领域特定概念；在未见数据上，下游top-1分类准确率绝对提升高达24个百分点。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以通过模型与偶然视觉结构的自主交互而涌现，完全无需任何人工监督。

Original Abstract: Current representation learning paradigms force a fundamental compromise: self-supervised methods scale to massive datasets but yield opaque features, whereas interpretable models remain bottlenecked by the need for dense human annotation. We introduce Self-Supervised Concept discOvery via Preference lEarning (\model), a label-free framework that resolves this dilemma. Instead of treating Vision-Large-Language Models (VLLMs) as static feature extractors, \model leverages them as active participants in a self-supervised preference optimization loop. By autonomously hypothesizing, validating, and reinforcing candidate visual attributes directly from raw imagery, our framework discovers novel, structured concepts without a single label. Extensive experiments across natural, medical, and physics domains demonstrate that \model successfully extracts domain-specific concepts where standard VLLMs often fail to generate. By amortizing concept discovery directly into the VLLM backbone through our self-supervised preference objective -- rather than relying on static generation and disjoint filtering -- we achieve up to a 24-point absolute improvement in downstream top-1 classification accuracy on unseen data. Our work suggest that interpretability can emerge through a model's autonomous interaction with incidental visual structures, without any human supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的表征学习范式存在一个根本性的权衡：自监督方法可以扩展到大规模数据集，但产生的是不透明的特征；而可解释模型则受限于密集人工标注的需求。本文提出了一种无需标签的框架——基于偏好学习的自监督概念发现（SCoPE），以解决这一困境。与将视觉大语言模型（VLLM）视为静态特征提取器不同，SCoPE将它们作为自监督偏好优化循环中的主动参与者。通过从原始图像中自主地假设、验证和强化候选视觉属性，我们的框架无需任何标签即可发现新颖的结构化概念。在自然、医学和物理领域的广泛实验表明，SCoPE成功提取了标准VLLM通常无法生成的领域特定概念。通过将概念发现直接摊销到VLLM骨干网络中的自监督偏好目标中——而非依赖静态生成和脱钩过滤——我们在未见数据上的下游top-1分类准确率实现了高达24个百分点的绝对提升。我们的研究表明，可解释性可以通过模型与偶然视觉结构的自主交互而涌现，完全无需任何人工监督。

</details>


### [57] [StereoGeo: an end-to-end stereo camera calibration method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14619)
*Imane Meddour,Andréa Macario Barros,Cédric Gouy-Pailler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StereoGeo是一种端到端的立体相机标定网络，能同时估计双目相机的焦距、重力方向和相对外参，在真实基准上达到与单目标定方法相当的内参精度，并提供更优的立体外参估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有相机标定方法多依赖结构化环境中的标定板，或仅针对单目相机配置，且局限于内参或外参的单独估计，缺乏一种同时估计双目相机内参和外参的端到端方法。

Method: StereoGeo扩展了GeoCalib算法，将深度神经网络特征提取与可微分优化器相结合，构建端到端网络，同时估计左右相机的焦距、重力方向以及相对外参变换。

Result: 在真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，StereoGeo在内参标定上达到了有竞争力的性能，并提供了准确的立体外参估计，优于现有的单目设置方法。

Conclusion: StereoGeo有效解决了双目立体相机标定问题，通过端到端学习同时估计内外参数，在真实场景中表现优异，且部分数据集已开源。

Original Abstract: In this work, we propose StereoGeo, an end-to-end network-based approach for stereo camera calibration. Our method estimates the focal lengths and gravity directions of the left and right cameras, as well as the relative extrinsic transformation relating them. Existing methods often rely on calibration patterns in structured environments or address only a single camera configuration, being limited to either intrinsic or extrinsic estimation, and depending on a multi-view setups. StereoGeo extends the GeoCalib algorithm, integrating deep neural network feature extraction with a differentiable optimizer. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that StereoGeo achieves competitive performance for intrinsic calibration and provides accurate stereo extrinsic estimation, outperforming existing methods that are limited to monocular settings. The dataset used in this work is partially publicly available at https://github.com/meddourimane/StereoGeo-dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 在这项工作中，我们提出了StereoGeo，一种基于端到端网络的立体相机标定方法。该方法能够同时估计左右相机的焦距和重力方向，以及它们之间的相对外参变换。现有方法通常依赖结构化环境中的标定图案，或仅针对单目相机配置，局限于内参或外参的单独估计，并且依赖于多视图设置。StereoGeo扩展了GeoCalib算法，将深度神经网络特征提取与可微分优化器相结合。在真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，StereoGeo在内参标定上取得了有竞争力的性能，并提供了准确的立体外参估计，优于现有的单目设置方法。本工作使用的数据集部分公开，地址为https://github.com/meddourimane/StereoGeo-dataset。

</details>


### [58] [SED:Lightweight Saliency prediction for Event-based data via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14631)
*Romaric Mazna,Jean Martinet,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度时空卷积块的轻量级事件驱动显著性预测网络，通过知识蒸馏将模型压缩562倍（180 MB→0.32 MB），在保持或超越教师模型性能的同时，具备从合成数据到真实数据的强泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件驱动显著性预测方法要么是神经形态方法在基准上表现不佳，要么依赖Transformer或3D卷积导致模型过重，无法部署于资源受限的边缘设备。因此需要一种轻量级且高效的网络。

Method: 提出轻量级网络，核心是Depthwise Spatio-Temporal Block (DSTconv)——对3D深度可分离卷积进行因式分解；并通过知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation）训练，以轻量学生网络学习教师网络的知识。

Result: 模型大小从180 MB降至0.32 MB（压缩562倍），参数量从45M降至81k（减少554倍）；在N-DHF1K和N-UCF Sports数据集上匹配或超越教师模型性能；从合成数据迁移到真实事件数据时表现出强泛化能力，而从头训练的模型则无法泛化。

Conclusion: 基于DSTconv的轻量级网络结合知识蒸馏，能够在极大幅度压缩模型的同时保持甚至提升性能，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力，适合边缘设备的实时事件驱动显著性预测任务。

Original Abstract: Event-based saliency prediction has gained attention recently, as combining event cameras with saliency estimation can act as an upstream stage that naturally improves the efficiency of downstream eventbased perception at the edge. However, current approaches are either neuromorphic, underperforming on event-based saliency benchmarks, or too heavy for resource-constrained edge applications due to their reliance on transformers or 3D convolutions. Drawing inspiration from efficient convolutional modules, SED and aiming to exploit the temporal information in event data, we propose a lightweight network, trained through knowledge distillation, built on a Depthwise Spatio-Temporal Block (DSTconv) -- a factorization of the 3D depthwise separable convolution. Relative to its teacher, our model reduces the model size from 180 MB to 0.32 MB (562x) and the parameter count from 45M to 81k (554x), while matching or outperforming it on the N-DHF1K and N-UCF Sports datasets. Moreover, it generalizes strongly beyond its training distribution, transferring from synthetic to real event data where a model trained from scratch fails.
