中文摘要翻译: 基于事件的显著性预测近期受到关注，因为将事件相机与显著性估计结合可以作为一个上游阶段，从而自然提升边缘端下游基于事件感知任务的效率。然而，现有方法要么是神经形态方法，在事件驱动的显著性基准上表现不佳，要么因依赖Transformer或3D卷积而对资源受限的边缘应用而言过于沉重。受高效卷积模块SED的启发，并旨在利用事件数据中的时间信息，我们提出了一种轻量级网络，通过知识蒸馏进行训练，其核心构建在深度时空卷积块（DSTconv）上——这是对3D深度可分离卷积的一种因式分解。相对于其教师模型，我们的模型将模型大小从180 MB降至0.32 MB（压缩562倍），参数量从45M降至81k（减少554倍），同时在N-DHF1K和N-UCF Sports数据集上匹配或超越了教师模型的性能。此外，该模型在其训练分布之外展现出强大的泛化能力，能够从合成事件数据迁移到真实事件数据，而从头训练的模型则无法实现这种泛化。

</details>


### [59] [Improving Lunar Topography with Deep Learning Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14638)
*Matthew Repasky,Erwan Mazarico,Michael K. Barker,Stefano Bertone,Terence J. Sabaka,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散薛定谔桥（Schrödinger Bridge）的生成式建模方法，用于月球地形超分辨率重建。该方法结合光学影像作为物理约束，将低分辨率地形分布映射到高分辨率地形分布，并在渲染的月球地形数据集上训练，实现灵活的超分辨率重建并能够提供像素级的不确定性估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有行星地形超分辨率方法（如分析型超分方法）计算成本高、难以大规模应用；而生成模型可以利用硬件加速和并行化实现规模化部署。本文旨在利用生成模型高效、可扩展地提升月球地形分辨率。

Method: 提出一种基于扩散薛定谔桥（Diffusion-based Schrödinger Bridge, SB）的生成式建模方法，连接低分辨率地形分布与高分辨率地形分布，并融入物理约束的光学影像。该方法受到Shape-from-Shading方法的启发，利用目标分辨率的光学图像改善先验的低分辨率地形。模型在一个新构建的渲染月球地形数据集上训练，该数据集模拟了月球勘测轨道飞行器窄角相机（LROC NAC）的光学影像。

Result: 该方法能够灵活地进行地形超分辨率重建，并在重建过程中提供像素级的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 基于扩散薛定谔桥的生成模型为行星地形超分辨率提供了一种灵活、可扩展且能够提供不确定性量化的有效途径。

Original Abstract: Increasing the resolution of planetary topography models can enable a better understanding of surface processes and geomorphology; however, existing analytical super-resolution methods are expensive and difficult to apply at large scales. Generative models provide the tools to learn complex relationships within data and can be applied at scale due to hardware accelerators and parallelization. We present a diffusion-based Schrödinger Bridge (SB) generative modeling approach for lunar topography super-resolution, connecting the distribution of low-resolution topography to that of high-resolution topography, incorporating physically-constraining optical imagery. Our approach is inspired by existing Shape-from-Shading methods, which improve a priori low-resolution topography by using optical images at the target resolution. We train SBs on a novel dataset of rendered lunar topography, emulating optical imagery from the Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter Narrow Angle Camera. The result is a flexible approach for topography super-resolution which can provide pixel-level uncertainties in the reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 提高行星地形模型的分辨率有助于更深入地理解地表过程和地貌特征；然而，现有的分析型超分辨率方法计算成本高昂，难以在大尺度上应用。生成模型提供了学习数据中复杂关系的工具，并且由于硬件加速器和并行化的支持，可以大规模部署。我们提出了一种基于扩散薛定谔桥（Schrödinger Bridge, SB）的生成式建模方法，用于月球地形超分辨率重建，该方法将低分辨率地形的分布与高分辨率地形的分布相连接，并融入了具有物理约束的光学影像。我们的方法受到现有从明暗恢复形状（Shape-from-Shading）方法的启发，后者通过使用目标分辨率的光学图像来改进先验的低分辨率地形。我们在一个新颖的渲染月球地形数据集上训练薛定谔桥模型，该数据集模拟了来自月球勘测轨道飞行器窄角相机（Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter Narrow Angle Camera）的光学影像。其成果是一种灵活的地形超分辨率方法，能够提供重建过程中的像素级不确定性估计。

</details>


### [60] [HPSv3++: Scaling Reward Models Across the Full Spectrum of Diffusion Model Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14657)
*Yijun Liu,Jie Huang,Zeyue Xue,Yuming Li,Ruizhe He,Haoran Li,Shijia Ge,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了HPSv3++奖励模型框架，通过双阶段训练和双维度偏好数据集，使奖励模型能够适应不同能力的T2I模型及其RL迭代变化，在多个基准上达到最优表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有奖励模型（如HPSv3）在预标注的早期数据上训练，无法适应T2I模型能力提升和RL迭代导致的质量判别分布偏移，限制了其广泛应用。

Method: (1) 构建HPDv3++数据集：212K双维度（文本忠实度+美学质量）偏好数据集，使用Qwen-Image模型与人工监督标注；(2) 两阶段训练框架：阶段一采用数据感知正交梯度投影，在保留HPSv3原有知识的同时融入HPDv3++的美学感知；阶段二利用来自不同能力和RL迭代的未标注数据，引入联合能力-迭代条件信号及标准差驱动的无监督指导机制。

Result: 在HPDv3上比HPSv3提升9.8%，在GenAI-Bench上提升5.5%，在提出的HPDv3++上达到79.1%/88.1%；用于T2I RL训练时，能一致性提升多种T2I模型的GenEval分数。

Conclusion: HPSv3++通过双阶段训练框架和双维度偏好数据集，有效解决了奖励模型在T2I模型能力和RL迭代变化下的适应性瓶颈，展现了跨能力-迭代谱系的广泛适用性。

Original Abstract: Reward models guide text-to-image (T2I) systems toward outputs aligned with human preferences. However, typical reward models such as HPSv3 are trained on pre-annotated data from earlier T2I models, without accounting for quality discriminative shifts arising from evolving model capabilities and reinforcement learning (RL) iterations, limiting their broader applicability. In this work, we propose HPSv3++, a reward model framework that elevates the HPSv3 model for varying T2I model capabilities and their RL iteration changes across the full capability-iteration spectrum. Specifically, we first introduce HPDv3++, a 212K dual-dimension preference dataset annotated for text fidelity and aesthetic quality using a recent high-capability (Qwen-Image) model with human supervision. We then propose a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 employs data-aware orthogonal gradient projection to incorporate diverse aesthetic perception from HPDv3++ while preserving the original effective human preference knowledge in HPSv3. Stage 2 further leverages unlabeled data from T2I models spanning different capability levels and RL iterations, and introduces a joint capability-iterations conditioned signal for the reward model together with a standard deviation-driven unsupervised guidance mechanism, strengthening reward model across the capability-iteration spectrum. HPSv3++ achieves state-of-the-art preference prediction, outperforming HPSv3 9.8% on HPDv3, 5.5% on GenAI-Bench, while achieving 79.1%/88.1% on our proposed HPDv3++. When used for T2I RL training, it consistently improves GenEval scores across diverse T2I models, demonstrating its wide-range capabilities. The code is available at https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/HPSv3-PlusPlus.

中文摘要翻译: 奖励模型引导文本到图像（T2I）系统生成与人类偏好对齐的输出。然而，典型的奖励模型（如HPSv3）是在早期T2I模型的预标注数据上训练的，未能考虑由模型能力提升和强化学习（RL）迭代引起的质量判别分布偏移，限制了其更广泛的应用。本文提出HPSv3++，一个奖励模型框架，将HPSv3模型提升至适应不同T2I模型能力及其RL迭代变化的全能力-迭代谱系。具体而言，我们首先引入HPDv3++，一个212K双维度偏好数据集，使用近期高能力模型（Qwen-Image）并在人工监督下对文本忠实度和美学质量进行标注。然后提出一个两阶段训练框架：阶段一采用数据感知正交梯度投影，在保留HPSv3原有有效人类偏好知识的同时，融入来自HPDv3++的多样化美学感知；阶段二进一步利用来自不同能力水平和RL迭代的T2I模型未标注数据，引入联合能力-迭代条件信号以及标准差驱动的无监督指导机制，增强奖励模型在能力-迭代谱系上的表现。HPSv3++实现了最先进的偏好预测，在HPDv3上比HPSv3提升9.8%，在GenAI-Bench上提升5.5%，并在我们提出的HPDv3++上达到79.1%/88.1%。当用于T2I RL训练时，它能一致性提升多种T2I模型的GenEval分数，展示了其广泛的能力范围。代码已开源：https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/HPSv3-PlusPlus。

</details>


### [61] [Giving AI a Headache: Acoustic Adversarial Attacks to Computer Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14658)
*Nicole Villavicencio-Garduño,Maksim Ekin Eren,Milo Prisbrey,Ben Migliori,Michael Teti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用 audible 频率（<20 kHz）的声波攻击商用摄像头，通过共振干扰其稳定机制，导致AI视觉模型（如YOLO11）产生误分类、漏检或幻觉目标，并分析了影响攻击效果的因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于超声波（>20 kHz）的声学攻击因高频衰减严重，作用距离受限。本文旨在探索使用更低频率（可听声范围）实现更远距离的攻击，并系统分析攻击对不同图像和目标特征的影响。

Method: 通过物理实验，使用不同频率的声波对商用摄像头进行共振攻击，测试其对现成目标检测模型YOLO11的干扰效果，并分析图像和目标特征与攻击效果的关系。

Result: 实验验证了 audible 频率声波攻击的有效性，并揭示了若干使AI视觉系统更易受攻击的因素，包括图像特征与目标特征层面的脆弱性分析。

Conclusion: 低频可听声波同样能有效干扰摄像头稳定机制并致盲AI CV模型，且作用距离可能更远。研究结果可为未来防御策略的制定提供依据。

Original Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to automate a variety of real-world computer vision (CV) applications, such as autonomous vehicle control, facial recognition, and security cameras. Recent research has shown that acoustic vibration can induce real physical motion in cameras, interfering with their internal stabilization mechanisms. Because the motion falls outside the conditions the stabilization system was designed to handle, the system introduces artifacts into the frame, causing AI-based CV models to misclassify, miss targets, or hallucinate objects.
  Previous work used ultrasonic frequencies (>20 kHz) to perform short-range attacks, which limits them to short distances due to the attenuation exhibited by high frequencies. In this work, we investigate acoustic attacks using lower frequencies in the audible range (<20 kHz), and we further expand our analysis to include how various image and object features are affected by the attacks.
  Specifically, we performed physical experiments to demonstrate the viability of our attacks on an off-the-shelf object detection model (YOLO11) by resonating a commercially available camera with various frequencies. Based on our results, we provide insights into several factors that make an AI CV system more vulnerable to these attacks, which could help inform the development of future mitigation strategies.

中文摘要翻译: 人工智能（AI）越来越多地被用于自动化各种现实世界的计算机视觉（CV）应用，如自动驾驶车辆控制、人脸识别和安全摄像头。最近的研究表明，声学振动能够在摄像头中诱发真实的物理运动，干扰其内部稳定机制。由于这种运动超出了稳定系统设计所能处理的条件，系统会在图像帧中引入伪影，导致基于AI的CV模型产生误分类、漏检目标或幻觉出目标等错误。先前的研究使用超声波频率（>20 kHz）进行短距离攻击，由于高频信号衰减严重，攻击距离受限。在本工作中，我们研究了使用可听声范围（<20 kHz）的较低频率进行声学攻击，并进一步扩展了分析，涵盖了攻击对各种图像和目标特征的影响。具体而言，我们通过物理实验，使用各种频率对商用摄像头进行共振，验证了我们对现成目标检测模型（YOLO11）攻击的可行性。基于我们的实验结果，我们揭示了若干使AI CV系统更易受这些攻击影响的因素，这有助于为未来防御策略的制定提供参考。

</details>


### [62] [Memento: Reconstruct to Remember for Consistent Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14667)
*Xuan Wei,Longbin Ji,Guan Wang,Xiangrui Liu,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Memento提出了一种基于主体重建指导的框架，通过联合训练自回归下一帧生成与基于记忆的主体重建，并引入双查询记忆机制和主体感知数据处理管线，实现了长视频生成中跨镜头的主体一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间分解方法在逐镜头生成长视频时，仅关注生成合理的下一镜头延续，但未验证历史记忆是否保留了主体身份的关键证据，导致随着生成过程推进，重复出现的主体信息被稀释、覆盖或遗忘。

Method: 1) 联合训练自回归下一镜头生成与基于记忆的主体重建任务；2) 提出双查询记忆机制，一个查询检索身份相关记忆，另一个选择短上下文关键帧；3) 设计主体感知的电影级数据处理管线，通过一致的无代词主体描述提供精确的重建监督。

Result: 在长期主体一致性、跨镜头连贯性和视觉质量方面达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 将主体保持视为显式身份接地问题，通过记忆银行应能仅从记忆重建主体这一前提来设计框架，可有效解决长视频生成中的主体一致性问题。

Original Abstract: Long-form video generation requires recurring subjects to remain consistent across various shots, viewpoints, motions, and scene transitions. Existing temporal decomposition methods improve scalability by generating videos shot by shot. However, they mainly focus on optimizing plausible next-shot continuations without verifying whether the historical memory preserves identity-critical subject evidence. Consequently, as generation proceeds, recurring subjects may be diluted, overwritten, or forgotten. In this paper, we propose Memento, a subject-reconstruction-guided framework that treats subject preservation as an explicit identity grounding problem, based on the premise that a memory bank faithfully preserving a subject should support reconstructing that subject from memory alone. Specifically, Memento jointly trains autoregressive next-shot generation with memory-based subject reconstruction, recovering target appearances using historical memory and global story captions. To disentangle long-range subject evidence from short-range cues, Memento introduces a dual-query memory mechanism, where one query retrieves identity-relevant memory and the other selects short-context keyframes for coherent continuation. Additionally, a subject-aware cinematic data pipeline provides precise reconstruction supervision via consistent, pronoun-free subject descriptions. Experiments demonstrate that Memento achieves state-of-the-art performance in long-term subject consistency, cross-shot coherence, and visual quality.

中文摘要翻译: 长视频生成需要重复出现的主体在各种镜头、视角、动作和场景转换中保持一致。现有的时间分解方法通过逐镜头生成视频来提升可扩展性，但它们主要专注于优化合理的下一镜头延续，而未验证历史记忆是否保留了主体身份的关键证据。因此，随着生成过程的推进，重复出现的主体可能会被稀释、覆盖或遗忘。在本文中，我们提出了Memento，一个基于主体重建指导的框架，将主体保持视为一个显式的身份接地问题，其前提是：一个忠实保存主体的记忆银行应能仅从记忆本身重建该主体。具体而言，Memento联合训练自回归下一镜头生成与基于记忆的主体重建，利用历史记忆和全局故事描述来恢复目标外观。为了将长距离主体证据与短距离线索分离，Memento引入了一种双查询记忆机制，其中一个查询检索与身份相关的记忆，另一个选择短上下文关键帧以实现连贯的延续。此外，一个主体感知的电影级数据处理管线通过一致的无代词主体描述提供精确的重建监督。实验表明，Memento在长期主体一致性、跨镜头连贯性和视觉质量方面达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [63] [HumP-KD: A Hybrid Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Stage Progressive Knowledge Distillation Framework for Efficient Fire Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14684)
*Mohammed Arif Mainuddin,Najifa Tabassum,Omar Ibne Shahid,Riasat Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HumP-KD，一种混合不确定性感知的多阶段渐进式知识蒸馏框架，用于高效火灾分类。通过从两个冻结的异构Transformer教师（Swin-Tiny和ViT-Base）及其Meta-MLP集成中蒸馏知识到轻量级MobileViT-S学生模型，在仅4.94M参数下达到0.9876的F1分数，显著优于基线，并适合实时部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实时火灾分类系统需要同时具备高精度、高计算效率且可在资源受限硬件上部署的模型，而现有模型在精度与效率之间难以取得平衡。

Method: HumP-KD包含三个紧密集成的组件：1）分层渐进式知识蒸馏，使用分层特征构建器生成融合空间注意力掩码，引导蒸馏聚焦于判别性区域；2）多阶段知识蒸馏，在训练过程中逐步激活三个蒸馏阶段；3）从两个冻结的异构Transformer教师（Swin-Tiny和ViT-Base）及其Meta-MLP集成中蒸馏知识到轻量级MobileViT-S学生模型。

Result: 在Dataset-II上，HumP-KD在10次独立实验中平均F1分数达到0.9876±0.0063，显著优于未使用蒸馏的MobileViT-S基线（0.9537±0.0351），独立t检验（p=0.0195）和Wilcoxon符号秩检验（W=1，p=0.0039）均确认统计显著性。学生模型仅保留4.94M参数和19.01Mb模型大小，相比Swin-Tiny减少5.7倍参数，相比ViT-Base减少17.5倍，实现37.72 CPU FPS，适合实时部署。

Conclusion: HumP-KD通过异构Transformer教师的知识蒸馏，在保持轻量级的同时显著提升了火灾分类精度，并展现出跨数据集的强泛化能力和在退化视觉条件下的鲁棒性，适合资源受限硬件上的实时部署。

Original Abstract: Real-time fire classification systems require models that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and deployable on resource-constrained hardware. This work proposes \textbf{HumP-KD}, a Hybrid Uncertainty-aware Multi-stage Progressive Knowledge Distillation framework for efficient fire classification. Two datasets, FlameVision and Dataset-II, containing 8,600 and 31,309 images, are used. Various CNN and transformer baselines are applied under standard preprocessing, online augmentation, Gaussian noise and motion blur robustness conditions. The proposed HumP-KD model distills knowledge from two frozen heterogeneous transformer teachers, Swin-Tiny and ViT-Base, along with their Meta-MLP ensemble, into a lightweight MobileViT-S student via three tightly integrated components. Hierarchical Progressive Knowledge Distillation employs a Hierarchical Feature Builder. It generates a fused spatial attention mask to guide distillation toward discriminative regions selectively. Multi-Stage Knowledge Distillation progressively activates three distillation stages across training. On Dataset-II, HumP-KD achieves a mean F1 score of $0.9876 \pm 0.0063$ across 10 independent trials, significantly outperforming the MobileViT-S baseline trained without distillation ($0.9537 \pm 0.0351$), with statistical significance confirmed by both independent t-test ($p = 0.0195$) and Wilcoxon signed-rank test ($W = 1$, $p = 0.0039$). The proposed method also demonstrates strong generalization across datasets and robustness under degraded visual conditions. The student model retains only 4.94M parameters and 19.01Mb model size, representing a $5.7\times$ parameter reduction over Swin-Tiny and a $17.5\times$ reduction over ViT-Base, while achieving 37.72 CPU FPS, making it suitable for real-time deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 实时火灾分类系统需要同时具备高精度、高计算效率且可在资源受限硬件上部署的模型。本文提出\textbf{HumP-KD}，一种混合不确定性感知的多阶段渐进式知识蒸馏框架，用于高效火灾分类。研究使用了两个数据集，FlameVision和Dataset-II，分别包含8,600张和31,309张图像。在标准预处理、在线增强、高斯噪声和运动模糊鲁棒性条件下，应用了多种CNN和Transformer基线模型。所提出的HumP-KD模型通过三个紧密集成的组件，将来自两个冻结的异构Transformer教师模型（Swin-Tiny和ViT-Base）及其Meta-MLP集成的知识蒸馏到轻量级MobileViT-S学生模型中。分层渐进式知识蒸馏采用分层特征构建器，生成融合空间注意力掩码，选择性地引导蒸馏聚焦于判别性区域。多阶段知识蒸馏在训练过程中逐步激活三个蒸馏阶段。在Dataset-II上，HumP-KD在10次独立实验中平均F1分数达到$0.9876 \pm 0.0063$，显著优于未使用蒸馏训练的MobileViT-S基线（$0.9537 \pm 0.0351$），独立t检验（$p = 0.0195$）和Wilcoxon符号秩检验（$W = 1$, $p = 0.0039$）均确认了统计显著性。所提出的方法还展现出跨数据集的强泛化能力和在退化视觉条件下的鲁棒性。学生模型仅保留4.94M参数和19.01Mb模型大小，相比Swin-Tiny参数减少5.7倍，相比ViT-Base参数减少17.5倍，同时达到37.72 CPU FPS，适合实时部署。

</details>


### [64] [CottonLeafVision: An Explainable and Robust Deep Learning Framework for Cotton Leaf Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14686)
*Rafi Ahamed,Md. Abir Rahman,Tasnia Tarannum Roza,Munaia Jannat Easha,Md. Asif Khan,Sudeepta Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CottonLeafVision系统，通过评估多种预训练深度卷积神经网络（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、VGG19）对棉花叶片病害图像进行分类，其中DenseNet201取得了98%的最高分类准确率，并结合Grad-CAM、遮挡敏感性分析和对抗训练等技术增强模型可解释性与鲁棒性，最终开发了面向实际农业应用的原型系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 棉花作为纺织业关键经济作物，精确识别和检测棉花叶片病害对经济稳定至关重要，因此需要开发高精度、可解释且鲁棒的深度学习分类系统以解决实际农业场景中的病害管理问题。

Method: 评估了DenseNet201、InceptionV3和VGG19三种预训练深度卷积神经网络；采用Grad-CAM进行梯度加权类激活映射以实现可解释性；使用遮挡敏感性分析评估模型对输入区域的依赖；应用对抗训练增强模型对噪声的鲁棒性；最终开发了面向实际农业应用的原型系统。

Result: 在包含7个类别（6种病害+1种健康）的公开棉花叶片病害数据集上，DenseNet201取得了98%的最高分类准确率，优于InceptionV3和VGG19。

Conclusion: 深度卷积神经网络在真实棉花病害管理场景中具备有效的病害分类能力，所提出的CottonLeafVision系统结合可解释性和鲁棒性增强技术，可为实际农业生产提供可靠的病害诊断支持。

Original Abstract: Globally, cotton is a highly economically beneficial crop, as the textile industry heavily depends on it. So, the precise identification and detection of cotton leaf disease is crucial for economic stability. The development goal of "CottonLeafVision" is to accurately classify and detect cotton leaf disease. With this goal, we have evaluated multiple pretrained Deep Convolutional Neural Networks, including DenseNet201, InceptionV3, and VGG19 on a publicly available cotton leaf disease image dataset. This image dataset includes seven classes, six disease classes, and one healthy class, collected under various field conditions reflecting real-world challenges. Among these pretrained models, with DenseNet201, we have achieved the highest classification accuracy of 98%. To enhance the model reliability and interpretability, we have implemented different techniques and methods such as Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM), occlusion sensitivity analysis and adversarial training to increase the noise resistance of the model. Finally, we have developed a prototype in order to utilize the model's capabilities on real life agriculture. This paper shows the deep learning model's capabilities to classify the disease in real-life cotton disease management situations.

中文摘要翻译: 在全球范围内，棉花作为纺织行业高度依赖的经济作物具有重要经济价值。因此，精确识别和检测棉花叶片病害对经济稳定至关重要。"CottonLeafVision"的开发目标是对棉花叶片病害进行准确分类与检测。为实现这一目标，我们在公开的棉花叶片病害图像数据集上评估了多种预训练深度卷积神经网络，包括DenseNet201、InceptionV3和VGG19。该图像数据集包含7个类别——6个病害类别和1个健康类别，是在反映真实世界挑战的各种田间条件下采集的。在这些预训练模型中，使用DenseNet201取得了98%的最高分类准确率。为增强模型的可靠性和可解释性，我们实现了多种技术与方法，如梯度加权类激活映射（Grad-CAM）、遮挡敏感性分析以及对抗训练以提高模型的抗噪能力。最后，我们开发了一个原型系统，以在真实农业生产中利用该模型的能力。本文展示了深度学习模型在真实棉花病害管理场景中对病害进行分类的能力。

</details>


### [65] [ClinHallu: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Stage-Wise Hallucinations in Medical MLLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14697)
*Sicheng Yang,Hangjie Yuan,Wenjun Zhang,Jinwang Wang,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Fan Wang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ClinHallu基准，用于在医学多模态大语言模型推理过程中诊断阶段级幻觉（视觉识别、知识回忆、推理整合），包含7,031个验证实例，并通过阶段替换干预和轨迹监督微调来检测和缓解幻觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学幻觉基准主要关注数据收集，忽视了幻觉在推理过程中不同阶段（视觉识别、医学知识回忆、推理整合）的起源差异，缺乏细粒度的阶段级诊断能力。

Method: 构建ClinHallu基准，包含7,031个实例，每个实例带有结构化的推理轨迹（分为视觉识别、知识回忆、推理整合三个阶段）；采用阶段替换干预方法测量纠正特定阶段对最终答案的影响；提出轨迹监督微调方法减少阶段级幻觉。

Result: ClinHallu提供了细粒度的幻觉诊断测试平台；阶段替换干预能够量化各阶段对最终答案的贡献；轨迹监督微调有效减少了阶段级幻觉。

Conclusion: ClinHallu为诊断和缓解医学多模态大语言模型中的推理失败提供了细粒度的幻觉测试平台，有助于构建更可信的临床决策支持系统。

Original Abstract: Building trustworthy medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is critical for reliable clinical decision support. Existing medical hallucination benchmarks mainly focus on data collection, but often ignore where hallucinations originate within the reasoning process. We find that hallucination sources vary across samples: errors may arise from visual misrecognition, incorrect medical knowledge recall, or flawed reasoning integration. To enable source-level hallucination diagnosis, we introduce ClinHallu, a benchmark for stage-wise hallucination diagnosis in medical MLLM reasoning. ClinHallu contains 7,031 validated instances, where each instance is augmented with a structured reasoning trace decomposed into Visual Recognition, Knowledge Recall, and Reasoning Integration. We also use stage-replacement interventions to measure how correcting specific stages affects the final answer. Beyond evaluation, we show that trace-supervised fine-tuning reduces stage-wise hallucinations. ClinHallu provides a fine-grained hallucination testbed for diagnosing and mitigating reasoning failures in medical MLLMs. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu.

中文摘要翻译: 构建可信的医学多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对于可靠的临床决策支持至关重要。现有的医学幻觉基准主要关注数据收集，但往往忽略了推理过程中幻觉的起源。我们发现，幻觉来源因样本而异：错误可能源于视觉误识别、不正确的医学知识回忆或有缺陷的推理整合。为了实现源级别的幻觉诊断，我们引入了ClinHallu——一个用于医学MLLM推理中阶段级幻觉诊断的基准。ClinHallu包含7,031个经过验证的实例，每个实例都附加了分解为视觉识别、知识回忆和推理整合三个阶段的结构化推理轨迹。我们还采用阶段替换干预来测量纠正特定阶段对最终答案的影响。除评估外，我们证明轨迹监督微调可以减少阶段级幻觉。ClinHallu为诊断和缓解医学MLLM中的推理失败提供了细粒度的幻觉测试平台。该基准已在https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu公开。

</details>


### [66] [Instruct-Particulate: Scaling Feed-Forward 3D Object Articulation with Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14699)
*Ruining Li,Yuxin Yao,Matt Zhou,Chuanxia Zheng,Christian Rupprecht,Joan Lasenby,Shangzhe Wu,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Instruct-Particulate模型，通过输入3D网格和目标运动学规范（部件描述、连接关系、关节类型等），预测部件的运动学分割与关节运动参数。模型利用异构数据集（15万+物体）训练，结合视觉-语言模型自动生成测试时的运动学规范，在跨类别泛化和AI生成网格上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法虽能估计3D物体的可动结构，但因标注数据稀缺导致泛化能力受限。为解决该问题，本文旨在利用更丰富的异构训练数据，并通过运动学规范消除任务歧义，提升模型对未见类别和AI生成网格的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Instruct-Particulate模型，输入为3D网格和目标运动学规范（部件描述、连接性、关节类型及可选点提示），输出为运动学部件分割与关节运动参数。利用大规模视觉-语言模型自动生成测试时的运动学规范。构建了一个包含15万+可动3D物体的异构数据集，通过对现有公开数据集和半标注3D模型进行运动学标注来扩充训练数据。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在跨类别泛化和AI生成网格上表现更优，并能通过图像到3D模型实现从真实世界图像重建可动资产。

Conclusion: Instruct-Particulate通过引入运动学规范和异构大规模训练数据，有效提升了可动3D物体结构估计的泛化能力，为动画、游戏和机器人仿真等领域提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing articulated 3D objects is important for animation, gaming, and robotic simulations. Recent neural networks can estimate the articulated structure of 3D objects, but their generalization remains limited by the scarcity of annotated data for this task. To address this gap, we introduce Instruct-Particulate, a model that takes a 3D mesh together with a target kinematic specification, including part descriptions, connectivity, joint types, and optional point prompts, and predicts the corresponding kinematic part segmentation and joint motion parameters. The kinematic specification disambiguates the task and allows the model to target annotations of different granularity, thereby making it possible to use more abundant heterogeneous training data. At test time, the kinematic specification can be obtained automatically from large-scale vision-language models, so the model can be applied to any input mesh. To train our model at scale, we construct a heterogeneous dataset of more than 150,000 articulated 3D objects, extending existing publicly available collections with data obtained by partially labelling other 3D models (monolithic or already decomposed into parts) with kinematic labels by means of vision-language models. Experiments show that our model generalizes better across categories and to AI-generated meshes, enabling articulated asset reconstruction from real-world images via image-to-3D models.

中文摘要翻译: 重建可动3D物体对于动画、游戏和机器人仿真至关重要。最近的神经网络能够估计3D物体的可动结构，但它们的泛化能力受到该任务标注数据稀缺的限制。为解决这一问题，我们引入了Instruct-Particulate，该模型以3D网格及目标运动学规范（包括部件描述、连接性、关节类型和可选的点提示）作为输入，并预测相应的运动学部件分割和关节运动参数。运动学规范消除了任务歧义，使模型能够针对不同粒度的标注进行学习，从而可以利用更丰富的异构训练数据。在测试阶段，运动学规范可由大规模视觉-语言模型自动获取，因此该模型可应用于任意输入网格。为了大规模训练我们的模型，我们构建了一个包含超过15万个可动3D物体的异构数据集，通过使用视觉-语言模型对现有公开数据集以及部分标注的其他3D模型（完整的或已分解为部件的）进行运动学标注，扩展了现有公开数据集合。实验表明，我们的模型在跨类别泛化和AI生成网格上表现更优，并能通过图像到3D模型实现从真实世界图像中重建可动资产。

</details>


### [67] [RepFusion: Leveraging Multimodal Priors for Denoising in Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14700)
*Xichen Pan,Aashu Singh,Satya Narayan Shukla,Xiangjun Fan,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepFusion将多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为噪声表示编码器，利用其输出的进化条件信号指导扩散Transformer进行去噪，从而在文本到图像生成中超越同等计算预算的基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的T2I系统中，LLM仅用于文本编码，而去噪过程由新训练的生成骨干网络处理。受MLLM中MLP投影器足以对齐干净视觉表示与预训练LLM的启发，作者希望将这种对齐机制从干净输入扩展到噪声输入，从而利用MLLM本身作为噪声表示编码器来提供更强的先验知识。

Method: RepFusion将MLLM重新用作噪声表示编码器，使其输出作为扩散Transformer的条件信号。具体来说，通过将MLLM从处理干净视觉表示扩展到处理噪声输入，实现对去噪过程中进化噪声表示的持续条件化。

Result: 在相似推理预算的控制比较中，RepFusion优于将相当容量分配给新初始化解码器的基线方法。结果表明，MLLM为去噪视觉表示提供了强先验，且通过重复的MLLM条件化可以有效地利用测试时计算。

Conclusion: MLLM能够为视觉表示的去噪过程提供强大的先验知识，通过在去噪过程中持续对进化的噪声表示进行条件化，可以更高效地利用测试时计算资源，从而提升T2I系统的生成质量。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in text-to-image (T2I) systems, but they are typically limited to text encoding, while denoising is handled by newly trained generative backbones. The emergence of representation autoencoders (RAEs) shifts the generation target toward semantically structured visual representations, creating a latent space that is more compatible with pretrained LLM priors. Inspired by multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), where an MLP projector is sufficient to align clean visual representations with a pretrained LLM, we repurpose the MLLM itself as a noisy representation encoder, extending this mechanism from clean to noisy inputs. We present RepFusion, which uses the resulting MLLM outputs as the conditioning signal for a diffusion transformer. In controlled comparisons at similar inference budgets, RepFusion outperforms baselines that devote comparable capacity to newly initialized denoisers. These results demonstrate that MLLMs provide strong priors for denoising visual representations and that, by conditioning on evolving noisy representations, test-time compute can be productively spent on repeated MLLM conditioning in modern T2I systems.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）被广泛用于文本到图像（T2I）系统，但它们通常仅限于文本编码，而去噪则由新训练的生成骨干网络处理。表示自编码器（RAE）的出现将生成目标转向语义结构化的视觉表示，创建了一个与预训练LLM先验更兼容的潜在空间。受多模态LLM（MLLM）的启发——其中MLP投影器足以对齐干净的视觉表示与预训练LLM——我们将MLLM本身重新用作噪声表示编码器，将这一机制从干净输入扩展到噪声输入。我们提出了RepFusion，该方法将生成的MLLM输出作为扩散Transformer的条件信号。在相似推理预算的控制比较中，RepFusion优于将相当容量分配给新初始化解码器的基线方法。这些结果表明，MLLM为去噪视觉表示提供了强大的先验，并且通过将条件化作用于不断进化的噪声表示，测试时计算可以有效地用于现代T2I系统中重复的MLLM条件化过程。

</details>


### [68] [RATS! Patches Talk Through Registers: Emergent Parts in Register Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14701)
*Timing Yang,Predrag Neskovic,Jansen Seheult,Wenchao Han,Anand Bhattad,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RATS（Register Attention Transformers），通过将分类令牌分解为N个可学习的注册令牌，并利用L→N→N→L瓶颈结构的压缩-通信-广播注意力机制，使自监督视觉模型自发学习到类似于物体部件的组合结构表示。无需辅助损失或部件标注，每个注册令牌自动特化为原语义区域。RATS在五个分割基准上平均超过基线+12 mIoU，并在ADE20K和COCO上持续提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人类在识别物体时能自动识别其组成部件（如鸟的头、翅膀、爪子），但自监督视觉模型能否自主发现这种组合结构尚不清楚。作者希望探索一种无需人工标注或辅助损失，就能让模型自发学习到部件级组合表示的架构。

Method: RATS将标准ViT中的[CLS]分类令牌分解为N个可学习的注册令牌（register tokens），通过L→N→N→L瓶颈结构实现三阶段注意力：压缩（Compress：将L个patch压缩到N个注册令牌）、通信（Communicate：注册令牌之间交互）、广播（Broadcast：将N个注册令牌的信息广播回L个patch）。N个注册令牌被分配到H个注意力头上，不同头之间的注册令牌互不交互。

Result: RATS在五个分割基准上平均超过基线+12 mIoU，ADE20K上提升+1.11 mIoU，COCO上提升+0.2 AP^m。其注册字典在相关类别间表现出部件级一致性和语义接近性。

Conclusion: RATS的注册令牌机制为结构化且可解释的视觉表示学习提供了一种有用的架构先验，表明自监督模型能够自发发现类似物体部件的组合结构。

Original Abstract: When humans see a bird, they recognize far more than just "bird" -- they see a head, wings, and talons, a structured assembly of reusable parts that can be identified across every bird they have ever seen. We ask whether a self-supervised visual model can discover the same compositional structure on its own. To this end, we propose RATS (Register Attention Transformers), which decomposes the classification token into N learnable register tokens that route patch information through an L->N->N->L bottleneck via a three-step compress-communicate-broadcast attention. The N registers are partitioned across the H attention heads, so that registers assigned to different heads do not interact with each other. Without auxiliary losses or part annotations, each register spontaneously specializes into a proto-semantic region whose emerging structure resembles object parts. RATS surpasses all baselines by +12 mIoU on average across five segmentation benchmarks, with consistent gains on ADE20K (+1.11 mIoU) and COCO (+0.2 AP^m). Its register dictionary further exhibits part-level consistency and semantic proximity across related categories. Our results suggest that RATS may provide a useful architectural prior for structured and interpretable visual representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 当人类看到一只鸟时，他们识别出的远不止"鸟"这一类别——他们看到头部、翅膀和爪子，这是一个由可复用部件组成的结构化组合，这些部件在他们见过的每一只鸟中都能被识别出来。我们探究自监督视觉模型能否自主发现同样的组合结构。为此，我们提出了RATS（Register Attention Transformers），该方法将分类令牌分解为N个可学习的注册令牌，通过L→N→N→L瓶颈结构，经由三步压缩-通信-广播注意力机制来路由图像块信息。N个注册令牌被分配到H个注意力头上，使得不同头之间的注册令牌互不交互。在无需辅助损失或部件标注的情况下，每个注册令牌自发特化为一个原语义区域，其涌现出的结构与物体部件相似。RATS在五个分割基准上平均超过所有基线+12 mIoU，并在ADE20K（+1.11 mIoU）和COCO（+0.2 AP^m）上持续取得提升。其注册词典在相关类别间进一步展现出部件级一致性和语义接近性。我们的结果表明，RATS可能为结构化且可解释的视觉表示学习提供了一种有用的架构先验。

</details>


### [69] [OmniVideo-100K: A Dataset for Audio-Visual Reasoning through Structured Scripts and Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14702)
*Xinyue Cai,Chaoyou Fu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Ran He,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniVideo-100K自动化数据引擎，通过实体锚定视频脚本和线索引导QA生成，解决音视频问答中模态分离、实体不一致和局部化问题，在多个基准上提升高达20.59%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音视频问答方法采用“视频-字幕-QA”范式，存在三个问题：将音视频分别描述割裂了声音与视觉源的关联；独立片段处理导致跨片段实体描述不一致；长文本理解和QA合成耦合，使得模型关注局部事件，缺乏长期时序连接和深度跨模态推理。

Method: 提出自动化数据引擎，包含两个机制：(1) 实体锚定视频脚本（Entity-Anchored Video Scripting），将视频转化为结构化脚本，包含摘要、主实体列表和逐段音视频描述，利用实体列表作为全局先验确保跨片段引用一致性和重建音视频关联；(2) 线索引导QA生成（Clue-Guided QA Generation），先挖掘跨片段、多模态线索，再基于这些高价值线索生成QA对。

Result: 构建了指令微调数据集OmniVideo-100K和人工验证测试集OmniVideo-Test。在OmniVideo-Test上取得高达20.59%的性能提升，在Daily-Omni和JointAVBench等基准上取得高达12.64%的改进，展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化数据引擎能有效解决现有音视频QA范式的固有缺陷，通过实体锚定和线索引导策略显著提升跨模态、跨片段推理能力，且具有良好的泛化性。

Original Abstract: Current automated pipelines for audio-visual Question Answering (QA) generally adopt a ``video-caption-QA'' paradigm. However, these methods typically segment videos into short clips and generate separate descriptions for audio and visual modalities. This decoupled processing severs inherent associations between sounds and their visual sources, while independent clip processing often causes inconsistent descriptions of the same entity across segments. Furthermore, coupling long-text comprehension and QA synthesis into a single step often restricts models to localized events, yielding questions lacking long-term temporal connections and deep cross-modal reasoning. To address these issues, we propose an automated data engine featuring two mechanisms: (1) \textbf{Entity-Anchored Video Scripting} transforms videos into structured scripts, comprising summaries, main entity lists, and segment-wise audio-visual descriptions. The entity list serves as a global prior to ensure cross-segment referential consistency and reconstruct audio-visual associations. (2) \textbf{Clue-Guided QA Generation} prompts models to first mine cross-segment, multimodal clues from the script, and subsequently generate QA pairs based on these high-value clues. Leveraging this pipeline, we construct the instruction-tuning dataset \textbf{OmniVideo-100K} and a human-verified test set, \textbf{OmniVideo-Test}. Fine-tuning VITA-1.5, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and Qwen3-Omni-30B on OmniVideo-100K yields performance gains of up to 20.59% on OmniVideo-Test, demonstrating strong generalization (up to 12.64% improvements) across established benchmarks like Daily-Omni and JointAVBench.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的音视频问答（QA）自动化流程通常采用“视频-字幕-QA”范式。然而，这些方法通常将视频切分为短片段，并分别为音频和视觉模态生成独立的描述。这种解耦处理割裂了声音与其视觉源之间的固有关联，而独立的片段处理常常导致同一实体在跨片段时出现不一致的描述。此外，将长文本理解与QA合成耦合为单一步骤，往往将模型限制在局部事件上，生成的问题缺乏长期时序连接和深度跨模态推理。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种自动化数据引擎，包含两个机制：(1) 实体锚定视频脚本（Entity-Anchored Video Scripting），将视频转换为结构化脚本，包含摘要、主实体列表和逐段音视频描述。实体列表作为全局先验，确保跨片段引用一致性并重建音视频关联。(2) 线索引导QA生成（Clue-Guided QA Generation），促使模型首先从脚本中挖掘跨片段、多模态线索，随后基于这些高价值线索生成QA对。利用这一流程，我们构建了指令微调数据集OmniVideo-100K和一个人工验证的测试集OmniVideo-Test。在OmniVideo-100K上微调VITA-1.5、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B和Qwen3-Omni-30B，在OmniVideo-Test上取得了高达20.59%的性能提升，并在Daily-Omni和JointAVBench等成熟基准上展现出强大的泛化能力（最高提升12.64%）。

</details>


### [70] [Gaze Heads: How VLMs Look at What They Describe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14703)
*Rohit Gandikota,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）的语言骨干中存在一小部分"凝视注意力头"（gaze heads），其注意力会追踪模型当前正在描述的图像区域。通过干预这些头部（不到全部头的9%），可以以83.1%的准确率引导模型描述任意指定的漫画面板区域，该机制在自然图像和不同模型规模中均有效，为无需重新训练即可操控多模态模型行为提供了实用接口。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型如何内部解决"描述图像"这一任务目前尚不明确，作者希望揭示其内部工作机制，并找到一种无需重新训练即可引导模型行为的方法。

Method: 1) 以漫画条（comic strips）作为受控测试场景，利用其叙事顺序在空间上明确排列的特点；2) 通过少量前向传播计算简单的相关性得分，定位出一组"凝视注意力头"（gaze heads）；3) 对Top-100个凝视头（不到全部注意力头的9%）施加单一的注意力掩码干预，将模型注意力重定向到目标区域。

Result: 1) 干预Top-100凝视头可使模型以83.1%的准确率描述指定漫画面板；2) 随机头部干预无法重定向回答，干预所有头部会破坏生成；3) 在生成过程中切换凝视目标，模型会在几个token内完成当前面板描述并转向新面板；4) 该方法在自然COCO图像上同样有效；5) 该机制在2B到32B参数规模的模型及不同VLM架构中重现，但部分冻结编码器家族无此现象。

Conclusion: 通过机械论分析识别的针对性编辑可以作为实用的推理时杠杆来操控多模态模型行为，无需任何重新训练，为理解和控制VLM的生成过程提供了新的视角和工具。

Original Abstract: How a vision-language model internally solves the task of describing an image is far from obvious. We find that the model develops a specific mechanism for this: a small set of attention heads in its language-model backbone, which we call gaze heads, whose attention tracks the image region the model is currently describing. We find them with a simple correlation score from a few forward passes, using comic strips as a controlled testbed where narrative order is laid out spatially. These gaze heads do not just track the image tokens being described: redirecting their attention to a chosen region forces the VLM to describe that region instead. A single attention-mask intervention on the top-100 gaze heads, fewer than 9% of all heads, steers the model's answer to any chosen comic panel at 83.1% accuracy, while the same intervention on random heads fails to redirect the answer, and intervening on all heads destroys generation. The same lever also extends to continuous control: switching the gaze target mid-generation makes the model wrap up its current panel description and move to the new one within a few tokens. Beyond comics, the same intervention redirects answers to chosen regions in natural COCO images. The mechanism further recurs across model sizes from 2B to 32B parameters and across other VLM architectures, although some frozen-encoder families show no comparable head set. More broadly, this shows that targeted edits identified through mechanistic analysis can serve as practical inference-time levers for steering multimodal model behavior, without any retraining. Our code, interactive demo, and datasets are available at https://gaze.baulab.info/

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型如何在内部解决描述图像的任务远非显而易见。我们发现模型为此发展出了一种特定机制：在其语言模型骨干中存在一小部分注意力头，我们称之为凝视注意力头（gaze heads），其注意力会追踪模型当前正在描述的图像区域。我们通过少量前向传播计算简单的相关性得分来发现它们，使用漫画条（comic strips）作为受控测试场景，其中叙事顺序在空间上明确排列。这些凝视头不仅追踪正在被描述的图像token：将它们的注意力重定向到选定区域会强制VLM转而描述该区域。对Top-100个凝视头（不到全部注意力头的9%）施加单一的注意力掩码干预，可以以83.1%的准确率引导模型回答任何选定的漫画面板，而同样的干预作用于随机头部则无法重定向回答，干预所有头部则会破坏生成。同一杠杆还可扩展到连续控制：在生成过程中切换凝视目标，会使模型在几个token内完成当前面板描述并转向新面板。超越漫画领域，同样的干预可将回答重定向至自然COCO图像中的选定区域。该机制在2B到32B参数规模的模型以及不同VLM架构中普遍存在，尽管部分冻结编码器家族未显示出可比的头部集合。更广泛地，这表明通过机械论分析识别的针对性编辑可以作为实用的推理时杠杆来操控多模态模型行为，无需任何重新训练。我们的代码、交互式演示和数据集可在https://gaze.baulab.info/获取。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [Decoding Semantic Categories from Picture-Naming EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14614)
*Wei Hu,Binbin Xu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过高密度脑电图（EEG）在图片命名任务中解码语义类别信息，发现早期和命名相关的时间窗口提供互补信息，结合两者可实现高于机会水平的语义类别区分（平衡准确率0.781，Macro-F1 0.784），表明语义类别结构在EEG活动中有所体现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究在图片命名过程中，语义类别信息是否可以从高密度EEG信号中恢复出来，以验证现代神经解码方法能否用于研究口语产生中的词汇-语义处理过程。

Method: 16名法语母语者完成线条图图片命名任务；使用多语言文本嵌入模型对图片标签进行语义嵌入并组织为9个可解释语义类别；采用预训练的单通道EEG编码器，按通道提取EEG特征，分别分析早期刺激后窗口、命名相关晚期窗口及其组合的9类语义分类解码。

Result: 所有时间窗口的9类解码均高于机会水平；平衡准确率从早期窗口的0.562提升至命名相关窗口的0.610，两者组合达0.781，最高Macro-F1为0.784；各语义类别F1分数均有稳定提升；传感器级解码图显示类别信息在空间上呈分布式模式。

Conclusion: 语义类别结构在图片命名过程的EEG活动中有所反映，且早期感知处理窗口与命名相关语言处理窗口提供互补信息，支持将现代神经解码方法用于研究口语产生中的词汇-语义加工。

Original Abstract: Picture naming requires the transformation of visual object information into a spoken lexical response through perceptual, semantic, lexical, and articulatory processes. This study asked whether semantic-category information is recoverable from high-density EEG during overt picture naming. Sixteen native French-speaking participants performed a picture-naming task using line drawings. Picture labels were embedded with a multilingual text-embedding model and organized into nine interpretable semantic categories, providing a data-driven semantic target space for neural decoding. EEG activity was represented channel-wise using a pre-trained single-channel EEG encoder over an early post-stimulus window, a later naming-related window, and their combination. Nine-class decoding showed above-chance semantic-category discrimination in all temporal representations. Balanced accuracy increased from 0.562 in the early window to 0.610 in the naming-related window, and reached 0.781 when both windows were combined, with a maximum Macro-F1 of 0.784. Class-level F1 scores showed consistent gains across semantic categories, and sensor-level decoding maps indicated spatially distributed category information. These findings suggest that semantic-category structure is reflected in EEG activity during overt picture naming and that early and naming-related temporal windows provide complementary information. The results support the use of modern neural decoding methods as tools for investigating lexical-semantic processing in spoken language production.

中文摘要翻译: 图片命名需要通过感知、语义、词汇和发音过程将视觉对象信息转化为口头词汇反应。本研究探究了在图片命名过程中，语义类别信息是否可以从高密度脑电图（EEG）中恢复。16名法语母语参与者使用线条图完成了图片命名任务。图片标签通过多语言文本嵌入模型进行嵌入，并组织为九个可解释的语义类别，为神经解码提供了数据驱动的语义目标空间。EEG活动采用预训练的单通道EEG编码器，按通道分别对早期刺激后窗口、后期命名相关窗口及其组合进行表征。九类解码结果显示，所有时间窗口的语义类别区分均高于机会水平。平衡准确率从早期窗口的0.562提升至命名相关窗口的0.610，当两个窗口联合使用时达到0.781，最高Macro-F1为0.784。各类别的F1分数显示语义类别均有一致的提升，传感器级解码图表明类别信息呈空间分布式。这些发现表明，在图片命名过程中，EEG活动反映了语义类别结构，且早期和命名相关时间窗口提供互补信息。结果支持使用现代神经解码方法作为研究口语产生中词汇-语义加工的工具。

</details>


### [72] [Prospective Coding and Path Integration Emerge as Equilibrium Solutions of Self-Organizing Neural Networks with Firing-Rate Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14649)
*Facundo Emina,Emilio Kropff*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出理论框架，揭示了连续吸引子神经网络（CANNs）中的结构化连接如何通过赫布可塑性、放电率适应和全局抑制自组织涌现，并证明前向架构本身就能自发产生预期性动态，速度调制可实现单向路径积分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索连续吸引子神经网络中结构化循环连接及其计算特性（空间表征、路径积分、预期动态）是否能够通过生物可解释的学习机制（而非手动预设）自组织涌现。

Method: 构建理论框架，利用赫布可塑性、放电率适应和全局抑制机制，分析平移不变输入下前馈权重自组织形成稳定高斯分布的过程；引入递归交互使网络学习自维持移动活动凸包；通过外部时变基线电流编码速度调制网络内在速度，实现路径积分。

Result: 1) 平移不变输入自然驱动前馈权重自组织为稳定高斯轮廓；2) 前馈架构自发产生预期性动态，使活动凸包向前移动而不需递归兴奋侧支；3) 多层网络中预期性位移可线性放大，与内嗅皮层浅层活动一致；4) 引入递归交互后可自维持移动凸包；5) 速度调制可实现精确单向路径积分。

Conclusion: 预期性编码和路径积分不是人工设计的特征，而是单一自组织竞争网络中自然共生的涌现属性。

Original Abstract: Continuous Attractor Neural Networks (CANNs) traditionally rely on pre-wired recurrent connectivity to model spatial representations, path integration, and anticipatory dynamics. However, the biological mechanisms through which this structured connectivity emerges via learning remain relatively unexplored. This work presents a theoretical framework revealing how continuous attractor connectivity and its computational properties self-organize through Hebbian plasticity, firing-rate adaptation, and global inhibition. We show that translationally invariant inputs naturally drive the emergence of stable, Gaussian-profiled feedforward weights. Crucially, anticipatory dynamics arise spontaneously within these feedforward architectures, shifting the activity bump forward without requiring recurrent excitatory collaterals. This predictive shift can be linearly amplified across multilayer networks, consistent with anticipatory activity observed in the superficial layers of the entorhinal cortex. Furthermore, introducing recurrent interactions allows the network to learn connections capable of self-sustaining a moving bump of activity. Finally, by modulating the network with an external, time-varying baseline current that encodes speed, the system adjusts its intrinsic velocity to function as a precise unidirectional path integrator. Ultimately, this study suggests that prospective coding and path integration are not manually engineered features, but rather naturally co-emergent properties of a single self-organizing competitive network.

中文摘要翻译: 传统上，连续吸引子神经网络（CANNs）依赖预先连接的循环连接来建模空间表征、路径积分和预期性动态。然而，这种结构化连接通过学习涌现的生物机制仍相对未被探索。本文提出了一个理论框架，揭示了连续吸引子连接性及其计算特性如何通过赫布可塑性、放电率适应和全局抑制进行自组织。我们证明，平移不变的输入自然驱动了稳定的、高斯轮廓的前馈权重的涌现。关键在于，预期性动态在这些前馈架构中自发产生，使活动凸包向前移动而无需递归兴奋性侧支。这种预期性位移可以在多层网络中线性放大，与内嗅皮层浅层中观察到的预期性活动一致。此外，引入递归交互使得网络能够学习能够自维持移动活动凸包的连接。最后，通过用编码速度的外部时变基线电流调制网络，系统调整其内在速度以充当精确的单向路径积分器。最终，本研究表明，预期性编码和路径积分不是人工设计的特征，而是单一自组织竞争网络中自然共生的涌现属性。

</details>


### [73] [Implications of hierarchical Markov models of behavior: on irreversibility, predictability, and dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14692)
*John J. Vastola,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了用马尔可夫模型描述动物行为序列的理论含义，指出模型矩阵的特征值和特征向量可提供可解释的时间尺度及其对应的行为变化，从而阐明了行为量化进展的理论意义。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着量化动物行为高级结构（尤其是将自发行为表示为固定"音节"序列）工具的成熟，需要重新审视一个基础理论问题：如果行为粗粒度结构能被马尔可夫模型准确描述，这些模型究竟揭示了行为的什么本质？

Method: 通过理论分析探讨马尔可夫模型描述行为序列的理论含义，利用模型关联矩阵的特征值和特征向量提取可解释的时间尺度及对应行为变化，并结合玩具示例和真实数据拟合的马尔可夫模型进行说明。

Result: 发现模型关联矩阵的特征值和特征向量能够提供可解释的时间尺度，以及在这些时间尺度上发生的行为修改模式；通过玩具示例和真实数据验证了这些理论发现。

Conclusion: 通过分析马尔可夫表示的理论后果，阐明了行为量化进展的理论意义。

Original Abstract: The maturation of quantitative tools for studying the high-level structure of animal behavior, and especially tools which represent spontaneous behavior as a sequence of stereotyped and neurally well-defined 'syllables', demands that the field revisit a fundamental theoretical question: if the coarse structure of behavior can be accurately described by Markov models, what do these models really tell us about behavior? In this work, we explore the theoretical implications of these models and discuss how they allow us to quantitatively formulate questions about the sequence-like nature and effective dimensionality of behavior. One important insight is that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of various model-associated matrices furnish interpretable time scales and modifications of behavior that occur on those time scales. We illustrate our points using both toy examples and Markov models fit to real data. By analyzing the consequences of Markov representations, we clarify the theoretical meaning of progress in quantifying behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 研究动物行为高级结构的量化工具日益成熟，尤其是那些将自发行为表示为一系列刻板且神经学上定义明确的"音节"的工具，这要求该领域重新审视一个基础理论问题：如果行为的粗粒度结构能够被马尔可夫模型准确描述，那么这些模型究竟告诉了我们关于行为的什么信息？在这项工作中，我们探讨了这些模型的理论含义，并讨论了它们如何使我们能够定量地阐述关于行为序列性质及其有效维度的问题。一个重要的洞见是，各种模型关联矩阵的特征值和特征向量提供了可解释的时间尺度，以及在这些时间尺度上发生的行为修改。我们通过玩具示例和拟合真实数据的马尔可夫模型来阐述我们的观点。通过分析马尔可夫表示的理论后果，我们阐明了行为量化进展的理论意义。

</details>

### [74] [GMN4AD: Graph Matching Network for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis with Test-Time Domain Adaptation using Multi-centered Structure Magnetic Resonance Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13919)
*Chen Zhao,Huan Huang,Yixin Xie,Jiajing Huang,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出GMN4AD，一种基于图匹配网络的阿尔茨海默病诊断方法，通过建模异质脑图间的跨图关系并引入测试时域自适应策略，在三个公开数据集上取得领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于图的方法在处理脑结构MRI数据时，难以应对模态异质性和跨站点的数据差异，导致诊断性能受限。

Method: 提出图匹配网络GMN4AD，利用图匹配技术捕获不同脑图之间的跨图关系；同时引入结合对比学习的测试时域自适应策略，缓解推理过程中的域偏移问题。

Result: 在三个公开AD数据集上的大量实验表明，GMN4AD相比现有最先进方法取得了更优的诊断性能，具有较强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GMN4AD通过图匹配和测试时域自适应，有效解决了脑图异质性和域偏移问题，为阿尔茨海默病诊断提供了一种鲁棒且可泛化的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Alzheimer's Disease (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder that affects millions of older adults, with prevalence expected to rise significantly in the coming years. Early diagnosis, particularly during the mild cognitive impairment (MCI) stage, is critical for timely intervention. Structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (sMRI) has emerged as a key modality for detecting AD-related brain changes, but traditional graph-based approaches often struggle with modality and inter-site heterogeneity, limiting diagnostic performance. In this paper, we propose Graph Matching Network for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis (GMN4AD), designed to model interactions between heterogeneous brain graphs derived from neuroimaging data. Unlike conventional methods that treat each brain graph independently, GMN4AD leverages graph matching to capture cross-graph relationships, enhancing diagnostic precision. Furthermore, we introduce a test-time domain adaptation strategy that combines contrastive learning to mitigate domain shifts during inference. Extensive experiments on three public AD datasets demonstrate that GMN4AD achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, offering a robust and generalizable solution for AD diagnosis.

中文摘要翻译: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）是一种进行性神经退行性疾病，影响数百万老年人，且其患病率在未来几年预计将显著上升。早期诊断，特别是在轻度认知障碍（MCI）阶段，对于及时干预至关重要。结构磁共振成像（sMRI）已成为检测AD相关脑部变化的关键模态，但传统的基于图的方法往往难以应对模态异质性和跨站点数据差异，限制了诊断性能。本文提出用于阿尔茨海默病诊断的图匹配网络（GMN4AD），旨在对从神经影像数据中提取的异质脑图之间的交互进行建模。与将每个脑图独立处理的传统方法不同，GMN4AD利用图匹配技术捕获跨图关系，从而提升诊断精度。此外，我们引入了一种测试时域自适应策略，结合对比学习来缓解推理过程中的域偏移。在三个公开AD数据集上的大量实验表明，GMN4AD相比现有最先进方法取得了更优的性能，为AD诊断提供了一种鲁棒且可泛化的解决方案。

</details>


### [75] [High-Fidelity Video Compression based on Invertible Neural Transform and Implicit Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13957)
*Siyue Teng,Ho Man Kwan,Yuxuan Jiang,Fan Zhang,David Bull*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于可逆神经网络的视频编解码器InnVC，通过可逆变换主路径与隐式条件场解耦内容与细节，结合调度掩码策略，在高质量区域相比x265实现21.66%（PSNR）和46.06%（MS-SSIM）的BD-rate节省。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于学习的视频压缩方法多采用非可逆的分析-合成变换，重建质量同时受到量化误差和变换近似误差的影响。在高质量区域，量化误差较小，变换引起的失真成为主导，限制了压缩性能。

Method: 1) 设计可逆神经网络作为量化前的主变换路径，保证信息无损传递；2) 通过紧凑的隐式条件场注入内容自适应上下文，将强相关的视频内容与难以建模的精细细节解耦，让不同组件互补；3) 提出调度掩码策略，渐进地将信息集中到更少的潜在通道中，提升熵编码效率。

Result: 在UVG和MCL-JCV基准上，InnVC在广泛质量范围内均实现了强压缩性能，尤其在高质量区域效果显著。在UVG上相对于x265，PSNR BD-rate降低21.66%，MS-SSIM BD-rate降低46.06%。

Conclusion: InnVC是首个在单一架构内覆盖从低比特率到高保真度（PSNR跨度超过20dB）操作点的神经视频编解码器，验证了可逆变换在视频压缩中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Learning-based video compression has recently achieved competitive rate-distortion performance compared to conventional video codecs. However, most existing methods rely on non-invertible analysis-synthesis transforms, with reconstruction quality subject to both quantization and transform approximation errors. This limitation becomes particularly restrictive at higher quality points, where quantization errors are small and transform-induced distortion dominates. To address this, we propose InnVC, an Invertible neural network based Video Codec for wide-range and high-fidelity compression. The core idea is to preserve an invertible main transform path prior to quantization, while injecting content-adaptive context through a compact implicit conditioning field. This decouples strongly correlated video content from harder-to-model fine details, allowing different components to specialize in complementary reconstruction tasks for more efficient compression. To further improve compressibility, we introduce a scheduled masking strategy that progressively concentrates informative content into fewer latent channels for more effective entropy coding. Experiments on the UVG and MCL-JCV benchmarks show that InnVC achieves strong compression performance over a broad quality range, being particularly effective in the high-quality regime, yielding BD-rate reductions of 21.66% in PSNR and 46.06% in MS-SSIM relative to x265 on UVG. To the best of our knowledge, InnVC is the first neural video codec covers operating poins from low bitrate to high fidelity within a single architecture scale, spanning more than 20 dB in PSNR.

中文摘要翻译: 基于学习的视频压缩最近在率失真性能上已能与传统视频编解码器相媲美。然而，现有方法大多依赖非可逆的分析-合成变换，其重建质量同时受限于量化和变换近似误差。这一局限在高质点尤为突出，此时量化误差较小，变换引起的失真成为主导。为解决此问题，我们提出InnVC——一种基于可逆神经网络的视频编解码器，面向宽范围高保真压缩。核心思想是在量化前保留可逆的主变换路径，同时通过紧凑的隐式条件场注入内容自适应上下文。这使强相关的视频内容与难以建模的精细细节得以解耦，让不同组件专注于互补的重建任务，实现更高效的压缩。为进一步提升可压缩性，我们引入调度掩码策略，逐步将信息集中到更少的潜在通道中，以实现更有效的熵编码。在UVG和MCL-JCV基准上的实验表明，InnVC在广泛质量范围内均实现了强压缩性能，尤其在高质区域表现突出：在UVG上相对于x265，PSNR BD-rate降低21.66%，MS-SSIM BD-rate降低46.06%。据我们所知，InnVC是首个在单一架构内覆盖从低比特率到高保真度（PSNR跨度超过20dB）操作点的神经视频编解码器。

</details>


### [76] [Spectrum Aware Illumination Estimation Using Multispectral Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14248)
*Hyejin Oh,Woo-Shik Kim,Sangyoon Lee,YungKyung Park,Je-Won Kang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度学习框架的多光谱图像照明白适应估计方法，通过空谱特征提取块、光谱注意力机制和照明显著先验（IP）增强光谱关联性，并引入光谱域变换实现不同传感器空间间的知识迁移，同时在真实多光谱数据集上取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法未能充分利用多光谱图像中的光谱信息，导致在不同光照条件和传感器域下表现欠佳。

Method: 提出了一个包含空谱特征提取块和光谱注意力机制的深度学习框架，引入照明显著先验（IP）突出信息量更高的通道，并设计了跨多光谱传感器空间的光谱域变换方法。

Result: 在高维传感器空间中学到的照明显著光谱可无需额外训练直接迁移到低维相机传感器空间；在真实多光谱数据集上的实验表明所提方法优于现有模型。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了多光谱图像照明显谱估计的准确性，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multispectral (MS) imaging extends beyond conventional RGB imaging by capturing more spectral bands, thereby improving illuminant spectrum estimation (ISE). However, existing methods often fail to fully exploit spectral information, resulting in suboptimal performance under diverse lighting conditions and across different sensor domains. Hence, we propose a deep learning framework with a spatio-spectral feature extraction block, which incorporates spectral attention mechanisms to enhance spectral correlation and preserve illuminant-relevant spatial features. Through the inclusion of an illuminant prior (IP), our approach prioritizes specific channels that provide more meaningful information in an MS image. We also propose a spectral-domain transform across different MS sensor spaces. The results demonstrate that illuminant spectra learned in high-dimensional sensor spaces can be effectively transformed to various lower-dimensional camera sensor spaces without any additional training. To facilitate evaluation, we introduce a real-world MS dataset containing high-dimensional ground-truth illumination spectra captured under diverse lighting conditions. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to existing models, thus providing a practical solution for real-world ISE. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/hyejin5/Spectrum-Aware-Illumination-Estimation-Using-Multispectral-Image.

中文摘要翻译: 多光谱（MS）成像通过捕获更多的光谱波段，超越了传统的RGB成像，从而改进了照明显谱估计（ISE）。然而，现有方法往往未能充分利用光谱信息，导致在多样光照条件和不同传感器域下的性能欠佳。为此，我们提出了一种包含空谱特征提取块的深度学习框架，该框架引入光谱注意力机制以增强光谱相关性并保留与照明显相关的空间特征。通过引入照明显著先验（IP），我们的方法能够优先关注多光谱图像中提供更有意义信息的特定通道。我们还提出了一种跨不同多光谱传感器空间的光谱域变换方法。结果表明，在高维传感器空间中学到的照明显谱可以有效地转换到各种较低维度的相机传感器空间，无需任何额外训练。为了便于评估，我们引入了一个真实世界的多光谱数据集，该数据集包含在多种光照条件下捕获的高维照明显谱真值。通过大量实验，我们证明该方法相比现有模型取得了更优的准确性，从而为真实场景中的照明显谱估计提供了实用的解决方案。代码和数据集可在 https://github.com/hyejin5/Spectrum-Aware-Illumination-Estimation-Using-Multispectral-Image 获取。

</details>


### [77] [Trimodal Glioma Representation Alignment via Volumetric Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14568)
*Denise Marini,Eleonora Grassucci,Danilo Comminiello*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: GLORIA是一个新型三模态框架，通过Gramian对比损失对齐组织病理学、mRNA表达和MRI三种模态数据，用于神经胶质瘤分级和生存预测，在三模态测试集上优于双模态基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有胶质瘤预后模型通常只融合两种模态数据，且模态对齐目标多为两两对齐，缺乏统一的三模态联合对齐框架，无法充分整合组织病理学、基因表达和MRI三种异质性信息的互补优势。

Method: GLORIA通过模态专用编码器处理全切片图像区域、基因表达谱和3D MRI体积三种模态数据，将其投影到共享潜空间，使用Gramian对比损失（度量三个模态嵌入张成的体积）进行对齐，再通过跨模态门控模块融合对齐后的表征，联合优化三分类胶质瘤分级和总生存期预测任务。

Result: 在包含132名患者的TCGA-GBM/LGG和BraTS21配对队列上评估，GLORIA在三模态测试集的所有指标上均优于WSI-mRNA双模态基线。

Conclusion: 三模态联合对齐和融合方案优于双模态方法，证明了整合组织病理学、转录组学和影像学数据对胶质瘤预后建模的有效性。

Original Abstract: Glioma grading and survival prediction require the integration of heterogeneous information collected at different spatial and biological scales. Histopathology describes tissue morphology, mRNA expression captures molecular activity, and magnetic resonance imaging provides a non-invasive view of tumor extent and radiological heterogeneity. Existing glioma prognosis models often combine only two of these sources, while their alignment objectives remain mostly pairwise. This paper introduces GLORIA, a novel trimodal framework for GLioma Omics - Radiology - hIstopathology Alignment. GLORIA processes whole-slide image regions, gene-expression profiles, and 3D MRI volumes through modality-specific encoders, projects them into a shared latent space, and aligns them with a Gramian contrastive loss that measures the volume spanned by the three modality embeddings. The aligned representations are fused through a cross-modal gating module and optimized jointly for three-class glioma grading and overall survival prediction. We evaluate GLORIA on a matched TCGA-GBM/LGG and BraTS21 cohort, comprising 132 patients with all three modalities. On the shared trimodal test set, GLORIA improves over the bimodal WSI-mRNA baseline in all the metrics considered.

中文摘要翻译: 神经胶质瘤分级和生存预测需要整合在不同空间和生物学尺度上收集的异质性信息。组织病理学描述组织形态，mRNA表达捕获分子活性，磁共振成像提供肿瘤范围和放射学异质性的非侵入性视图。现有的胶质瘤预后模型通常仅融合其中两个数据源，而其对齐目标仍然主要是成对的。本文提出了GLORIA，一个用于胶质瘤组学-放射学-组织病理学对齐的新型三模态框架。GLORIA通过模态专用编码器处理全切片图像区域、基因表达谱和3D MRI体积，将它们投影到共享潜空间，并使用Gramian对比损失进行对齐，该损失度量三个模态嵌入所张成的体积。对齐后的表征通过跨模态门控模块进行融合，并针对三分类胶质瘤分级和总生存期预测进行联合优化。我们在配对的TCGA-GBM/LGG和BraTS21队列（包含132名拥有全部三种模态的患者）上评估了GLORIA。在共享的三模态测试集上，GLORIA在所有指标上均优于WSI-mRNA双模态基线。

</details>
