### [137] [Machine Learning for Biomedical Raman Spectroscopy: From Spectral Acquisition to Clinical Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14169)
*Bogdan Oancea,Ana Maria Seciu-Grama,Nicoleta Siminea,Laura Mihaela Stefan,Alice Stoica,Joel Sjoberg,Marian Necula,Ana-Maria Prelipcean,Corneliu Ovidiu Vrancianu,Eduard Milea,Andrei Păun,Ion Petre,Mihaela Păun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习在生物医学拉曼光谱全流程中的作用，涵盖预处理、信号校正、无监督结构发现、监督诊断、分子分层、表征与迁移学习、可解释性、生物标志物发现以及多模态融合，并分析了临床转化的主要障碍与未来方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物医学拉曼光谱数据存在高维、噪声强、荧光背景干扰、采集变异及生物异质性等问题，传统分析方法难以应对，亟需借助机器学习实现鲁棒的计算分析，以推动拉曼光谱从科研走向临床落地。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了机器学习在拉曼光谱全流程中的应用，包括预处理与信号校正、无监督结构发现、监督诊断与分子分层、表征与迁移学习、可解释性分析、生物标志物发现，以及与影像、病理和分子谱的多模态融合。

Result: 揭示了机器学习在拉曼光谱分析中的广泛应用潜力，同时指出临床转化面临数据集规模有限、仪器间变异、预处理不一致、外部验证不足、可重复性问题以及软件数据共享有限等主要障碍。

Conclusion: 实现可靠的临床可部署拉曼-AI系统需要方法学进步与标准化、稳健验证、可解释性以及可部署分析框架相结合，研究应整合方法论、生物医学和转化视角，推动可靠系统的开发。

Original Abstract: Raman spectroscopy provides label-free, chemically specific characterization of biological systems and has become an important tool for cancer diagnosis, molecular subtyping, microbiological identification, and intraoperative decision support. Biomedical Raman spectra are, however, high-dimensional, noisy, and affected by fluorescence background, acquisition variability, and biological heterogeneity, making robust computational analysis essential.
  This review examines the role of machine learning across the biomedical Raman spectroscopy pipeline, from preprocessing and signal correction to unsupervised structure discovery, supervised diagnosis and molecular stratification, representation and transfer learning, explainability, biomarker discovery, and multimodal integration with imaging, pathology, and molecular profiling. Emphasis is placed on the use of machine learning not only for diagnostic classification, but also for biologically interpretable and clinically actionable analysis.
  We also discuss the main barriers to clinical translation, including limited dataset sizes, inter-instrument variability, inconsistent preprocessing, insufficient external validation, reproducibility concerns, and limited sharing of software, data, and metadata. We argue that progress will require methodological advances together with standardization, robust validation, explainability, and deployment-ready analytical frameworks. By integrating methodological, biomedical, and translational perspectives, this review outlines key directions for developing reliable and clinically deployable Raman-AI systems.

中文摘要翻译: 拉曼光谱能够以无标记、具有化学特异性的方式表征生物系统，已成为癌症诊断、分子分型、微生物鉴定及术中决策支持的重要工具。然而，生物医学拉曼光谱数据具有高维、噪声强、受荧光背景影响、采集变异大及生物异质性等特点，因此需要鲁棒的计算分析。本文综述了机器学习在生物医学拉曼光谱全流程中的作用，涵盖预处理与信号校正、无监督结构发现、监督诊断与分子分层、表征与迁移学习、可解释性、生物标志物发现，以及与影像、病理和分子谱的多模态融合。重点强调机器学习不仅用于诊断分类，还应服务于具有生物学可解释性和临床可操作性的分析。我们还讨论了临床转化的主要障碍，包括数据集规模有限、仪器间变异、预处理不一致、外部验证不足、可重复性问题以及软件、数据和元数据共享有限。我们认为，取得进展需要方法学进步与标准化、稳健验证、可解释性及可部署分析框架相结合。通过整合方法论、生物医学和转化视角，本综述勾勒了开发可靠且可临床部署的拉曼-AI系统的关键方向。

</details>


### [138] [Context-aware Modality-Topology Co-Alignment for Multimodal Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14172)
*Sirui Zhang,Xu Wang,Zhengyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Hongchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoMAG，一个统一的多模态属性图（MAG）骨干网络，通过任务自适应可靠上下文学习和模态保持的跳令牌对齐，同时支持图级任务和模态级任务，在九个数据集上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的MAG方法依赖固定的图上下文或统一融合表示，导致任务无关的传播和过度压缩的融合，无法兼顾不同任务需求并保留模态特有的证据。

Method: CoMAG包含两部分：(1) 可靠上下文学习——从多模态语义一致性估计边可靠性，用语义邻居补充原始拓扑，通过任务感知门选择上下文组件；(2) 模态保持的跳令牌对齐——维护模态特定的多跳轨迹，跨模态匹配模态-跳令牌，解耦共享和私有表示。

Result: 在九个OpenMAG数据集上，相比特征-only、图-only、多模态和统一MAG基线，CoMAG在图级预测、模态匹配和图条件生成任务上均取得最佳性能，同时保持稀疏边线性复杂度。

Conclusion: 任务自适应可靠上下文和模态保持的对齐能够同时改进结构预测、跨模态匹配和图条件生成，证明了统一MAG骨干的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Attributed Graphs (MAGs) model real-world entities by coupling graph topology with heterogeneous attributes such as text and images. They support graph-centric tasks requiring structural and class-discriminative representations, and modality-centric tasks requiring fine-grained cross-modal correspondence. However, existing MAG methods often rely on fixed graph contexts or uniformly fused representations, causing task-agnostic propagation and over-compressed fusion that hinder diverse task requirements and modality-specific evidence preservation. To address this, we propose CoMAG, a unified MAG backbone that learns task-adaptive reliable contexts and modality-preserving alignment within them. CoMAG first conducts Reliable Context Learning by estimating edge reliability from multimodal semantic consistency, complementing raw topology with semantic neighbors, and selecting context components through a task-aware gate. It then performs Modality-preserving Hop-token Alignment by maintaining modality-specific multi-hop trajectories, matching modality-hop tokens across modalities, and decoupling shared and private representations. Thus, CoMAG produces graph and modality representations from one forward pass while retaining modality-specific cues. We further analyze stable propagation, over-smoothing mitigation, and modality-collapse control. Experiments on nine OpenMAG datasets compare CoMAG with feature-only, graph-only, multimodal, and unified MAG baselines across graph-level prediction, modality matching, and graph-conditioned generation. Results show that CoMAG achieves the best reported performance, demonstrating that task-adaptive reliable contexts and modality-preserving alignment improve structural prediction, cross-modal matching, and graph-conditioned generation while retaining sparse edge-linear complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态属性图（MAG）通过将图拓扑结构与文本、图像等异构属性耦合来建模现实世界实体。它们支持需要结构和类别区分性表示的中心图任务，以及需要细粒度跨模态对应的中心模态任务。然而，现有的MAG方法通常依赖固定的图上下文或统一融合表示，导致任务无关的传播和过度压缩的融合，从而阻碍了多样化的任务需求和模态特定证据的保留。为解决这一问题，我们提出CoMAG，一个统一的MAG骨干网络，该网络学习任务自适应的可靠上下文并在其中保持模态对齐。CoMAG首先通过从多模态语义一致性估计边可靠性、用语义邻居补充原始拓扑以及通过任务感知门选择上下文组件来进行可靠上下文学习。然后，它通过维护模态特定的多跳轨迹、跨模态匹配模态-跳令牌以及解耦共享和私有表示来执行模态保持的跳令牌对齐。因此，CoMAG在单次前向传播中生成图和模态表示，同时保留模态特定的线索。我们进一步分析了稳定传播、缓解过平滑和控制模态坍塌。在九个OpenMAG数据集上的实验将CoMAG与特征-only、图-only、多模态和统一MAG基线在图级预测、模态匹配和图条件生成任务上进行了比较。结果表明，CoMAG达到了最佳报告性能，证明任务自适应可靠上下文和模态保持的对齐能够改善结构预测、跨模态匹配和图条件生成，同时保持稀疏边的线性复杂度。

</details>


### [139] [Zeta: Dual Whitening for Matrix Optimization via Coordinate-Adaptive Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14187)
*Kaiwen Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Linxiao Li,Ying Sun,Yuchen Li,Yifan Zhang,Bo Han,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Zeta优化器，通过双重白化（坐标白化+谱白化）解决矩阵感知优化器中Newton-Schulz迭代对输入条件的依赖问题，在语言模型（0.6B-8B参数）、MoE架构和视觉任务上匹配或超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有矩阵感知优化器（如Muon）的Newton-Schulz迭代严重依赖输入条件，而原始动量矩阵存在严重的坐标尺度异质性，导致正交化误差和收敛不稳定。

Method: 提出Zeta双重白化优化器：首先进行坐标白化（消除坐标尺度异质性），然后进行谱白化，两者严格顺序执行而非可调选项，因为坐标白化为谱白化建立所需的统计各向同性。

Result: 通过卡方均匀性检验验证了坐标尺度异质性普遍存在；证明双重白化比纯谱方法能降低正交化误差；在语言模型（0.6B至8B参数）、混合专家架构和视觉任务上匹配或超越强基线。

Conclusion: 在正交化前解决尺度不平衡问题能够加快收敛并获得更好的泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Large-scale neural network training increasingly relies on matrix-aware optimizers that exploit the structure of weight parameters beyond element-wise adaptation. However, existing matrix-aware methods such as Muon have an underappreciated vulnerability: their core operation, Newton-Schulz iteration, depends critically on input conditioning, yet the raw momentum matrices exhibit severe coordinate-wise scale heterogeneity. In this paper, we first verify this scale heterogeneity through a chi-square uniformity test, showing that intra-matrix scale imbalance is prevalent across Transformer layers and that coordinate whitening effectively corrects it. Motivated by this finding, we propose Zeta, a dual whitening optimizer that applies coordinate whitening and spectral whitening in a strictly ordered pipeline. The ordering is not a tunable choice but follows from a mathematical dependency: coordinate whitening establishes the statistical isotropy that spectral whitening requires to function reliably. We further prove that this dual pipeline strictly reduces orthogonalization error relative to pure spectral methods by improving the condition number of the input. Empirically, Zeta matches or surpasses strong baselines across language modeling (0.6B to 8B parameters), mixture-of-experts architectures, and vision tasks, demonstrating that resolving scale imbalance before orthogonalization leads to faster convergence and better generalization. Code is available at https://gitcode.com/kevin259/MindSpeed.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模神经网络训练越来越依赖于利用权重参数结构的矩阵感知优化器，而不仅仅进行逐元素自适应。然而，现有的矩阵感知方法（如Muon）存在一个未被充分认识的脆弱性：其核心操作Newton-Schulz迭代关键依赖于输入的条件数，但原始动量矩阵表现出严重的坐标尺度异质性。在本文中，我们首先通过卡方均匀性检验验证了这种尺度异质性，表明矩阵内尺度不平衡在Transformer各层中普遍存在，并且坐标白化可以有效纠正这一问题。受此发现启发，我们提出Zeta，一种双重白化优化器，它按照严格的顺序管线的顺序先后进行坐标白化和谱白化。这种顺序并非可调的选择，而是源于数学上的依赖关系：坐标白化建立了谱白化可靠运行所需的统计各向同性。我们进一步证明，相较于纯谱方法，这种双重管线通过改善输入的条件数，严格降低了正交化误差。实验上，Zeta在语言建模（0.6B至8B参数）、混合专家架构和视觉任务上匹配或超越了强基线，表明在正交化前解决尺度不平衡问题能够加快收敛并获得更好的泛化性能。代码已开源在https://gitcode.com/kevin259/MindSpeed。

</details>


### [140] [DRIVE: Distributional and Retrieval-Augmented Bidding with Value Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14192)
*Miduo Cui,Haochen Wang,Shangqin Mao,Xun Yang,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Xuri Ge,Ying Zhou,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIVE是一个基于Transformer的统一框架，通过将候选动作生成与决策解耦，结合分布性动作建模、检索增强的候选生成和基于价值的评估，解决离线自动出价中多策略坍缩和长尾流量不可靠的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于离线强化学习和Transformer序列建模的出价策略存在两个主要问题：一是单峰且纯参数化的模型将多个有效出价策略坍缩为次优的平均动作；二是在稀疏或长尾流量场景下表现不可靠。

Method: DRIVE框架包含三个核心组件：1）分布式动作建模（Distributional Action Modeling）；2）基于检索增强的高质量历史决策候选生成（Retrieval-Augmented Candidate Generation）；3）基于价值的评估（Value-based Evaluation），在推理时选择最优出价动作。

Result: 在AuctionNet和额外的离线强化学习基准测试上的大量实验表明，DRIVE持续提升了出价性能，并且在多种基于Transformer的方法上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将候选动作生成与决策过程解耦，DRIVE有效克服了传统方法的多策略坍缩问题，在离线自动出价任务上实现了更优且更可靠的性能。

Original Abstract: Auto-bidding is a core component of real-time advertising systems, where decisions must optimize long-term performance under budget and cost constraints, while online exploration is prohibitively risky. Offline reinforcement learning and, more recently, Transformer-based sequence modeling have shown promise for learning bidding policies from logged data, but their unimodal and purely parametric formulations often collapse multiple effective bidding strategies into suboptimal averaged actions and perform unreliably under sparse or long-tail traffic. To mitigate these limitations, we propose DRIVE (Distributional and Retrieval-Augmented Bidding with Value Evaluation), a unified Transformer-based framework that decouples candidate action generation from decision making for offline auto-bidding. DRIVE combines distributional action modeling, retrieval-augmented candidate generation from high-quality historical decisions, and value-based evaluation to select the most promising bid at inference time. Extensive experiments on AuctionNet and additional offline reinforcement learning benchmarks demonstrate that DRIVE consistently improves bidding performance and generalizes well across multiple Transformer-based methods.

中文摘要翻译: 自动出价是实时广告系统的核心组成部分，其决策必须在预算和成本约束下优化长期性能，而在线探索风险极高。离线强化学习以及近期的基于Transformer的序列建模，在从日志数据中学习出价策略方面展现出前景，但其单峰且纯参数化的形式往往将多个有效的出价策略坍缩到次优的平均动作中，并在稀疏或长尾流量下表现不可靠。为缓解这些局限，我们提出了DRIVE（基于分布性与检索增强的出价与价值评估），一个统一的基于Transformer的框架，将候选动作生成与决策制定解耦，用于离线自动出价。DRIVE结合了分布式动作建模、基于高质量历史决策的检索增强候选生成，以及基于价值的评估，在推理时选择最有前景的出价。在AuctionNet和额外离线强化学习基准测试上的大量实验证明，DRIVE持续提升了出价性能，并在多种基于Transformer的方法上具有良好的泛化能力。

</details>


### [141] [Structured Noise Adaptation for Sequential Bayesian Filtering with Embedded Latent Transfer Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14195)
*Naichang Ke,Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ELTO的贝叶斯滤波方法，通过结构化噪声参数化实现噪声自适应，提升了非平稳过程中动态状态估计的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于ELTO的卡尔曼滤波器使用简化的噪声模型，无法动态适应非平稳过程，导致在时变环境中的估计性能受限。

Method: 引入一种新的结构化参数化方法来构建滤波器的噪声模型，将数据驱动学习的最优时不变噪声模型与动态参数自适应相结合，实现结构化噪声自适应。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的结构化噪声自适应方法在含噪、时变的环境中显著提升了滤波器的动态状态估计性能。

Conclusion: 结构化噪声自适应能够有效提升ELTO贝叶斯滤波器在非平稳过程中的状态估计能力。

Original Abstract: Kalman filters based on the Embedded Latent Transfer Operators (ELTO) emerge as novel statistical tools for sequential state estimation. However, a critical limitation stems from their use of simplified noise models, which fail to dynamically adapt to non-stationary processes. To address this limitation, we introduce an ELTO-based Bayesian filtering approach with a new structured parameterization for the filter's noise model. This parameterization enables structured noise adaptation, which couples the data-driven learning of an optimal time-invariant noise model with dynamic parameter adaptation that responds to changes in dynamics within non-stationary processes. Empirical results show that our structured noise adaptation improves the filter's dynamic state estimation performance in noisy, time-varying environments.

中文摘要翻译: 基于嵌入潜在传递算子（ELTO）的卡尔曼滤波器是一类用于序贯状态估计的新型统计工具。然而，这类滤波器的一个关键局限性在于其使用了简化的噪声模型，无法动态适应非平稳过程。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于ELTO的贝叶斯滤波方法，并引入了一种新的滤波器噪声模型结构化参数化方案。该参数化实现了结构化噪声自适应，将数据驱动学习的最优时不变噪声模型与响应该非平稳过程中动态变化的参数自适应相结合。实验结果表明，我们的结构化噪声自适应方法在含噪、时变的环境中提升了滤波器的动态状态估计性能。

</details>


### [142] [LapidaryEngine: Fully Conversational Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14215)
*Yusei Ito,Yuta Suzuki,Tomoya Murata,Masaki Adachi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LapidaryEngine是首个支持全对话式晶体生成的模型，通过枢轴表示机制实现文本与晶体结构间的双向翻译，无需配对数据集即可进行迭代式细化和编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到晶体生成模型存在两个关键局限：(i) 输入格式受限，需要高度结构化的描述（如化学式）；(ii) 单向生成，只能从文本到晶体，无法反向进行。这些限制阻碍了全对话式工作流程的实现，也无法对齐用户模糊且不断变化的需求。

Method: 提出枢轴表示（pivot representation）作为中间形式，实现文本与晶体结构之间的双向翻译，无需直接的配对数据集即可解读用户反馈并进行精确的结构控制。

Result: 在绝缘体发现、稳定性优化、成分修改和结构编辑等多种任务上展示了LapidaryEngine的能力，验证了其以交互方式将生成材料与用户意图对齐的效果。

Conclusion: LapidaryEngine通过枢轴表示机制成功实现了首个支持全对话式晶体生成的模型，能够处理自由形式自然语言请求并以对话方式迭代优化，克服了现有模型在输入格式和生成方向上的限制。

Original Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has inspired the vision of generating bespoke crystal materials directly from natural-language instructions, enabling users to design materials through intuitive, conversational interaction. Existing text-to-crystal generative models represent important early steps toward this goal, but they suffer from two critical limitations: (i) restricted input formats that require highly structured descriptions (e.g., chemical formulas), and (ii) one-directional generation, where models can map text to crystal but cannot perform the inverse. These limitations prevent fully conversational workflows and hinder alignment with users' inherently ambiguous and evolving desiderata. We address these challenges with LapidaryEngine, the first model to support fully conversational crystal generation. LapidaryEngine accepts free-form natural-language requests and performs iterative refinement and editing in a dialogue-like manner. The key innovation is a pivot representation, a third, intermediate form that enables bidirectional translation between text and crystal structures despite the absence of direct paired datasets. Leveraging this pivot allows robust interpretation of user feedback and precise structural control. We demonstrate LapidaryEngine across diverse tasks, including insulator discovery, stability optimization, compositional modification, and structural editing, showcasing its ability to align generated materials with user intent in an interactive manner.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）的出现激发了直接从自然语言指令生成定制晶体材料的愿景，使用户能够通过直观的对话式交互来设计材料。现有的文本到晶体生成模型虽然代表了实现这一目标的重要早期步骤，但存在两个关键局限：(i) 输入格式受限，需要高度结构化的描述（如化学式）；(ii) 单向生成，模型只能从文本映射到晶体，无法执行反向过程。这些限制阻碍了全对话式工作流程的实现，也无法与用户固有模糊且不断变化的需求对齐。我们通过LapidaryEngine解决了这些挑战，这是首个支持全对话式晶体生成的模型。LapidaryEngine接受自由形式自然语言请求，并以对话方式执行迭代细化和编辑。其关键创新在于一种枢轴表示（pivot representation），这是一种中间形式，能够在缺乏直接配对数据集的情况下实现文本与晶体结构之间的双向翻译。利用这种枢轴表示，模型能够稳健地解读用户反馈并进行精确的结构控制。我们在多种任务上展示了LapidaryEngine的能力，包括绝缘体发现、稳定性优化、成分修改和结构编辑，证明了其以交互方式将生成材料与用户意图对齐的能力。

</details>


### [143] [Curvature-Informed Potential Energy Surface for Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14217)
*Peng-Fei Sun,Chuan-Xian Ren,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种曲率信息势能面（CPES）图神经网络，通过物理启发的曲率表示建模分子柔性，结合动态与静态特征预测蛋白质-配体结合亲和力，在多个基准上取得更优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖单一结合构象的静态相互作用几何，忽略了分子柔性和结合诱导的构象变化，限制了预测精度和物理可解释性。

Method: 1. 从势能面Hessian矩阵导出曲率谱描述子，定义局部主曲率；2. 使用谱交叉注意力比较未结合配体/蛋白与结合复合物，捕获结合诱导的构象动力学变化；3. 通过几何感知消息传递、软聚类和双向交叉注意力学习静态层次化相互作用表示；4. 融合曲率感知的动态表示与静态相互作用表示进行亲和力回归。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，CPES取得了更优的预测性能，并具备物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 结合物理启发的曲率信息与几何深度学习可以有效提升蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测的准确性和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Accurate prediction of protein-ligand binding affinity is essential for structure-based drug discovery. Recent geometric deep learning methods have achieved promising performance by representing protein-ligand complexes as three-dimensional graphs. However, most existing approaches mainly rely on static interaction geometry from a single bound conformation, while neglecting molecular flexibility and binding-induced conformational changes. To address this limitation, we propose a curvature-informed potential energy surface (CPES) graph neural network for protein-ligand binding affinity prediction, which incorporates physics-informed curvature representations to model conformational flexibility. CPES first derives curvature spectral descriptors from the Hessian of the potential energy surface evaluated at equilibrium configurations, whose eigenvalues define the local principal curvatures of the potential energy surface. It then uses spectral cross-attention to compare the unbound ligand and protein with the bound complex, thereby capturing binding-induced changes in conformational dynamics. In parallel, hierarchical protein-ligand interaction representations are learned from static structural features through geometry-aware message passing, soft clustering, and bidirectional cross-attention. Finally, CPES fuses the curvature-informed dynamic representations with static interaction representations for affinity regression. Extensive evaluations on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that CPES achieves improved predictive performance and offers physical interpretability.

中文摘要翻译: 准确预测蛋白质-配体结合亲和力对于基于结构的药物发现至关重要。近期几何深度学习方法通过将蛋白质-配体复合物表示为三维图取得了有前景的性能。然而，现有方法大多依赖单一结合构象的静态相互作用几何，忽视了分子柔性和结合诱导的构象变化。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种曲率信息势能面（CPES）图神经网络用于蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测，该方法引入物理启发的曲率表示来建模构象柔性。CPES首先从平衡构型下的势能面Hessian矩阵导出曲率谱描述子，其特征值定义了势能面的局部主曲率。然后，它使用谱交叉注意力比较未结合配体和蛋白与结合复合物，从而捕获结合诱导的构象动力学变化。同时，通过几何感知消息传递、软聚类和双向交叉注意力从静态结构特征中学习层次化的蛋白质-配体相互作用表示。最后，CPES融合曲率感知的动态表示与静态相互作用表示进行亲和力回归。在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，CPES取得了更优的预测性能，并具备物理可解释性。

</details>


### [144] [Learning the Context of Errors: Black-Box Online Adaptation of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14222)
*Xilin Dai,Yiding Liu,Hongjie Xia,Yifan Hu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列基础模型（TSFM）在黑盒API服务场景下的在线自适应问题，提出ORCA方法，通过建模基础模型的预测误差与输入输出之间的条件依赖关系，仅使用黑盒访问即可实现高效的在线自适应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线自适应方法大多依赖白盒访问（参数微调或梯度反向传播），这与未来TSFM作为商业化闭源API服务的范式不匹配。因此需要探索黑盒在线自适应方法，回答"在黑盒在线自适应中应该学习什么"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出ORCA（Online Residual Contextual Adaptation）方法，其核心洞察是基础模型的预测误差同时依赖于模型输入和输出（即误差的上下文信息），通过构建误差条件模型来实现无需白盒访问的在线自适应。

Result: 在5个最先进的TSFM和8个数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了ORCA方法的有效性。通过消融研究定量分析了不同适配器学习假设在黑盒在线自适应中对最终性能的影响。

Conclusion: ORCA方法有效解决了TSFM在闭源API场景下的黑盒在线自适应问题，验证了预测误差条件于输入输出上下文的洞察，为未来TSFM的商业化部署提供了可行的自适应方案。

Original Abstract: The rapid evolution of Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) has advanced zero-shot forecasting across diverse domains. Inspired by the current form of Large Language Models, future TSFMs may be offered as commercialized, closed-source API services. However, many existing online adaptation methods still rely on white-box access for parameter fine-tuning or gradient backpropagation. This paradigm mismatch raises a question: In black-box online adaptation for TSFMs, what should we learn? We answer this with an insight: the predictive errors of the base model are conditioned on both the input and output of the base model (i.e., the context of errors). To validate this insight, we propose ORCA (Online Residual Contextual Adaptation). We conduct extensive experiments across 5 state-of-the-art TSFMs and 8 datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, through ablation studies, we quantitatively analyze the impact of different adapter learning hypotheses on the final adaptation performance in black-box online adaptation. Code available at https://github.com/Fifthky/ORCA.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的快速发展推动了跨领域零样本预测的进步。受当前大语言模型形态的启发，未来的TSFM可能以商业化闭源API服务的形式提供。然而，许多现有的在线自适应方法仍依赖白盒访问来进行参数微调或梯度反向传播。这种范式不匹配引发了一个问题：在TSFM的黑盒在线自适应中，我们应该学习什么？我们的答案是：基础模型的预测误差同时取决于基础模型的输入和输出（即误差的上下文）。为验证这一洞察，我们提出了ORCA（在线残差上下文自适应）。我们在5个最先进的TSFM和8个数据集上进行了广泛实验，证明了我们方法的有效性。此外，通过消融研究，我们定量分析了不同适配器学习假设对黑盒在线自适应最终性能的影响。代码已开源：https://github.com/Fifthky/ORCA。

</details>


### [145] [Implicit Variational Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14235)
*Jian Xu,Shigui Li,Wei Chen,Jiacheng Li,Zhiqi Lin,Delu Zeng,Xinghao Ding,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐式变分拒绝采样（IVRS）方法，将隐式分布与拒绝采样相结合，通过神经网络构建隐式提议分布并使用判别器网络估计密度比来改进后验近似，同时引入隐式重采样证据下界（IR-ELBO）作为更紧的变分下界指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统变分推断依赖平均场分解，难以捕捉真实后验的复杂性；近期虽有用神经网络建模隐式分布的方法，但网络架构的实际限制仍会导致近似误差。

Method: 使用神经网络构建隐式提议分布，通过拒绝采样结合判别器网络估计隐式提议分布与真实后验之间的密度比，从而优化后验近似；同时提出隐式重采样证据下界（IR-ELBO）作为衡量重采样分布质量的指标。

Result: 实验结果表明所提方法优于传统变分推断技术。

Conclusion: IVRS方法通过融合隐式分布与拒绝采样，能有效提升后验近似的精度，为贝叶斯推断提供了更灵活的框架。

Original Abstract: Variational Inference (VI) is a fundamental inference technique in Bayesian machine learning for approximating complex posterior distributions. Traditional VI often relies on the mean-field factorization, which can inadequately capture true posterior complexity. Recent advancements have leveraged neural networks to model implicit distributions, offering increased flexibility. However, the practical constraints of neural network architectures still produces inaccuracies. In this paper, we propose a method called Implicit Variational Rejection Sampling (IVRS), which integrates implicit distributions with rejection sampling to improve the posterior approximation. Our method uses neural networks to construct implicit proposal distributions, and rejection sampling with a discriminator network that estimates the density ratio between the implicit proposal and the true posterior for refining the approximation. Towards this end, we introduce the Implicit Resampling Evidence Lower Bound (IR-ELBO) as a metric to characterize the resampled distribution's quality and derive a tighter variational lower bound. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms traditional variational inference techniques.

中文摘要翻译: 变分推断是贝叶斯机器学习中用于近似复杂后验分布的基本推断技术。传统的变分推断通常依赖于平均场分解，这难以充分捕捉真实后验的复杂性。近期的进展利用神经网络建模隐式分布，提供了更高的灵活性。然而，神经网络架构的实际限制仍然会产生近似误差。在本文中，我们提出了一种称为隐式变分拒绝采样（IVRS）的方法，该方法将隐式分布与拒绝采样相结合，以改进后验近似。我们的方法使用神经网络构建隐式提议分布，并通过带有判别器网络的拒绝采样来优化近似，该判别器网络估计隐式提议分布与真实后验之间的密度比。为此，我们引入了隐式重采样证据下界（IR-ELBO）作为衡量重采样分布质量的指标，并推导出更紧的变分下界。实验结果表明，我们的方法优于传统的变分推断技术。

</details>


### [146] [Where Black-box Drug-Target Interaction Prediction Models Look: Cross-Method Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14245)
*Ali Vefghi,Zahed Rahmati,Mohammad Akbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对BridgeDPI架构进行了可解释性审计，结合多种梯度归因方法和特征消融分析，揭示了药物-靶标相互作用预测模型中存在的模态主导、填充伪影和化学一致性模式等问题，表明可解释性作为模型批评工具可为计算药物发现提供可验证假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前药物-靶标相互作用（DTI）和亲和力（DTA）预测模型在基准测试中表现优异，但其内部使用的序列、指纹和图神经网络特征往往不透明，缺乏对模型决策机制的系统性可解释性分析。

Method: 对BridgeDPI架构进行可解释性审计，结合五种梯度归因方法（积分梯度、显著性图、逐层相关性传播、SmoothGrad和SmoothGrad-IG）与特征级遮挡消融，并采用交集共识策略减少单一解释器偏差。在三个数据集（Gao、Human和C.elegans）上分析原始输入、桥接相似性支架和图卷积层的敏感性与符号效应，包括边级敏感性和目标边移除。

Result: 可解释性作为模型批评工具揭示了：模态主导现象、填充和特殊标记伪影、跨层数据依赖的协同与抑制效应，以及方法一致时化学上合理的片段和组成模式。

Conclusion: 这些分析不能替代结构或实验真值，但可为计算药物发现流水线中的下游验证提供可测试假设。将现代可解释AI应用于DTI/DTA模型仍处于早期阶段，但已能帮助研究者将预测与药物侧和目标侧表征联系起来，并优先安排外部验证。

Original Abstract: Drug-target interaction (DTI) and affinity (DTA) predictors increasingly achieve strong benchmark scores, yet their internal use of sequence, fingerprint, and graph features often remains opaque. We present an interpretability audit of BridgeDPI architecture on three different datasets including Gao, Human, and C.elegans. This study combines gradient-based attributions -- integrated gradients, saliency, layer-wise relevance propagation, SmoothGrad, and SmoothGrad-IG -- with feature-wise occlusion ablation and strict intersection consensus across methods to reduce single-explainer bias. We summarize sensitivity and signed effects at raw inputs, at the bridge similarity scaffold, and through the graph convolution, including edge-level sensitivities and targeted edge removals. The results show that explainability is most informative when treated as model criticism: it reveals modality dominance, padding and special-token artifacts, dataset-dependent cooperative versus suppressive effects across layers, and chemistry-consistent fragment and composition motifs where methods agree. These analyses do not substitute for structural or experimental ground truth, yet they can provide testable hypotheses for downstream validation in computational drug discovery pipelines. More broadly, applying modern XAI to contemporary DTI/DTA models is still an early pass over the rich structure implicit in trained weights and data -- yet even this first layer of scrutiny already helps researchers relate predictions to drug- and target-side representations and to prioritize external validation.

中文摘要翻译: 药物-靶标相互作用（DTI）和亲和力（DTA）预测器在基准测试中日益取得优异成绩，但其内部使用的序列、指纹和图神经网络特征往往仍不透明。本文对BridgeDPI架构在三个不同数据集（包括Gao、Human和C.elegans）上进行了可解释性审计。本研究将基于梯度的归因方法——积分梯度、显著性图、逐层相关性传播、SmoothGrad和SmoothGrad-IG——与特征级遮挡消融以及跨方法的严格交集共识相结合，以减少单一解释器偏差。我们总结了原始输入、桥接相似性支架以及图卷积层中（包括边级敏感性和目标边移除）的敏感性和符号效应。结果表明，当将可解释性作为模型批评工具处理时最具信息量：它揭示了模态主导性、填充和特殊标记伪影、跨层的数据依赖协同与抑制效应，以及在方法一致时化学上合理的片段和组成模式。这些分析不能替代结构或实验真值，但可以为计算药物发现流水线中的下游验证提供可测试假设。更广泛地说，将现代可解释AI应用于当代DTI/DTA模型仍是对训练权重和数据中隐含的丰富结构的初步探索——但即使是这第一层 scrutiny 已经能够帮助研究者将预测与药物侧和目标侧表征联系起来，并优先安排外部验证。

</details>


### [147] [Beyond a Single Explanation of the Adam--SGD Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14259)
*Chenxiang Zhang,Rustem Islamov,Enea Monzio Compagnoni,Jun Pang,Aurelien Lucchi,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过跨视觉、语言、基因组学和图任务的受控实证研究，发现Adam与SGD之间的性能差距并非由单一因素决定，而是源于数据与架构的非平凡交互。关键发现包括：存在一个“交叉批大小”，在该批大小下优势从SGD转向Adam；理论模型能够捕捉这一批大小依赖的交叉现象。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有研究提出了多个可能解释Adam与SGD性能差距的因素（如数据特性、架构设计、优化属性），但这些因素往往被孤立研究，其相对重要性尚不明确。本文旨在通过系统性对照实验，厘清这些因素的交互作用，并揭示差距的根源。

Method: 本文采用受控实证研究方法，在视觉、语言、基因组学和图任务上，涵盖现代和经典架构，通过精心设计的训练设置，系统比较Adam和SGD在不同条件下的表现。同时提出了一个理论模型来解释批大小依赖的交叉现象。

Result: 1. Adam的优势在均匀词汇分布下持续存在，但在重尾分布下几乎消失；2. 在softmax注意力模型中，优势反转，SGD表现更好；3. 软架构修改（如ReLU替换为GeLU）会扩大Adam优势；4. 存在一个“交叉批大小”，在该批大小下优势从SGD转向Adam；5. 理论模型能够准确预测这一批大小依赖的交叉现象。

Conclusion: Adam-SGD差距源于数据和架构的非平凡交互，而非单一因素。交叉批大小的发现为调和现有多个假说提供了统一视角，并为不同领域的实践选择提供了指导。

Original Abstract: Prior work has identified several factors that can contribute to the performance gap between Adam and SGD, spanning data aspects, architecture design, and optimization properties. Yet these explanations are often studied in isolation, leaving their relative importance unclear. In this work, we revisit these hypotheses through a controlled empirical study across vision, language, genomics, and graph tasks, spanning modern and classical architectures, and carefully designed training setups. Our results suggest that no single factor consistently explains the Adam--SGD gap. For instance, the Adam advantage can (1) persist under a uniform vocabulary distribution yet nearly disappear under a heavy-tailed one; (2) reverse in favor of SGD in softmax-attention models; and (3) become larger under soft architectural modifications, e.g., when ReLU is replaced by a GeLU nonlinearity. This suggests that the gap arises from nontrivial data and architecture interactions, rather than from a single common factor. Yet, we observe a pattern across our settings: a \emph{crossover batch size} at which the relative advantage shifts from SGD to Adam as the batch size scales. These empirical results are captured by our theoretical gap model, which predicts this batch-size-dependent crossover. Our perspective helps reconcile several existing hypotheses while offering practical insights across domains.

中文摘要翻译: 已有研究识别出多个可能导致Adam与SGD之间性能差距的因素，涵盖数据特性、架构设计和优化属性等方面。然而，这些解释往往被孤立研究，其相对重要性尚不明确。在本工作中，我们通过跨视觉、语言、基因组学和图任务的受控实证研究，涵盖现代与经典架构，并采用精心设计的训练设置，重新审视这些假说。我们的结果表明，没有任何单一因素能够一致地解释Adam与SGD之间的差距。例如，Adam的优势（1）在均匀词汇分布下持续存在，但在重尾分布下几乎消失；（2）在softmax注意力模型中反转，SGD表现更优；（3）在软架构修改（例如ReLU替换为GeLU非线性激活）下变得更大。这表明差距源于数据与架构之间非平凡的交互，而非某个单一因素。然而，我们在所有设置中观察到一个模式：存在一个“交叉批大小”，在该批大小下，相对优势随批大小缩放从SGD转向Adam。这些实证结果被我们的理论差距模型所捕捉，该模型预测了这种批大小依赖的交叉现象。我们的视角有助于调和现有多个假说，同时为跨领域的实际应用提供洞见。

</details>


### [148] [DIFF-ERO: A Conformance-Aware Loss for Deep Learning in Process Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14283)
*Johannes De Smedt,Jari Peeperkorn,Artem Polyvyanyy,Jochen De Weerdt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFF-ERO，一种可微的一致性感知损失函数，通过将控制流信息以可微形式引入训练过程，使深度学习模型在流程预测任务中不仅提高局部预测精度，还能学习到全局过程模型结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准损失函数（如交叉熵）仅优化局部下一步预测似然，隐式捕捉控制流结构，导致模型在令牌级准确率高但全局行为不精确的问题。

Method: 提出DIFF-ERO损失函数，该函数是熵基随机一致性的可微形式，通过构建批次级随机转移矩阵和软边归属，使得结构精确率和召回率信号可直接用于反向传播；该损失与模型无关，可应用于任何以随机转移为最终表示的模型。

Result: 在多个基准上，DIFF-ERO在结构至关重要的任务上提升了预测性能，在其他任务上保持同等水平；同时，学习到的随机自动机收敛到结构真实值，表明网络内化了过程模型结构。

Conclusion: DIFF-ERO作为一种一致性感知损失函数，能够有效提升深度学习模型在流程分析中的结构感知能力，实现局部预测与全局结构学习的统一。

Original Abstract: Deep learning has driven many recent advances in process analytics, especially for predictive and prescriptive monitoring. However, standard objectives such as cross-entropy optimize local next-step likelihoods and only implicitly capture control-flow structure. As a result, models can achieve high token-level accuracy while permitting imprecise global behaviour. We introduce DIFF-ERO, a conformance-aware loss function for deep learning models on process data. DIFF-ERO is a differentiable formulation of entropy-based stochastic conformance that incorporates control-flow information during training. Our approach constructs batch-level stochastic transition matrices with soft edge memberships, allowing structural precision and recall signals to directly inform backpropagation. The loss is model-agnostic and can be applied whenever the final representation parametrizes stochastic transitions. We instantiate DIFF-ERO in transformer encoder-decoder pipelines for next-activity prediction and use it jointly with cross-entropy to analyse its theoretical components with respect to convergence. Across benchmarks comparing other loss functions and targets, DIFF-ERO shows improved predictive performance where structure matters most while maintaining parity elsewhere. At the same time, the learned stochastic automaton converges towards the structural ground truth, indicating that the network internalizes process model structure.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习推动了过程分析领域的诸多进展，尤其是在预测性和规范性监控方面。然而，标准目标函数（如交叉熵）仅优化局部下一步似然，隐式地捕捉控制流结构。这导致模型在实现高令牌级准确率的同时，可能产生不精确的全局行为。本文提出DIFF-ERO，一种用于过程数据深度学习模型的一致性感知损失函数。DIFF-ERO是基于熵的随机一致性的可微形式，在训练过程中纳入控制流信息。我们的方法通过软边归属构建批次级随机转移矩阵，使得结构精确率和召回率信号能够直接指导反向传播。该损失函数与模型无关，可应用于任何最终表示参数化随机转移的模型。我们将DIFF-ERO实例化到用于下一活动预测的Transformer编码器-解码器流水线中，并将其与交叉熵联合使用，从收敛性角度分析其理论组成。在多个基准上，与其他损失函数和目标进行比较，DIFF-ERO在结构至关重要的任务上展现出更优的预测性能，而在其他任务上保持同等水平。同时，学习到的随机自动机向结构真实值收敛，表明网络内化了过程模型结构。

</details>


### [149] [Hierarchical ODE: Learning Continuous-Time Physical Prototypes for Early Link Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14284)
*Jiaen Lv,Leran Qi,Shaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化常微分方程聚类网络（Hierarchical-ODE），利用神经常微分方程对潜在状态演化进行连续建模，并采用自适应层次机制自主确定原型数量，在不规则采样时间序列的早期链路故障检测任务中有效提取物理原型，实现鲁棒故障检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统离散架构无法解耦随机噪声与连续动态，且刚性闭集假设无法捕获未见过的多样性，导致时间序列原型学习面临观测模糊性的根本挑战。

Method: 提出层次化常微分方程聚类网络，利用神经常微分方程将潜在状态演化建模为连续积分曲线，通过强制时间连续性将平滑特征趋势与随机噪声有效分离；同时设计自适应层次机制，无需刚性先验约束即可自主确定原型数量。

Result: 在不规则采样时间序列的早期链路故障检测任务上验证了方法有效性，所提方法能有效提取潜在物理原型，实现鲁棒故障检测。

Conclusion: 该方法通过连续建模与自适应层次机制解决了观测模糊性和闭集假设局限，在时间序列原型学习中展现出优越性能。

Original Abstract: Time series prototype learning is fundamentally challenged by observational ambiguity. Discrete architectures fail to resolve this, as they lack the capacity to decouple stochastic noise from continuous dynamics. Furthermore, rigid closed-set assumptions fail to capture unseen diversity. To address these limitations, we propose a hierarchical ordinary differential equation clustering network, which utilizes neural ordinary differential equation to model latent state evolution as a continuous integral curve. This formulation enforces temporal continuity to effectively disentangle smooth feature trends from stochastic noise, while our adaptive hierarchical mechanism autonomously determines the appropriate number of prototypes without rigid prior constraints. Validated on the early link failure detection task with irregularly sampled time series, the proposed method effectively extracts underlying physical prototypes, thereby enabling robust failure detection. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJ-LNN/Hierarchical-ODE.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列原型学习从根本上受到观测模糊性的挑战。离散架构无法解决这一问题，因为它们缺乏将随机噪声从连续动态中解耦的能力。此外，刚性的闭集假设无法捕获未见过的多样性。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种层次化常微分方程聚类网络，利用神经常微分方程将潜在状态演化建模为一条连续的积分曲线。该公式通过强制时间连续性，有效将平滑特征趋势与随机噪声分离开来，同时我们的自适应层次机制能够在无需刚性先验约束的情况下自主确定合适的原型数量。在不规则采样时间序列的早期链路故障检测任务上进行验证，所提方法有效提取了潜在的物理原型，从而实现了鲁棒的故障检测。我们的代码开源于 https://github.com/NJ-LNN/Hierarchical-ODE。

</details>


### [150] [Riemannian Metric Matching for Scalable Geometric Modeling of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14334)
*Jacob Bamberger,Adam Gosztolai,Pierre Vandergheynst,Michael Bronstein,Iolo Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼度量匹配框架，通过神经网络学习数据的黎曼几何，无需显式构造核函数，实现高效推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高维数据常集中在低维结构附近，但现有的基于图或核的方法在数据集规模和维度增大时扩展性差，计算成本高。

Method: 利用扩散几何中的carré du champ算子，将其公式化为关于数据随机扰动的条件期望，通过神经网络进行样本级训练和常数成本摊销推理，避免显式核构造。

Result: 在精度上与基于k-NN的扩散几何估计器相当或更优，而摊销推理速度最高提升400倍；支持在k-NN失效的高维图像上进行无图几何分析。

Conclusion: 黎曼度量匹配是一种高效、可扩展的黎曼几何学习框架，兼顾精度与速度，适用于高维大规模数据场景。

Original Abstract: High-dimensional datasets often concentrate near low-dimensional structures, but estimating their geometry from samples typically relies on graphs and kernels that scale poorly with dataset size and dimension. We propose Riemannian metric matching: a denoising probabilistic framework for learning the Riemannian geometry of data using neural networks. Specifically, we learn the carré du champ operator, which, using diffusion geometry, gives us access to the Riemannian geometry toolkit for downstream machine learning and statistical tasks. Our key observation is that the carré du champ operator can be formulated as a conditional expectation over random perturbations of the data, which can be exploited for sample-wise training and constant cost, amortized inference without explicit kernel construction. Empirically, metric matching rivals or improves the accuracy of $k$-NN-based diffusion geometry estimators, while enabling amortized inference that is up to $400\times$ faster, and supports graph-free geometric analysis on high-dimensional images where nearest neighbors break down.

中文摘要翻译: 高维数据集通常集中在低维结构附近，但从样本中估计其几何结构通常依赖于图和核方法，这些方法在数据集规模和维度增大时扩展性很差。我们提出黎曼度量匹配：一个基于去噪概率框架的、利用神经网络学习数据黎曼几何的方法。具体来说，我们学习carré du champ算子，借助扩散几何，该算子为后续的机器学习与统计任务提供了黎曼几何工具包。我们的关键发现是，carré du champ算子可以公式化为关于数据随机扰动的条件期望，这可用于样本级训练和常数成本的摊销推理，无需显式构造核函数。实验表明，黎曼度量匹配在精度上与基于k-NN的扩散几何估计器相当或更优，同时实现了最高400倍的摊销推理加速，并支持在最近邻方法失效的高维图像上进行无图几何分析。

</details>


### [151] [More with LESS -- Local Scene Representations for Tactile Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14344)
*Zohar Rimon,Elisei Shafer,Tal Tepper,Daniel Kozin,Alon Malka,Roy Holland,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LESS的物体中心触觉表示方法，利用触觉的局部特性，通过循环编码器网格建模触觉场景并融合状态以重建内部结构图像，在泛化、手持式成像和3D重建方面取得突破。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法依赖于全局非结构化表示和机器人控制的传感，限制了泛化能力和实际应用，缺乏对触觉局部性质的利用。

Method: 提出LESS（Local Encoder for Spatial Sensing）方法，将触觉场景建模为具有局部感受野的循环编码器网格，通过融合编码器状态来重建2D或3D内部结构图像，采用组合式设计实现强泛化。

Result: 在单一内含物幻影上训练的模型能够准确成像具有多个内含物和不同尺寸的物体；支持空间不确定性估计；实现了通过外部位姿追踪和类人触诊数据的手持式触觉成像；扩展到全3D重建。

Conclusion: LESS通过利用触觉的局部本性，以组合式设计实现了强泛化能力，并拓展了触觉成像在手持式应用和3D重建方面的实际可行性。

Original Abstract: Tactile imaging seeks to reconstruct the internal structure of soft objects through touch sensing, with applications in medical diagnosis and robotic manipulation. Recent self-supervised learning approaches have shown promising results, but rely on global, unstructured representations and robot-controlled sensing, limiting generalization and practical use. We propose Local Encoder for Spatial Sensing (LESS), an object-centric tactile representation that exploits the local nature of touch. The tactile scene is modeled as a grid of recurrent encoders with local receptive fields, whose states are fused to reconstruct 2D or 3D images of internal structure. This compositional design enables strong generalization: models trained on single-inclusion phantoms accurately image objects with multiple inclusions and varying sizes. The local structure further supports spatial uncertainty estimation. In addition, we enable hand-held tactile imaging via external pose tracking and human-like palpation data, and extend tactile imaging to full 3D reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 触觉成像旨在通过触觉传感重建软物体的内部结构，应用于医学诊断和机器人操作。近期的自监督学习方法取得了令人鼓舞的成果，但这些方法依赖于全局非结构化表示和机器人控制的传感，限制了泛化能力和实际应用。我们提出了局部空间传感编码器（LESS），这是一种以物体为中心的触觉表示方法，利用了触觉的局部本性。触觉场景被建模为一个具有局部感受野的循环编码器网格，其状态被融合以重建内部结构的2D或3D图像。这种组合式设计实现了强泛化能力：在单一内含物幻影上训练的模型能够准确成像具有多个内含物和不同尺寸的物体。局部结构进一步支持空间不确定性估计。此外，我们通过外部位姿追踪和类人触诊数据实现了手持式触觉成像，并将触觉成像扩展到全3D重建。

</details>


### [152] [Squeeze-Release: Iterative Pruning with Exact Structural Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14346)
*Roman Denkin,Ida Akerholm,Prashant Singh,Ida-Maria Sintorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Squeeze-Release循环，通过结构重写（最小化）将掩码剪枝网络转换为更小的稠密网络，并引入CompensatedLayerNorm实现通道缩减，在全连接和现代CNN上分别实现39倍和14.8倍压缩，且可扩展到Transformer架构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准非结构化剪枝虽然产生稀疏张量，但张量形状不变，部署模型并未真正缩小。现有方法无法有效利用剪枝后零位带来的容量冗余，且难以在带有LayerNorm的残差流上实现通道缩减。

Method: 提出最小化（minimization）——一种精确的结构重写方法，将掩码网络转换为更小的稠密网络，前向函数仅存在浮点舍入误差。在此基础上设计Squeeze-Release循环：剪枝→最小化→释放（将紧凑张量中的精确零位重新初始化为小校准噪声，使闲置容量变为可训练参数），反复迭代以发现单次剪枝无法触及的结构冗余。同时提出CompensatedLayerNorm，一种保持函数等价性的LayerNorm替代方案，将最小化扩展到带有LayerNorm的残差流通道缩减。

Result: 在全连接网络上将可部署模型压缩至未剪枝模型的39倍，在现代CNN（ConvNeXt-Tiny）上压缩14.8倍，且精度相当。理论证明该重写方法可扩展到Transformer架构。

Conclusion: Squeeze-Release循环通过迭代剪枝与结构重写，能有效压缩网络至更小的稠密模型，同时保持精度，并可推广到Transformer等现代架构。

Original Abstract: Unstructured pruning produces sparse weight tensors, but the standard implementation keeps tensor shapes unchanged so the deployed model is no smaller than before pruning. We present an exact structural rewrite, which we call minimization, that converts a masked network into a smaller dense network with the same forward function up to floating-point rounding. The Squeeze-Release cycle iterates pruning and minimization with an intermediate release step that re-enables the exact-zero positions inside the compacted tensors as small calibrated noise, turning otherwise wasted capacity back into trainable parameters. Successive cycles use that capacity to find structural redundancy a single pass cannot reach. We additionally introduce CompensatedLayerNorm, a function-preserving replacement for LayerNorm that extends minimization to channel reduction across LayerNorm-equipped residual streams. Squeeze-Release compresses the deployable network to 39x smaller than the unpruned model on a fully-connected model network and 14.8x smaller on modern CNN (ConvNeXt-Tiny), at comparable accuracy. In addition we prove that the rewrite can be extended to transformer architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 非结构化剪枝产生稀疏权重张量，但标准实现保持张量形状不变，导致部署模型并未比剪枝前更小。我们提出一种精确的结构重写方法——称为最小化（minimization），它能够将掩码网络转换为更小的稠密网络，且前向函数仅在浮点舍入误差范围内保持一致。Squeeze-Release循环通过迭代执行剪枝与最小化，并引入中间的释放步骤——将紧凑张量中的精确零位重新初始化为小的校准噪声，从而将原本浪费的容量转化为可训练参数。连续的循环利用该容量发现单次剪枝无法触及的结构冗余。此外，我们提出了CompensatedLayerNorm，一种保持函数等价性的LayerNorm替代方案，将最小化扩展到配备LayerNorm的残差流中的通道缩减。Squeeze-Release在全连接模型网络上将可部署模型压缩至未剪枝模型的39倍，在现代CNN（ConvNeXt-Tiny）上压缩14.8倍，且精度相当。此外，我们证明了该重写方法可以扩展到Transformer架构。

</details>


### [153] [When Language Representations Interact: Separability and Cross-Lingual Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14347)
*Boris Marinov,Angira Sharma,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Philip Torr,Anisoara Calinescu,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果几何分析研究多语言大语言模型中的语言表示，发现语言概念存在稳定的线性表示，且同一语系的语言呈现类似单纯形的层次化几何结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解多语言模型中语言身份如何以因果几何结构进行编码，以及语言之间是否存在可分离的独立因子表示或结构化依赖关系，对于多语言系统的可靠部署和可解释性至关重要。

Method: 对三个多语言大语言模型中的28组双语对比进行因果几何分析，使用协方差调整（因果）内积来检验语言表示的线性可分性和结构化依赖关系。

Result: 语言概念存在稳定的线性表示，在因果内积下高度可分离；同一语系（如日耳曼语系、罗曼语系）的语言呈现单纯形几何结构，表明层次化组织；结构化偏差反映了语言间的相似性。

Conclusion: 因果几何可解释性可扩展至多语言场景；语言表示既具有可分性又保留结构化依赖，这为预测和控制多语言模型中的跨语言影响提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Large language models exhibit strong multilingual capabilities, however, their internal representations are difficult to interpret. Understanding these interactions is important for ensuring reliable behavior in multilingual systems. Recent work has shown that causal-geometric structure can explain how certain concepts are encoded as approximately linear and separable directions, but whether this framework extends to multilingual models, where language identity is correlated and hierarchical, is underexplored. We apply causal-geometric analysis to multilingual LLMs, studying 28 bilingual contrasts across three models, allowing us to analyze when languages behave as approximately independent factors and when structured dependencies persist. We find evidence that language concepts admit stable linear representations that are largely separable under a covariance-adjusted (causal) inner product, with structured deviations reflecting linguistic similarity. Moreover, languages within the same family (such as Germanic or Romance) exhibit a simplex-like geometric structure, suggesting hierarchical organization. These results extend causal-geometric interpretability to multilingual settings and provide insight into how separability and similarity may exist in multilingual LLM representations, motivating interpretability analyses that diagnose when and how structured dependencies between concepts can be anticipated. This has implications for trustworthy deployment, as residual structure between languages may lead to unintended cross-lingual effects when models are monitored or intervened upon.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型展现出强大的多语言能力，然而其内部表示难以解释。理解这些交互对于确保多语言系统的可靠行为至关重要。近期研究表明，因果几何结构可以解释某些概念如何被编码为近似线性且可分离的方向，但该框架是否适用于语言身份具有相关性和层次性的多语言模型，尚待探索。我们将因果几何分析应用于多语言大语言模型，研究了三个模型中的28组双语对比，从而分析语言何时表现为近似独立的因子，何时存在结构化依赖关系。我们发现，语言概念存在稳定的线性表示，在协方差调整（因果）内积下大致可分离，且结构化偏差反映了语言相似性。此外，同一语系（如日耳曼语系或罗曼语系）内的语言呈现出类似单纯形的几何结构，暗示了层次化组织方式。这些结果将因果几何可解释性扩展到了多语言场景，并揭示了多语言大语言模型表示中可分性与相似性可能共存的内在机制，为诊断何时以及如何预期概念间的结构化依赖关系提供了可解释性分析思路。这对可信部署具有重要意义，因为语言间的残余结构可能导致模型在监控或干预时产生非预期的跨语言效应。

</details>


### [154] [Can Deep Neural Networks Improve Compression of Very Large Scientific Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14353)
*Muhannad Alhumaidi,Guozhong Li,Spiros Skiadopoulos,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨将现代机器学习模型（如预训练天气预报基础模型）作为预测器集成到传统有界误差压缩流水线中，发现尽管ML预测器能提升预测精度和重建质量，但由于残差空间结构不利于熵编码，并未提升整体数据集级压缩比。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有有界误差压缩器多采用预测-残差范式，压缩效果高度依赖于预测器的准确性。然而，开发压缩专用的机器学习预测器成本高昂。作者利用气候领域已有高精度预训练天气预报基础模型作为测试平台，探究现代ML模型能否作为科学数据压缩的优越预测器。

Method: 提出一个将空间和时间深度学习模型集成到传统有界误差压缩流水线的框架，支持自回归预测模型并避免误差累积。使用ERA5气候数据（约1.7 TB），评估三种ML预测器（CRA5、GraphCast、Aurora），在相同的量化和熵编码后端下与SZ3.1进行对比。

Result: ML预测器能生成更准确的预测，对高可预测变量可将重建质量提升高达91%，压缩比提升高达9.6倍；但在整体数据集层面，ML预测器并未提升压缩比。原因在于预测精度本身不够：残差的空间结构对熵编码效率起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 仅有预测精度不足以保证压缩性能的提升，残差的空间结构对熵编码效率至关重要。这为未来将ML模型用于科学数据压缩提供了重要指导方向。

Original Abstract: Error-bounded lossy compression is a fundamental technique for managing the rapidly growing volumes of scientific data produced by modern simulations and observational instruments. Most state-of-the-art-compressors follow a prediction-residual paradigm, where compression effectiveness depends on the quality of the predictor: more accurate predictions generate smaller residuals that are easier to compress. This observation raises a question: can modern machine learning models serve as superior predictors for scientific data compression? Answering this question directly is challenging because developing compression-specific ML predictors requires substantial resources. Instead, we leverage the climate domain where highly accurate pretrained weather forecasting foundation models already exist, making them an ideal testbed. We present a framework that integrates spatial and temporal deep learning models into a conventional error-bounded compression pipeline. The framework supports auto-regressive forecasting models and avoids error accumulation. Using ERA5 climate data as a representative large-scale scientific dataset, we evaluate three distinct ML predictors: a VAEformer-based codec (CRA5), a graph neural network forecaster (GraphCast), and a vision-transformer forecaster (Aurora), against the state-of-the-art compressor SZ3.1 under identical quantization and entropy-coding backends. Our evaluation over approximately 1.7 TB of data reveals a surprising result: although ML predictors generate more accurate predictions and can improve reconstruction quality by up to 91% while achieving up to 9.6x higher compression ratios for highly predictable variables, they do not improve overall dataset-level compression ratio. We show that prediction accuracy alone is insufficient: the spatial structure of the resulting residuals plays a decisive role in entropy coding efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 有界误差有损压缩是现代模拟和观测仪器产生的大规模科学数据管理中的一项基础技术。大多数先进的压缩器遵循预测-残差范式，其压缩效果取决于预测器的质量：更准确的预测产生的残差更小，更易于压缩。这一观察引出一个问题：现代机器学习模型能否作为科学数据压缩的优越预测器？直接回答这一问题具有挑战性，因为开发压缩专用的ML预测器需要大量资源。相反，我们利用气候领域——该领域已存在高精度的预训练天气预报基础模型，使其成为理想的测试平台。我们提出了一个将空间和时间深度学习模型集成到传统有界误差压缩流水线中的框架。该框架支持自回归预测模型并避免误差累积。以ERA5气候数据作为代表性大规模科学数据集，我们在相同的量化和熵编码后端下，评估了三种不同的ML预测器：基于VAEformer的编解码器（CRA5）、图神经网络预测器（GraphCast）和视觉变换器预测器（Aurora），与最先进的压缩器SZ3.1进行对比。我们对约1.7 TB数据的评估揭示了一个令人惊讶的结果：尽管ML预测器能生成更准确的预测，并且可将高可预测变量的重建质量提升高达91%，同时实现高达9.6倍更高的压缩比，但它们并未提升整体数据集级的压缩比。研究表明，仅凭预测精度是不够的：所得残差的空间结构对熵编码效率起着决定性作用。

</details>


### [155] [MUFFLe: Efficient Model Update Compression via Generalized Deduplication for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14354)
*Xiaobo Zhao,Daniel E. Lucani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MUFFLe，一种将广义去重（GD）集成到FedAvg流水线中的通信高效联邦学习更新压缩方案，通过去重更新向量中的重复模式实现固定码率、可变计数的压缩，实验表明其在达到目标精度的前提下显著降低了上行通信量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联邦学习适用于边缘环境，但传输模型更新的上行链路成本往往限制了其应用，因此需要一种通信高效的更新压缩方案来降低通信开销。

Method: 将广义去重（Generalized Deduplication, GD）集成到FedAvg流水线中，对更新向量中的重复模式进行去重，实现一种固定码率、可变计数（fixed-rate, variable-count）的压缩方案。

Result: 在IID MNIST数据集、20个客户端的环境下，MUFFLe达到92.93%目标精度仅需38 MB累积上行通信量，而8位量化需要75 MB，Top-k稀疏化需要86 MB，未压缩的FedAvg需要310 MB。

Conclusion: 实验证明了将广义去重应用于通信高效的联邦学习是可行的，MUFFLe在大幅降低通信开销的同时能保持模型精度。

Original Abstract: Federated learning is well suited to edge environments but is often limited by the uplink cost of transmitting model updates. This Work-in-Progress paper presents MUFFLe, a communication-efficient update compression scheme that integrates generalized deduplication (GD) into the FedAvg pipeline. MUFFLe deduplicates repeated patterns across the update vector, yielding a fixed-rate, variable-count compression scheme. Preliminary experiments on IID MNIST with 20 clients show that MUFFLe reaches the target accuracy of $92.93\%$ with 38~MB cumulative uplink communication, compared with 75~MB for 8-bit quantization, 86~MB for Top-$k$ sparsification, and 310~MB for uncompressed FedAvg. These results demonstrate the feasibility of applying GD to communication-efficient federated learning.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习非常适合边缘环境，但通常受限于传输模型更新的上行链路成本。这篇进行中的工作提出了MUFFLe，一种将广义去重（GD）集成到FedAvg流水线中的通信高效更新压缩方案。MUFFLe对更新向量中的重复模式进行去重，实现了一种固定码率、可变计数的压缩方案。在20个客户端上的IID MNIST初步实验表明，MUFFLe达到92.93%的目标精度仅需38 MB累积上行通信量，而8位量化需要75 MB，Top-k稀疏化需要86 MB，未压缩的FedAvg需要310 MB。这些结果证明了将GD应用于通信高效联邦学习的可行性。

</details>


### [156] [SemPiper: Interactive Code Synthesis for Semantic Operators in Machine Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14361)
*Olga Ovcharenko,Luciano Duarte,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SemPipes，一种将声明式、LLM驱动的语义数据算子扩展到ML管道的编程模型，通过SemPiper可视化界面实现可控、可优化且实用的LLM集成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ML管道开发需要大量数据准备、特征工程和异构源集成，过程繁琐且易错；现有LLM虽能辅助编程，但基于聊天的接口对管道行为控制有限，生成的代码难以优化或集成到生产系统。

Method: 提出SemPipes编程模型，支持开发者用自然语言指令声明语义算子，并与标准数据科学库的Python代码无缝结合；在管道训练时基于数据集特征和管道上下文为语义算子合成专用实现，并通过进化搜索过程进行迭代优化；构建SemPiper交互式界面可视化计算图、算子实现及优化轨迹。

Result: 通过三个端到端场景演示，与会者可以修改管道、检查生成的代码，观察语义算子如何被合成并迭代优化，展示了声明式语义算子在可控性、可优化性和实用性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 声明式语义算子能够实现LLM在ML管道开发中可控、可优化且实用的集成。

Original Abstract: Machine learning (ML) pipelines require extensive data preparation, feature engineering, and integration across heterogeneous sources, making them tedious and error-prone to develop. While large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise for assisting programming tasks, chat-based interfaces provide limited control over pipeline behavior and often produce code that is difficult to optimize or integrate into production systems. We demonstrate SemPipes, a novel programming model that extends ML pipelines with declarative, LLM-powered semantic data operators. SemPipes allows developers to specify high-level natural language instructions for data-centric operations, while seamlessly combining these operators with arbitrary Python code from standard data science libraries. For the semantic operators, it synthesizes specialized implementations at pipeline training time, conditioned on dataset characteristics and pipeline context, enabling the flexible yet controlled integration of LLM capabilities. We demonstrate SemPipes through SemPiper, an interactive interface that visualizes computational graphs of the pipelines, synthesized operator implementations, and optimization trajectories produced by an evolutionary search procedure. Attendees can explore three end-to-end scenarios, modify pipelines, inspect generated code, and observe how semantic operators are synthesized and iteratively optimized. The demonstration highlights how declarative semantic operators enable controllable, optimizable, and practical integration of LLMs into ML pipeline development.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习（ML）管道需要大量的数据准备、特征工程以及跨异构源的集成，这使得其开发过程既繁琐又容易出错。尽管大语言模型（LLM）近期在辅助编程任务方面展现出潜力，但基于聊天的接口对管道行为的控制有限，且生成的代码往往难以优化或集成到生产系统中。我们展示了SemPipes，一种新颖的编程模型，它将ML管道扩展为具有声明式、由LLM驱动的语义数据算子。SemPipes允许开发者使用高级自然语言指令来指定以数据为中心的操作，同时将这些算子与来自标准数据科学库的任意Python代码无缝结合。针对语义算子，它在管道训练阶段基于数据集特征和管道上下文合成专用实现，从而实现LLM能力的灵活且可控的集成。我们通过SemPiper——一种交互式界面来展示SemPipes，该界面可视化管道的计算图、合成的算子实现以及由进化搜索过程产生的优化轨迹。与会者可以探索三个端到端场景，修改管道，检查生成的代码，并观察语义算子如何被合成和迭代优化。该演示突显了声明式语义算子如何实现LLM在ML管道开发中可控、可优化且实用的集成。

</details>


### [157] [Be My Tutor: On-Policy Co-Distillation for Mutual LLM Improvement via Peer Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14368)
*Woohyeon Byeon,Jiwon Jeon,Jeonghye Kim,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为OPCoD的在线策略共蒸馏方法，通过两个各有所长的LLM相互教导，实现跨领域的帕累托改进，无需单模型微调或单向蒸馏。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法（如单向知识蒸馏或单模型微调）无法实现跨领域模型的相互增强与帕累托改进，即一个模型在两个领域都提升而不损失原有优势。

Method: OPCoD包含三个核心机制：每个学生的自蒸馏基于自身正确 rollout 和同伴反馈；基于认知（cognizance-based）的门控机制决定何时提供反馈；反馈锚定（feedback anchoring）将反馈扎根于问题本身。

Result: 在Science Q&A任务上，OPCoD在所有评估的领域对和学生模型上均一致优于基线方法，实现了帕累托改进。

Conclusion: 通过在线策略的相互教导和精心设计的反馈机制，两个不同领域的强模型可以实现相互提升，达成帕累托最优改进。

Original Abstract: We study multi-domain LLM training in which two models, each stronger in a different domain, co-evolve by tutoring each other through on-policy feedback. Unlike one-way distillation or single-model fine-tuning, our goal is mutual Pareto improvement: each model improves across domains without losing its original strength. To this end, we propose On-Policy Co-Distillation (OPCoD), where each student's self-distillation is conditioned on its own correct rollout and feedback from its peer. To make feedback exchange effective, OPCoD uses cognizance-based gating to decide when to give feedback and feedback anchoring to ground feedback in the problem. On Science Q\&A tasks, OPCoD consistently outperforms baselines and achieves Pareto improvement across all evaluated domain pairs and students.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了多领域大语言模型训练，其中两个各自在不同领域更强的模型通过在线策略反馈相互教导，实现共同进化。与单向蒸馏或单模型微调不同，我们的目标是互惠的帕累托改进：每个模型在跨领域提升的同时不损失其原始优势。为此，我们提出了在线策略共蒸馏（OPCoD），其中每个学生的自蒸馏以自身的正确展开和来自同伴的反馈为条件。为了使反馈交换有效，OPCoD使用基于认知的门控机制来决定何时提供反馈，以及反馈锚定机制将反馈扎根于问题本身。在科学问答任务上，OPCoD在所有评估的领域对和学生模型上均一致优于基线方法，实现了帕累托改进。

</details>


### [158] [Discovery under Hypothesis Redundancy: A Geometric Theory of Discovery Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14386)
*Li Xia,Baoxun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出搜索压缩假说，认为LLM引导的非局部探索仅在谱压缩、正交逃逸和残差信号对齐三个几何条件同时满足时才能提升科学发现效率，实验证明仅靠新颖性不足，需目标对齐才有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有科学发现系统中，即使假设空间很大，新假设也可能停止提供独立信息。作者希望厘清LLM生成的非局部提议何时真正有助于科学发现，而非仅靠新颖性。

Method: 提出搜索压缩假说，形式化三个几何条件（谱压缩、正交逃逸、残差信号对齐），推导混合系统优势的必要条件，通过受控合成环境、大规模A股因子发现、符号回归基准和表格操作合理性检验等实验验证。

Result: 信号植入和定向vs随机实验表明，仅新颖性不够：随机正交跳扩大覆盖但无预测对齐则无收益。混合增益集中于弱表示但含目标信号的方向，当假设空间接近满秩时增益消失。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM引导的发现从通用新颖搜索转化为判断何时需要定向非局部探索的诊断方法。

Original Abstract: Scientific discovery saturates when new hypotheses cease to provide independent information, even if the nominal hypothesis space remains large. We study hybrid discovery systems that combine structured local search with LLM-generated non-local proposals and pose the Search Compression Hypothesis: non-local exploration helps only when three geometric conditions co-occur: spectral compression, orthogonal escape from the explored span, and residual signal alignment with the target. We formalize these conditions, derive necessary conditions for hybrid advantage, and test the mechanism in controlled synthetic environments, large-scale A-share factor discovery, and symbolic-regression benchmarks; a public tabular operational sanity check tests the associated budget-allocation implication. Signal-planting and directed-versus-random experiments show that novelty alone is insufficient: random orthogonal jumps expand coverage but do not improve yield without predictive alignment. Across compression sweeps, real factor archives, and LLM-SRBench tasks, hybrid gains concentrate in weakly represented but target-bearing directions and vanish as the hypothesis space approaches full rank. The framework turns LLM-guided discovery from generic novelty search into a diagnostic procedure for deciding when directed non-local exploration is warranted.

中文摘要翻译: 当新假设停止提供独立信息时，科学发现达到饱和，即使名义假设空间仍然很大。我们研究了将结构化局部搜索与LLM生成的非局部提议相结合的混合发现系统，并提出搜索压缩假说：非局部探索仅在三个几何条件同时发生时才有帮助——谱压缩、从已探索子空间的正交逃逸，以及残差信号与目标的对齐。我们形式化了这些条件，推导了混合优势的必要条件，并在受控合成环境、大规模A股因子发现和符号回归基准中测试了该机制；一项公开的表格操作合理性检验测试了相关的预算分配含义。信号植入和定向与随机实验表明，仅靠新颖性是不够的：随机正交跳跃扩大了覆盖范围，但如果没有预测对齐则不会提高收益。在压缩扫描、真实因子档案和LLM-SRBench任务中，混合增益集中在弱表示但含有目标信号的方向上，并随着假设空间接近满秩而消失。该框架将LLM引导的发现从通用新颖搜索转变为一种诊断程序，用于判断何时需要进行定向非局部探索。

</details>


### [159] [A Low-Rank Subspace Analysis of LLM Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14388)
*Angira Sharma,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Philip Torr,Anisoara Calinescu,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，分析LLM中行为干预的副作用，发现行为共享内部表征且干预效果不对称，受子空间重叠度和与决策子空间夹角的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 针对LLM的特定行为干预（如拒绝回答、谄媚）常常意外改变其他行为，导致难以设计可靠的安全控制。现有方法缺乏对这类副作用的系统性理解。

Method: 将行为建模为激活空间中的低秩子空间，引入两个几何量：(i)行为子空间之间的重叠度（主角度平均平方余弦）；(ii)各行为子空间与决策子空间的夹角。通过分析干预在不同行为间的传播效应来量化相互作用。

Result: 在多个指令微调模型（7B-70B）和拒绝、越狱、谄媚设置下发现：(1)不同行为共享内部表征，干预会以不对称方式改变其他行为；(2)某些行为作为上游控制点，其干预广泛传播，而另一些则更孤立；(3)子空间重叠越大、源行为子空间离决策子空间越近（夹角越小），干预对其他行为的影响越大。

Conclusion: 行为难以独立修改，干预会通过共享表征和不对称交互传播，这为实现精准行为控制带来了根本性挑战。

Original Abstract: Interventions designed to modify a particular behavior in LLMs, such as refusal or sycophancy, often produce unintended changes in other behaviors. This lack of targeted control makes it difficult to design and implement reliable safety controls. To understand these side-effects, we introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing interacting behaviors in LLMs. We model behaviors as low-rank subspaces in activation space, and study how interventions influence across behaviors. Across multiple instruction-tuned models (7B-70B) and across refusal, jailbreak, and sycophancy settings, we find that different behaviors share internal representations, and intervening on one behavior alters others in asymmetric ways. Some behaviors act as upstream control points whose interventions propagate broadly across other behaviors, while others remain more isolated. We relate these effects to two geometric quantities: (i) the overlap between behavior subspaces, measured as the average squared cosine of principal angles, and (ii) the angle between each behavior subspace and the decision subspace (capturing the model's final decision e.g., refuse vs. comply). Empirically, intervention effects on other behaviors tend to be larger for behavior pairs with higher subspace overlap, and for source behaviors whose subspaces lie closer (smaller angle) to the decision subspace. These findings highlight a challenge for targeted behavior control: behaviors are difficult to modify independently, as interventions can propagate through shared representations and asymmetric interactions.

中文摘要翻译: 旨在修改大语言模型中特定行为（如拒绝回答或谄媚）的干预措施常常会导致其他行为的意外改变。这种缺乏精确控制的问题使得设计和实现可靠的安全控制变得困难。为了理解这些副作用，我们引入了一个诊断框架来分析大语言模型中交互行为的特征。我们将行为建模为激活空间中的低秩子空间，并研究干预如何在不同行为之间产生相互影响。在多个指令微调模型（7B-70B）以及拒绝、越狱和谄媚的场景下，我们发现不同行为共享内部表征，且对某一行为的干预会以不对称的方式改变其他行为。一些行为作为上游控制点，其干预效果会广泛传播到其他行为，而另一些行为则相对孤立。我们将这些效应与两个几何量联系起来：(i)行为子空间之间的重叠度，通过主角度的平均平方余弦来衡量；(ii)每个行为子空间与决策子空间（捕捉模型最终决策，如拒绝与顺从）之间的夹角。实验结果表明，对于子空间重叠度较大的行为对，以及其子空间更接近决策子空间（夹角更小）的源行为，干预对其他行为的影响往往更大。这些发现揭示了精准行为控制面临的一个挑战：行为很难独立修改，因为干预效果会通过共享表征和不对称交互传播。

</details>


### [160] [Running the Gauntlet: Re-evaluating the Capabilities of Agents Beyond Familiar Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14397)
*Mykola Vysotskyi,Runqi Lin,Grzegorz Biziel,Michal Zakrzewski,Sebastian Montagna,Damian Rynczak,Shreyansh Padarha,Kumail Alhamoud,Zihao Fu,William Lugoloobi,Kai Rawal,Hanna Yershova,Xander Davies,Taras Rumezhak,Guohao Li,Fazl Barez,Baoyuan Wu,Arkadiusz Drohomirecki,Yarin Gal,Chris Russell,Christopher Summerfield,Adam Mahdi,Volodymyr Karpiv,Philip Torr,Adel Bibi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GauntletBench 是一个基于 Web 的智能体泛化能力基准测试，聚焦时间感知、图形理解和 3D 推理三类未被充分探索的能力，涵盖五个专业应用领域的 100 个视觉密集型任务。实验表明，即使是顶尖智能体也仅达到 19.1% 的成功率，与非专家人类 80% 以上的成功率形成鲜明对比，揭示了当前智能体在复杂现实场景中的巨大局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准测试通常基于简单任务且仅关注少数能力维度，导致智能体性能饱和，无法探测其真实局限性。因此需要构建涵盖更广泛能力维度和更具挑战性场景的基准，以忠实评估现代智能体的泛化能力。

Method: 构建了 GauntletBench 基准测试，包含：(1) 兼容开源和闭源智能体框架的模块化环境；(2) 五个专业应用（视频编辑器、工作流构建器、3D 建模器、飞行分析器、电路设计器）的受控 Web 应用；(3) 100 个视觉密集型任务（每应用 20 个），聚焦时间感知、图形理解和 3D 推理三类能力；(4) 具备多样化指标的自动化评估引擎。

Result: 当前最先进的智能体在 GauntletBench 上仅达到 19.1% 的成功率，而非专家人类标注者的成功率超过 80%，揭示了当前智能体系统与实现复杂现实场景所需能力之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体系统远未达到人类水平，特别是在时间感知、图形理解和 3D 推理等被忽视的能力以及泛化方面存在显著局限，亟需进一步研究以弥合这一差距。

Original Abstract: As agentic systems continue to evolve and are widely deployed in real-world scenarios, there is a growing demand to faithfully evaluate their capabilities. However, current benchmarks are typically built on popular applications with relatively simple tasks and focus on a narrow set of capabilities while overlooking broader dimensions, resulting in saturated performance on modern agents and failing to probe their limitations. To this end, we introduce GauntletBench, a web-based benchmark for evaluating agent generalisation in challenging scenarios, focusing on three underexplored capabilities (temporal perception, graphical understanding, and 3D reasoning), across five less-covered professional applications (Video Editor, Workflow Builder, 3D Modeller, Flight Analyser, and Circuit Designer), each with 20 vision-intensive tasks (100 in total). Our benchmark provides a modular pipeline that comprises an environment compatible with both open- and closed-source agent frameworks, a controlled web-based application, a well-structured task suite, and an automated evaluation engine with diverse metrics. Contrary to widespread expectations, our empirical results reveal that frontier agentic systems remain far from achieving human-level performance. Even the state-of-the-art agent achieves only a 19.1% success rate on our GauntletBench, highlighting the limitations in these overlooked capabilities and generalisation. By comparison, non-expert human annotators achieve over 80% success on our challenging yet feasible tasks, revealing the substantial gap between current agent capabilities and those required for complex real-world scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 随着智能体系统的不断演进并被广泛部署于现实场景，对其能力进行忠实评估的需求日益增长。然而，当前的基准测试通常建立在任务相对简单的流行应用之上，仅关注少数能力维度而忽视了更广泛的方面，导致现代智能体性能饱和，无法探测其局限性。为此，我们引入了 GauntletBench——一个基于 Web 的基准测试，用于评估智能体在挑战性场景中的泛化能力，聚焦三类未被充分探索的能力（时间感知、图形理解和 3D 推理），涵盖五个覆盖不足的专业应用（视频编辑器、工作流构建器、3D 建模器、飞行分析器和电路设计器），每个应用包含 20 个视觉密集型任务（共 100 个任务）。我们的基准测试提供了一个模块化流水线，包括一个兼容开源和闭源智能体框架的环境、一个受控的基于 Web 的应用、一个结构良好的任务套件，以及一个具备多样化指标的自动化评估引擎。与普遍预期相反，我们的实证结果表明，前沿智能体系统远未达到人类水平的性能。即使是最先进的智能体在 GauntletBench 上也仅达到 19.1% 的成功率，突显了这些被忽视的能力和泛化方面的局限性。相比之下，非专家人类标注者在我们具有挑战性但可行的任务上实现了超过 80% 的成功率，揭示了当前智能体能力与复杂现实场景所需能力之间的巨大差距。

</details>


### [161] [A theoretical model for task routing in mixture-of-expert transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14398)
*Yongli Xiang,Vinoth Nandakumar,Yunzhi Yao,Peike Li,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过离散语言模型（语法模板+键值字典）形式化证明了单层MoE变压器可以通过任务专用专家编码知识，专家规模仅取决于任务内在复杂度，为MoE中的专家专业化现象提供了理论支撑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MoE专家专业化的实证研究缺乏理论解释，且已有理论工作使用无法有效建模自然语言的连续混合模型。本文旨在用离散语言模型从理论上解释MoE变压器中的任务-专家专业化现象。

Method: 通过语法模板和有限键值字典表示结构化知识，形式化证明单层MoE变压器可以通过将查询路由到特定任务的专用专家来编码知识，专家的大小仅取决于任务的内在复杂度（语法模板和事实字典的组合大小）。

Result: 构造性证明表明MoE模型可以实现任务-专家专业化；理论结果为MoE模型中局部化知识回路的实证发现提供了理论支持；通过实验评估了不同MoE损失函数下的模型性能。

Conclusion: 本文从离散模型角度为MoE变压器的专家专业化提供了严格的理论证明，揭示了专家规模与任务复杂度之间的关系，弥合了理论与实证研究之间的差距。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers enable the scaling of transformer models while keeping the inference compute fixed. While task-expert specialization has been observed in empirical studies of frontier MoE transformer models, existing theoretical work analyzes this using continuous mixture models that cannot be used to model natural language effectively. An important open question is to \textit{theoretically explain task-expert specialization in transformer MoE models using discrete models of language}. To address this, we represent structured knowledge via syntactic templates and finite key-value dictionaries, and prove formally that a single-layer MoE transformer can encode knowledge by using experts that specialize in the corresponding tasks. Our construction shows how queries are routed to unique, task-specific experts whose size depends solely on the intrinsic complexity of the given task (i.e. the combined size of its syntactic templates and factual dictionary). Our construction provides a theoretical support for empirical results on localized knowledge circuits in MoE models. We support our theoretical findings with experiments evaluating model performance under varying MoE loss functions.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（MoE）层使得变压器模型能够在保持推理计算量不变的情况下进行扩展。尽管在前沿MoE变压器模型的实证研究中已经观察到任务-专家专业化现象，但现有的理论工作使用连续混合模型进行分析，无法有效建模自然语言。一个重要的开放性问题是：使用离散语言模型从理论上解释MoE变压器模型中的任务-专家专业化现象。为解决这一问题，我们通过语法模板和有限键值字典来表示结构化知识，并形式化证明了单层MoE变压器可以通过使用对应于特定任务的专用专家来编码知识。我们的构造展示了查询如何被路由到唯一的、任务特定的专家，这些专家的大小仅取决于给定任务的内在复杂度（即其语法模板和事实字典的组合大小）。我们的构造为MoE模型中局部化知识回路的实证结果提供了理论支持。我们通过实验评估了不同MoE损失函数下的模型性能，进一步支持了我们的理论发现。

</details>


### [162] [Federated Learning for Feature Generalization with Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14416)
*Dongwon Kim,Donghee Kim,Sung Kuk Shyn,Kwangsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCONST通过自适应调节更新幅度和对全局模型参数施加线性凸约束，解决联邦学习中客户端数据异构导致的局部过拟合和泛化能力差的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端数据的异构性导致局部模型容易过拟合自身数据分布，且可迁移的特征在聚合过程中可能被扭曲，限制了全局模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出FedCONST方法：基于全局模型的参数强度自适应调节更新幅度，防止过度强调已学好的参数同时加强未充分学习的参数；采用线性凸约束确保训练稳定性并保留局部学到的泛化能力；通过梯度信噪比（GSNR）分析验证特征可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Result: GSNR分析验证了FedCONST在增强特征可迁移性和鲁棒性方面的有效性；在多种异构FL环境中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: FedCONST有效对齐局部与全局目标，减轻过拟合，在不同异构联邦学习环境中提升了模型泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Federated learning (FL) often struggles with generalization due to heterogeneous client data. Local models are prone to overfitting their local data distributions, and even transferable features can be distorted during aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose FedCONST, an approach that adaptively modulates update magnitudes based on the parameter strength of the global model. This prevents over-emphasizing well-learned parameters while reinforcing underdeveloped ones. Specifically, FedCONST employs linear convex constraints to ensure training stability and preserve locally learned generalization capabilities during aggregation. A Gradient Signal to Noise Ratio (GSNR) analysis further validates the effectiveness of FedCONST in enhancing feature transferability and robustness. As a result, FedCONST effectively aligns local and global objectives, mitigating overfitting and promoting stronger generalization across diverse FL environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）常因客户端数据的异构性而难以实现良好的泛化。局部模型容易过拟合其本地数据分布，甚至可迁移的特征也会在聚合过程中被扭曲。为解决这些挑战，我们提出FedCONST，一种基于全局模型参数强度自适应调节更新幅度的方法。这能防止过度强调已学好的参数，同时加强未充分学习的参数。具体而言，FedCONST采用线性凸约束来确保训练稳定性，并在聚合过程中保留局部学到的泛化能力。梯度信噪比（GSNR）分析进一步验证了FedCONST在增强特征可迁移性和鲁棒性方面的有效性。因此，FedCONST有效对齐了局部与全局目标，减轻了过拟合，并在多样化的异构FL环境中促进了更强的泛化能力，达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [163] [EM-NeSy: Expectation Maximization for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14463)
*Annegret Seibt,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EM-NeSy，将概率神经符号学习转化为EM算法实例，无需符号组件可微，支持近似推理，并恢复标准梯度下降设置。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经符号（NeSy）模型要求符号组件可微，导致近似推理复杂化，限制了EM算法在NeSy学习中的潜力。

Method: 将概率NeSy学习建模为EM算法：E步通过概率推理计算后验（神经预测符号在标签条件下的后验），M步仅通过神经组件进行梯度下降更新参数，无需符号组件可微或额外修改。

Result: 实验证明EM-NeSy在可扩展性和计算效率上表现优异，在精确推理下可恢复标准端到端梯度NeSy设置，在近似推理下无需额外修改即可自然扩展。

Conclusion: EM-NeSy释放了EM算法在NeSy学习中的全部潜力，无需符号组件可微性要求，支持近似推理，兼具可扩展性和计算效率。

Original Abstract: Neurosymbolic (NeSy) models integrate neural networks and symbolic reasoning for robust and interpretable AI. State-of-the-art NeSy models require that the symbolic component is expressed in a differentiable way, often complicating the use of approximate inference. We propose EM-NeSy which casts probabilistic NeSy learning as an instance of the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. In the expectation step, we compute the posterior over the neurally predicted symbols conditioned on the label via probabilistic inference. In the maximization step, we update the neural parameters based on this posterior using gradient descent only through the neural component. This formulation unlocks the full potential of the EM algorithm for NeSy learning. It allows NeSy to extend naturally to approximate reasoning without any additional modifications or differentiability requirements of the symbolic component. Furthermore, it recovers the standard end-to-end gradient-based NeSy setting under exact inference. Our experimental results demonstrate the scalability and computational efficiency of EM-NeSy.

中文摘要翻译: 神经符号（NeSy）模型融合了神经网络和符号推理，以实现鲁棒且可解释的人工智能。最先进的NeSy模型要求符号组件以可微方式表达，这常常使近似推理的使用复杂化。我们提出了EM-NeSy，它将概率NeSy学习转化为期望最大化（EM）算法的一个实例。在期望步骤中，我们通过概率推理计算在标签条件下神经预测符号的后验。在最大化步骤中，我们基于该后验仅通过神经组件进行梯度下降来更新神经参数。这一公式释放了EM算法在NeSy学习中的全部潜力。它使得NeSy能够自然地扩展到近似推理，而无需对符号组件进行任何额外修改或可微性要求。此外，在精确推理下，它恢复了标准端到端梯度NeSy设置。我们的实验结果证明了EM-NeSy的可扩展性和计算效率。

</details>


### [164] [Recipe-Controlled Decoder Audit for Structural Knowledge-Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14492)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种配方控制解码器审计（RCDA）方法，用于结构化归纳知识图谱补全。通过在同一种训练配方下交换解码器（ComplEx vs DistMult）来分离编码器和解码器对性能的贡献，发现解码器效应在小知识图谱中更为显著，且受配方和数据来源影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有知识图谱补全研究往往将性能提升笼统归因于编码器或训练配方的改进，但缺乏系统方法区分解码器变化带来的影响。作者希望通过设计一种审计协议，在控制训练配方的条件下单独评估解码器的作用，从而更准确地归因性能增益来源。

Method: 提出配方控制解码器审计（RCDA）方法：以ComplEx和DistMult作为受控解码器对，辅以RotatE/TransE定点检查，在7个基准数据集上评估。通过在同一训练配方下交换解码器，对比解码器效应与编码器效应的大小，并特别关注小知识图谱中数据来源和随机种子对解码器选择的影响。

Result: 在5个标准知识图谱上，ComplEx与DistMult的差异不大但一致（MRR提升+0.005至+0.012）；CompGCN类编码器效应则随数据集变化更大。小知识图谱上解码器效应成为主要诊断指标：Kinship上ComplEx稳定优势+0.143 MRR（6种子），UMLS上ComplEx优势+0.022 MRR但早期数据版本结果相反。解码器选择与编码器深度在WN18RR上存在交互，在YAGO3-10上L=0时ComplEx在d=128达到0.6971±0.0048 MRR。

Conclusion: 提出了一个紧凑的审计协议：报告匹配的解码器行、记录小知识图谱的数据来源、在做出编码器层面的结论前先对解码器×深度进行扫描。解码器选择在小知识图谱中应视为对配方和数据来源敏感的，而非固定的数据集最佳选择。

Original Abstract: We present a recipe-controlled decoder audit (RCDA) for structural transductive knowledge-graph completion (KGC). The audit asks a simple reporting question: before attributing gains to an encoder or training recipe, what changes when the decoder is swapped under the same recipe? Using ComplEx and DistMult as the primary controlled pair, with targeted RotatE/TransE spot-checks, we evaluate seven benchmarks. On five standard KGs, ComplEx-vs-DistMult differences are modest but consistent under our recipe (+0.005 to +0.012 MRR), whereas CompGCN-style encoder effects vary more by dataset. On small KGs, decoder effects become the main diagnostic: Kinship shows a stable ComplEx advantage of +0.143 MRR (6 seeds), while UMLS favours ComplEx by +0.022 MRR in a clean 6-seed server rerun but reverses in an earlier provenance variant. We therefore treat small-KG decoder choice as recipe- and provenance-sensitive rather than as a fixed dataset winner. We further show that decoder choice interacts with encoder depth on WN18RR, and that under our recipe L=0 ComplEx on YAGO3-10 reaches 0.6971 +/- 0.0048 MRR at d=128. The result is a compact audit protocol: report matched decoder rows, log small-KG provenance, and sweep decoder x depth before making encoder-level claims.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种用于结构化归纳知识图谱补全（KGC）的配方控制解码器审计（RCDA）方法。该审计提出一个简单的报告问题：在将性能增益归因于编码器或训练配方之前，当在同一配方下交换解码器时，结果会发生什么变化？我们以ComplEx和DistMult作为主要的受控解码器对，辅以针对性的RotatE/TransE定点检查，在七个基准数据集上进行了评估。在五个标准知识图谱上，ComplEx与DistMult的差异虽不大但一致（在我们的配方下MRR提升+0.005至+0.012），而CompGCN风格的编码器效应则因数据集而异。在小知识图谱上，解码器效应成为主要的诊断指标：Kinship显示ComplEx具有稳定的+0.143 MRR优势（6个随机种子），而UMLS在干净的6种子服务器重跑中偏向ComplEx（+0.022 MRR），但在早期数据变体中结果相反。因此，我们将小知识图谱的解码器选择视为对配方和数据来源敏感的，而非固定的数据集最佳选择。我们进一步展示了在WN18RR上解码器选择与编码器深度存在交互，且在我们的配方下，YAGO3-10上L=0的ComplEx在d=128时达到0.6971±0.0048 MRR。最终得到一个紧凑的审计协议：报告匹配的解码器行、记录小知识图谱的数据来源，并在做出编码器层面的声明之前先对解码器×深度进行扫描。

</details>


### [165] [PepALD: Macrocyclic Peptide Generation via Autoregressive Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14510)
*Junming Zhang,Siyu Yi,Wei Ju,Zhonghui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PepALD是一个基于自回归潜在扩散（ALD）的宏环肽从头生成基础模型，通过结构化化学嵌入、上下文条件潜在扩散、R基团感知环闭合和偏好优化，在生成质量和奖励优化方面优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SMILES或HELM字符串生成模型要么在原子级长序列空间中操作，要么将单体视为缺乏化学基础的符号化令牌，难以同时控制非天然单体化学、环拓扑、膜渗透性和靶点结合，因此需要一种能有效结合化学信息与生成能力的宏环肽设计方法。

Method: PepALD模型包含四个关键组件：1) 对HELM单体使用结构化化学嵌入表示；2) 通过化学信息潜在空间中的上下文条件扩散生成每个残基；3) 在自回归生成过程中预测R基团感知的环闭合；4) 使用胜者保护扩散自适应偏好优化（WDPO）将去噪器与亲和力奖励对齐。

Result: 在计算实验中，PepALD在生成质量和奖励优化性能上均优于代表性肽生成基线方法。

Conclusion: PepALD作为一种新颖的宏环肽生成基础模型，通过融合结构化化学嵌入、潜在空间扩散和偏好优化，有效提升了宏环肽从头设计的质量和靶点亲和力优化能力。

Original Abstract: Macrocyclic peptides are promising therapeutic candidates for intracellular targets, but their design requires simultaneous control over non-natural monomer chemistry, ring topology, membrane permeability, and target binding. Existing SMILES- or HELM-string generative models either operate in long atom-level sequence spaces or treat monomers as symbolic tokens with limited chemical grounding. We introduce PepALD, an Autoregressive Latent Diffusion (ALD) foundation model for \textit{de novo} macrocyclic peptide generation. The model represents HELM monomers with structured chemical embeddings, generates each residue through context-conditioned diffusion in chemically informed latent space, predicts R-group-aware ring closures during autoregressive generation, and aligns the denoiser to affinity rewards using winner-protected diffusion-adapted preference optimization. In silico experiments demonstrate PepALD's generation quality and reward-optimization performance against representative peptide generation baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 宏环肽是面向细胞内靶点的有前景的治疗候选分子，但其设计需要同时控制非天然单体化学、环拓扑、膜渗透性和靶点结合。现有的SMILES或HELM字符串生成模型要么在原子级长序列空间中操作，要么将单体视为缺乏化学基础的符号化令牌。我们提出了PepALD，一个用于从头宏环肽生成的自回归潜在扩散（ALD）基础模型。该模型使用结构化化学嵌入表示HELM单体，通过化学信息潜在空间中的上下文条件扩散生成每个残基，在自回归生成过程中预测R基团感知的环闭合，并利用胜者保护扩散自适应偏好优化将去噪器与亲和力奖励对齐。计算实验表明，PepALD在生成质量和奖励优化性能方面均优于代表性肽生成基线。

</details>


### [166] [Behavioral Audit of Machine Unlearning Has a Privacy Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14518)
*Liou Tang,James Joshi,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器遗忘（MU）审计中的隐私-审计权衡问题。作者通过信息论证明：对于凸机器学习模型，仅依赖查询模型行为信号的通用审计方案，无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的情况下识别出未充分遗忘的模型。实验验证了凸模型中的结论，并进一步表明该权衡在非凸模型中依然存在。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘审计工作要么假设模型所有者诚实，要么仅关注单方面的不诚实行为，但模型所有者和审计者之间互不信任的场景尚未被探索。本文旨在揭示在这种相互不信任的假设下，审计MU存在的固有限制。

Method: 采用信息论方法进行理论证明：对于凸机器学习模型，证明了仅依赖行为信号（模型查询）的通用审计方案无法同时实现有效审计和保留集成员隐私保护。随后在凸模型和非凸模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明表明：在模型所有者不诚实、审计者诚实但好奇的假设下，审计MU面临固有的隐私-审计权衡。凸模型上的实验强烈支持该理论结果，进一步实验表明该权衡在非凸模型中依然存在。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁在更现实的审计者威胁模型下，更仔细地考虑隐私-审计权衡，并为MU管道中隐私保护审计方案的设计提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: The removal of learned data from Machine Learning models through Machine Unlearning (MU) has been widely studied; however, there has yet to be an agreed-upon scheme for auditing MU. Existing work has shown that a dishonest model owner can falsify evidence to avoid executing MU, while curious auditors (and adversaries) can infer the privacy-sensitive properties of the model and its training data even with limited access. Yet auditing of MU under mutual distrust between the model owner and the auditor remains unexplored. We provide an information-theoretic proof for this scenario: for convex ML models, a generic audit scheme that relies solely on querying the model for \textit{behavioral} signals cannot identify insufficiently unlearned models without revealing membership information of the retained set. Therefore, auditing MU under the assumption of a dishonest model owner and an honest-but-curious auditor faces an inherent privacy-audit tradeoff. Our empirical results on convex models strongly supports this result, while further experiments demonstrate that this privacy-audit tension persists in non-convex models. Our results call for a more careful consideration of the privacy-audit tension under a realistic auditor threat model, and serve as a foundation for more scrutiny of designs of privacy-preserving audit schemes for the MU pipeline. We also release our code implementation at https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit.

中文摘要翻译: 通过机器遗忘从机器学习模型中移除已学习数据的方法已被广泛研究，然而尚未出现公认的审计MU方案。现有工作表明，不诚实的模型所有者可以伪造证据来避免执行MU，而好奇的审计者（及 adversaries）即使访问权限有限，也能推断出模型及其训练数据的隐私敏感属性。然而，在模型所有者和审计者相互不信任的条件下审计MU仍未得到探索。我们针对该场景提供了一项信息论证明：对于凸机器学习模型，任何仅依赖查询模型获取行为信号的通用审计方案，都无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的情况下识别出未充分遗忘的模型。因此，在假设模型所有者不诚实而审计者诚实但好奇的条件下，审计MU面临固有的隐私-审计权衡。我们在凸模型上的实验结果强烈支持这一结论，进一步的实验表明该隐私-审计紧张关系在非凸模型中依然存在。我们的结果呼吁在更现实的审计者威胁模型下更仔细地考虑隐私-审计权衡，并为MU管道的隐私保护审计方案的设计提供了更深入审视的基础。我们还发布了代码实现：https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit。

</details>


### [167] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型隐藏状态中代码正确性的可解码性，发现在生成前代码正确性可线性解码（AUC 0.931），但修复过程中的隐藏状态偏移只是修复上下文的伪相关，经残差化控制后消失。方法贡献与实证贡献并重：诚实地同时报告了正向和负向结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在生成代码之前，其隐藏状态中是否编码了代码正确性信息；以及在模型尝试修复失败代码时，隐藏状态变化是否反映了修复成功与否的内部表征。

Method: 使用线性探针从Qwen3-4B-Instruct的隐藏状态中解码代码正确性，采用残差化（residualization）技术控制提示长度等混淆因素；对修复场景，分析隐藏状态偏移的方向性，并通过条件残差化控制修复上下文协变量。

Result: 1) 首次尝试代码的正确性可从最后一层隐藏状态线性解码，AUC达0.931，残差化后仍有0.911；2) 修复失败代码时，隐藏状态偏移存在统计显著的对比方向，但该方向在控制修复上下文协变量后消失，表明其仅为修复上下文的伪相关。

Conclusion: 代码正确性在生成前即可从隐藏状态中解码，但修复过程中的方向性偏移并非修复理解的独立特征，而是由修复上下文驱动；方法学上强调了残差化作为混淆控制工具的诚实诊断价值——能同时揭示正向和负向发现。

Original Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型在其隐藏状态中编码了丰富的信息。本文研究在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型中，代码正确性在其生成之前以及修复失败尝试的过程中，是否可以从隐藏状态中解读，研究基于444个LiveCodeBench任务。本文报告了两个发现，并通过一个单一的混淆控制工具——残差化（residualization）——将它们联系起来。第一，模型首次尝试代码的正确性可以从提示结束时的隐藏状态中线性解码，在50次外部分割上无泄漏的留出AUC为0.931 ± 0.008。在从每个隐藏状态维度中去除提示长度的线性效应后，探针仍然达到0.911 ± 0.010，远高于提示长度基线0.754 ± 0.014。第二，在236个经过清洗的案例中，模型尝试修复失败的首次尝试，隐藏状态从失败尝试到其修复的偏移携带了一个统计上可检测的对比方向，在幅度检验和分割半检验中均显著，均优于标签打乱的零假设。然而，该方向在针对修复上下文协变量进行条件残差化后不再存续，这些协变量在成功和失败的修复之间有所不同，表明它是修复成功的一个相关因素，由修复上下文驱动，而非一个独立的修复理解特征。探针层通过嵌套交叉验证选择，同样支撑生成前正确性结果的残差化方法却推翻了修复方向解读。本文的贡献既是方法学上的，也是实证上的：一个足够诚实的诊断方法，在报告正面结果的同时也报告了负面结果。

</details>


### [168] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了PCA在保留方差时可能完全抹去稀有但高影响事件的全部信号（即使保留99.9999%的方差），并提出了两种新的降维方法（ExPCA和TP-PCA）来打破这种“风险阴影”，在理论、合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: PCA最大化方差，但高方差分量可能不包含稀有灾难性事件的信息，导致降维后这些事件的信号被完全擦除，进而使下游分类器退化为常数预测器。这种方差最大化与尾部风险意识之间的根本不匹配亟需解决。

Method: 提出Expectile PCA (ExPCA) 和 Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA) 两种方法。这两种方法通过重新加权数据协方差矩阵，将权重向高影响事件倾斜，从而在降维过程中保留稀有事件的信息。

Result: 理论上证明了ExPCA在保留稀有事件信息方面严格优于PCA。在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果呼吁在高风险决策中从根本上重新审视基于方差的降维方法。

Original Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events.
